Selected preserved BBN source files for the AUTODIN II/TCP comparison
Prepared October 9, 2026. Documentary source packet; static inspection only.
Complete listed files with line numbers added; not either complete archive.
LABEL is a preservation-era tape-label transcription. The October 1982 README is included only to identify excluded material.
Archive SHA-256: jnc_bbn.tar.gz 00f1b98bdabc60429622b410b46fb52748bc1d08d226de898e67b635cd999365
Archive SHA-256: tape1.tgz 82b968169c70f8672779ecef33d56683c00b24f59a1403d25e65baeb25f44ff8

==============================================================================
BBN PDP-11 / tcp/tcp.c
SHA-256 c8fb21e6bfd7e9ef0303a0fd755b3f3c2509797cfbaf36ca011cb9c2481fed1c

    1  #
    2  # include "tcpstru.h"
    3  
    4  /*
    5  Written by:
    6  
    7  Michael A. Wingfield
    8  Bolt Beranek & Newman
    9  Cambridge, MA 02138
   10  
   11  */
   12  
   13  /* data declarations */
   14  
   15  struct PortLdr PortHdr;
   16  
   17  char CmdUsrName[]   CMDUSRNM;    /* user cmd port name */
   18  char CmdTcpName[]   CMDTCPNM;    /* tcp cmd port name */
   19  char SendName[]     SENDNM;      /* send data port name */
   20  char RecvName[]     RECVNM;      /* recv data port name */
   21  
   22  char CmdTcp[CMDTCPSIZE];      /* tcp command out buffer */
   23  char CmdUsr[CMDUSRSIZE];      /* user command out buffer */
   24  
   25  struct WorkBlk WorkAvl[WORKSIZE], *WrkAvlPtr, *WorkAvail, *WorkPtr;
   26  struct WorkBlk WorkEnds[2],  *WorkHead, *WorkTail;
   27  
   28  struct FdsBlk FdsBffer[FDSSIZE]; /* index relating TCB to port */
   29  
   30  struct TcpTcb TCBList[NUMBTCBS],        /* the TCBs */
   31  *TcbAvl, *RetranHd, *TcbHd;
   32  
   33  int PortMap[PORTCNT];    /* bit map for selecting local port */
   34  
   35  char TcpOut[TCPOTSZ];   /* tcp output buffer */
   36  char TcpIn[TCPINSZ];    /* tcp input buffer */
   37  char FlkBuff[TCPOTSZ];  /* buffer for flaky gateway/2 */
   38  
   39  /* the next two tables used to simulate Stop/Resume */
   40  long StopSkt[NSTOP];    /* foreign skts from which to discard segs */
   41  int  StopTcc[NSTOP];    /* list of tcc from which to discard segs */
   42  
   43  int NetFdsR;            /* fds for net in traffic */
   44  int NetFdsW;            /* fds for net out traffic */
   45  int EarFds;             /* fds for ear port */
   46  
   47  int PartWr;             /* 1 => part of segment needs to be written to net */
   48  char *OutPtr;           /* ptr to next byte to send */
   49  int OutCnt;             /* count of remaining bytes */
   50  
   51  int *TimePtr;           /* ptr to relative time */
   52  int RTime;              /* relative time for flaky netout */
   53  int FlkFlag;            /* 1 => dup or out of order seg in progress */
   54  
   55  int SegNum;             /* segment identification number */
   56  int ShortIn;            /* 1 => PsipIn: segment in TcpIn buffer */
   57  
   58  int ShortCkt;           /* 1 => local traffic not sent to net */
   59  int ANLngth;            /* Arpanet leader size (2 or 6 only ) */
   60  int Debug;              /* 1 => turn on debug output */
   61  int NoRetran;           /* 1 => disable retransmissions */
   62  int SegData;            /* max amount of data in segment */
   63  int RWindow;            /* maximum receive window size */
   64  int WindNum;            /* numerator of window size calc */
   65  int WindDenom;          /* denominator of window size calc */
   66  int LocHost;            /* local host number */
   67  int LocImp;             /* local imp number */
   68  int Auth;               /* 1 => do authorization */
   69  int LocNet;             /* the local net number */
   70  int InLink;             /* the internet link number */
   71  int DoChkSum;           /* 1 => checksum the segments */
   72  int NetTable[NUMNET];   /* map between dest net and gateway */
   73  int RetFast;            /* time between fast retransmissions */
   74  int RetSlow;            /* time between slow retransmissions */
   75  int RetCount;           /* number of retransmissions before slow mode */
   76  
   77  /* parameters for the flaky gateway/2 */
   78  
   79  char NumLost;           /* % of segments discarded */
   80  char NumBad;            /* % of segments smashed */
   81  char NumDup;            /* % of segments duplicated */
   82  char TimeDup;           /* time until duped segment is sent */
   83  char NumReOrd;          /* % of segments reordered */
   84  char TimeReOrd;         /* time until saved segment is sent */
   85  
   86  /* The main program */
   87  
   88  main(argc, argv)
   89  
   90  /* arguments (if present):
   91  
   92  1.  Debug parameter      : values 1 to 4 - see below
   93  2.  ShortCkt parameter   : 1 => allow local traffic to be shorted
   94  3.  Auth parameter       : 1 => authorize the user
   95  4.  DoChkSum parameter   : 1 => checksum the segments
   96  5.  NoRetran parameter   : 1 => no retransmissions today
   97  6.  SegData parameter    : maximum data bytes in segment
   98  7.  RWindow parameter    : maximum receive window size
   99  8.  WindNum parameter    : numerator - window size
  100  9.  WindDenom parameter  : denominator - window size
  101  10. RetFast parameter    : retransmission time - fast
  102  11. RetSlow parameter    : retransmission time - slow
  103  12. RetCount parameter   : max retransmissions before slow mode
  104  
  105  Debug:
  106  
  107  1 => turn on all debugging including printfs
  108  2 => only net out log
  109  3 => only net in log
  110  4 => net out and net in log
  111  
  112  */
  113  
  114  int argc;
  115  char *argv[];
  116  
  117  {
  118     extern sg1, sg2, sg3, sg8, sg13;
  119  
  120     int WorkType;
  121     struct TcpTcb *TempPtr, *XPtr, *NxtPtr, *SavePtr;
  122     char AwaitBuffer[AWTLENGTH];
  123     char *AwaitPtr;
  124     struct FdsBlk *FdsBffPtr;
  125     int Error;
  126     int Err;
  127     int TimeOut;
  128     int i;
  129     int tvec[2];
  130     char *ctime();
  131     int TcbTm;
  132     int Stop;
  133     int TimeSw;
  134     int Time1;
  135     int Time2;
  136     int **ITimePtr;
  137     int OldTime;
  138     struct PMask1 *LPtr;
  139     int n;
  140     int SaveFds;
  141     int Host;
  142  
  143     struct
  144     {  int ZMode;      /* 0 => read, 1 => write, 2 => read/write */
  145        long ZHost;     /* 0 => any host */
  146        int ZLoMsg;     /* low end of msg id range */
  147        int ZHiMsg;     /* high end: 0 => set to ZLoMsg */
  148      } RawParams;
  149  
  150  
  151     Debug = 0;      /* default: no trace */
  152     ShortCkt = 1;   /* default: allow local echoing */
  153     Auth = 0;       /* default: don't authorize */
  154     DoChkSum = 0;   /* default: don't compute checksums */
  155     NoRetran = 0;   /* default: retransmissions allowed */
  156     SegData = 600;  /* default: data in TCP segment */
  157     RWindow = 1000; /* default: maximum receive window size */
  158     WindNum = 1;    /* default: numerator of window calculation */
  159     WindDenom = 1;  /* default: denominator of window calculation */
  160     RetFast = 2;    /* default: seconds */
  161     RetSlow = 5;    /* default: seconds */
  162     RetCount = 6;   /* default: number of retransmissions */
  163  
  164     /* process the arguments */
  165  
  166     for (i = 1; i < argc; i++)
  167     {  switch(i)
  168        {
  169           case 1:
  170           {  Debug = atoi(argv[i]);
  171              break;
  172            }
  173           case 2:
  174           {  ShortCkt = atoi(argv[i]);
  175              break;
  176            }
  177           case 3:
  178           {  Auth = atoi(argv[i]);
  179              break;
  180            }
  181           case 4:
  182           {  DoChkSum = atoi(argv[i]);
  183              break;
  184            }
  185           case 5:
  186           {  NoRetran = atoi(argv[i]);
  187              break;
  188            }
  189           case 6:
  190           {  SegData = atoi(argv[i]);
  191              break;
  192            }
  193           case 7:
  194           {  RWindow = atoi(argv[i]);
  195              break;
  196            }
  197           case 8:
  198           {  WindNum = atoi(argv[i]);
  199              break;
  200            }
  201           case 9:
  202           {  WindDenom = atoi(argv[i]);
  203              break;
  204            }
  205  
  206           case 10:
  207           {  RetFast = atoi(argv[i]);
  208              break;
  209            }
  210           case 11:
  211           {  RetSlow = atoi(argv[i]);
  212              break;
  213            }
  214           case 12:
  215           {  RetCount = atoi(argv[i]);
  216              break;
  217            }
  218           default:
  219           {  printf("too many args\n");
  220            }
  221         }
  222      }
  223  
  224     time(tvec);    /* print the time tcp started */
  225     printf("%s", ctime(tvec));
  226  
  227     printf("Startup Parameters\n");
  228     printf("Debug: %d\n", Debug);
  229     printf("ShortCkt: %d\n", ShortCkt);
  230     printf("Auth: %d\n", Auth);
  231     printf("DoChkSum: %d\n", DoChkSum);
  232     printf("NoRetran: %d\n", NoRetran);
  233     printf("SegData: %d\n", SegData);
  234     printf("RWindow: %d\n", RWindow);
  235     printf("WindNum: %d\n", WindNum);
  236     printf("WindDenom: %d\n", WindDenom);
  237     printf("RetFast: %d\n", RetFast);
  238     printf("RetSlow: %d\n", RetSlow);
  239     printf("RetCount: %d\n", RetCount);
  240  
  241     /* get the "tcptable" contents */
  242     /* the tcptable essentially tells tcp where in the
  243     internet it is executing and how it can talk to
  244     hosts on other networks. See ReadTbl for table format. */
  245  
  246     if (ReadTbl() == -1)
  247     {  printf("bad tcptable entry\n");
  248        exit();
  249      }
  250  
  251     if ((ANLngth != 2)&&(ANLngth != 6))
  252     {  printf("bad leader length parameter - (2 or 6 only)\n");
  253        exit();    /* die */
  254      }
  255  
  256     /* set up the signal routines */
  257     if ((signal(1,&sg1) == -1)||
  258     (signal(2,&sg2) == -1)||
  259     (signal(3,&sg3) == -1)||
  260     (signal(8,&sg8) == -1)||
  261     (signal(13,&sg13) == -1))
  262  
  263     {  printf("bad signal call\n");
  264        exit();
  265      }
  266  
  267     /* close unused file descriptors */
  268     close(0);    /* standard input */
  269     close(2);    /* error output */
  270  
  271     /* initialize the work avail list */
  272     for (WrkAvlPtr = WorkAvl;WrkAvlPtr < &WorkAvl[WORKSIZE-1];)
  273     {  WorkPtr = WrkAvlPtr;
  274        WorkPtr -> NextWork = ++WrkAvlPtr;
  275      }
  276     WrkAvlPtr -> NextWork = 0;     /* null pointer */
  277  
  278     WorkAvail = WorkAvl;
  279  
  280     /* inz the two ends of the work queue */
  281     WorkHead = &WorkEnds[0];
  282     WorkTail = &WorkEnds[1];
  283     WorkHead -> NextWork = WorkTail;
  284     WorkHead -> LastWork = 0;
  285     WorkTail -> LastWork = WorkHead;
  286     WorkTail -> NextWork = 0;
  287  
  288     SegNum = 0;
  289     PartWr = 0;
  290     RetranHd = 0;
  291     FlkFlag = 0;
  292     ShortIn = 0;
  293  
  294     /* clear the FdsBffer which relate Ports to TCBs */
  295     for (i = 0; i < FDSSIZE; i++)
  296     {  FdsBffer[i].BuffFlags = 0;
  297        FdsBffer[i].BuffFds = 0;
  298        FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr = 0;
  299      }
  300  
  301     /* set up TCB avail list */
  302     for (i = 0; i < (NUMBTCBS-1); i++)
  303     {  TCBList[i].NxtActTcb = &TCBList[i+1];
  304      }
  305     TCBList[i].NxtActTcb = 0;
  306     TcbAvl = TCBList;
  307     TcbHd = 0;
  308  
  309     /* clear the bit map for selecting local ports */
  310     for (i = 0; i < PORTCNT; i++)
  311     {  PortMap[i] = 0;
  312      }
  313  
  314     /* clear the tables used for Stop/Resume */
  315     for (i = 0;i < NSTOP; i++)
  316     {  StopSkt[i] = 0;
  317        StopTcc[i] = 0;
  318      }
  319  
  320     /* create the "ear" port */
  321     unlink (EAR);
  322     EarFds = port (EAR,0170222,0);
  323     if (EarFds == -1)
  324     {  /* couldn't open ear port */
  325        printf("Tcp: couldn't open ear port\n");
  326      }
  327     else if (EarFds > (FDSSIZE-1))
  328     {  /* too big */
  329        printf("Tcp: fds1 too big\n");
  330      }
  331     else
  332     {  /* set up corresponding entry to FdsBffer */
  333        FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[EarFds];
  334        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = EARIN;
  335        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = EarFds;
  336        FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = 0;
  337        awtenb(EarFds);
  338      }
  339  
  340     /* open connection to raw message facility */
  341  
  342     RawParams.ZMode = 0;    /* read */
  343     RawParams.ZHost = 0;    /* any host */
  344     RawParams.ZLoMsg = InLink << 4;    /* set link field */
  345     RawParams.ZHiMsg = 0;    /* set to ZLoMsg */
  346  
  347     NetFdsR = open("/dev/net/rmi", &RawParams);
  348  
  349     if (NetFdsR == -1)
  350     {  /* can't open read connection to raw msg */
  351        printf("can't connect to ncp - read\n");
  352        exit();    /* halt */
  353      }
  354     else if (NetFdsR > (FDSSIZE-1))
  355     {  /* too big */
  356        printf("Tcp: fds2 too big\n");
  357        exit();    /* halt */
  358      }
  359     else
  360     {  /* set up corresponding entry in FdsBffer */
  361        FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[NetFdsR];
  362        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = NETINHP;
  363        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = NetFdsR;
  364        FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = 0;
  365        awtenb(NetFdsR);      /* enable the file descriptor */
  366      }
  367  
  368     RawParams.ZMode = 1;    /* write */
  369  
  370     NetFdsW = open("/dev/net/rmi", &RawParams);
  371  
  372     if (NetFdsW == -1)
  373     {  /* can't open write connection to raw msg */
  374        printf("can't connect to ncp - write\n");
  375        exit();    /* halt */
  376      }
  377     else if (NetFdsW > (FDSSIZE-1))
  378     {  /* too big */
  379        printf("Tcp: fds20 too big\n");
  380        exit();    /* halt */
  381      }
  382     else
  383     {  /* set up corresponding entry in FdsBffer */
  384        FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[NetFdsW];
  385        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = NETOUTHP;
  386        FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = NetFdsW;
  387        FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = 0;
  388        awtenb(NetFdsW);      /* enable the file descriptor */
  389      }
  390  
  391     if (Debug > 0)    /* don't waste fds unless debugging */
  392  
  393     {  Error = LogInit("net.log");
  394        if (Error == -1)
  395        {  printf("error in LogInit\n");
  396         }
  397      }
  398  
  399     TimeSw = 1;
  400     Time2 = 0;
  401     ITimePtr = &TimePtr;
  402     TimePtr = &Time2;
  403     OldTime = 0;
  404  
  405     itime(ITimePtr);
  406  
  407     while(1)     /* infinite loop */
  408     {  /* process the work-to-do list */
  409        WorkPtr = WorkHead -> NextWork;
  410        while ((WorkPtr -> NextWork) != 0)
  411        {  /* process the quantum of work */
  412           WorkType = (WorkPtr -> PortFlags)&037;
  413           switch (WorkType)
  414           {  case EARIN:     /* a message to the "ear" */
  415              {  Error = EarIn(WorkPtr);
  416                 break;
  417               }
  418              case USRCMD:
  419              {  Error = CmdIn(WorkPtr);
  420                 break;
  421               }
  422              case NETOUTHP:
  423              {  Error = PsipOut(WorkPtr);
  424                 break;
  425               }
  426              case NETINHP:
  427              {  Error = PsipIn(WorkPtr);
  428                 break;
  429               }
  430              case USERINHP:
  431              {  Error = DataIn(WorkPtr);
  432                 break;
  433               }
  434              default:
  435              {  /* something bad */
  436                 printf("Sched: bad opcode: %d\n",WorkType);
  437                 Error = 0;
  438               }
  439            }
  440  
  441           switch(Error)
  442           {  /* determine what to do with the work block */
  443              case 0:    /* remove the work entry */
  444              {  TempPtr = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  445                 (WorkPtr -> NextWork) -> LastWork = WorkPtr -> LastWork;
  446                 (WorkPtr -> LastWork) -> NextWork = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  447                 WorkPtr -> NextWork = WorkAvail;
  448                 WorkAvail = WorkPtr;
  449                 break;
  450               }
  451  
  452              case 1:    /* do not remove the work block */
  453              {  TempPtr = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  454                 break;
  455               }
  456  
  457              case 2:    /* move work block to end of queue */
  458              {
  459                 TempPtr = WorkPtr -> LastWork;
  460                 MakeWrk(0, WorkPtr -> PortFlags,
  461                 WorkPtr -> TCBPtr, WorkPtr -> PortFds);
  462                 TempPtr = TempPtr -> NextWork;
  463                 break;
  464               }
  465              case 3:    /* connection is to be aborted */
  466              {  /* move WorkPtr to next block not associated
  467                 with this TCB */
  468  
  469                 XPtr = WorkPtr -> TCBPtr;
  470  
  471                 while((WorkPtr -> NextWork != 0)&&(WorkPtr -> TCBPtr == XPtr))
  472                 {  WorkPtr = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  473                  }
  474  
  475                 /* remove work blocks, etc assoc with XPtr */
  476                 Abort(XPtr, 0);
  477  
  478                 TempPtr = WorkPtr;
  479                 break;
  480               }
  481              case 4:     /* abort all connections associated with user */
  482              {
  483                 SaveFds = WorkPtr -> PortFds;
  484  
  485                 /* delete work block entry */
  486  
  487                 TempPtr = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  488                 (WorkPtr -> NextWork) -> LastWork = WorkPtr -> LastWork;
  489                 (WorkPtr -> LastWork) -> NextWork = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  490                 WorkPtr -> NextWork = WorkAvail;
  491                 WorkAvail = WorkPtr;
  492  
  493                 /* find associated TCBs */
  494  
  495                 XPtr = TcbHd;
  496  
  497                 while(XPtr != 0)
  498                 {  SavePtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
  499  
  500                    if (XPtr -> UserCmdPort == SaveFds)
  501                    {  /* found an associated TCB */
  502  
  503                       switch(XPtr -> State)
  504                       {  case CLOSED:
  505                          case OPEN:
  506                          case SYNSENT:
  507  
  508                          {  /* clean up and close out the connection */
  509                             /* move TempPtr to next block not associated with this TCB */
  510  
  511                             while((TempPtr -> NextWork != 0)&&(TempPtr -> TCBPtr == XPtr))
  512                             {  TempPtr = TempPtr -> NextWork;
  513                              }
  514  
  515                             Abort(XPtr, 1);
  516                             break;
  517                           }
  518                          default:
  519                          {  /* every other connection state */
  520                             /* set the RST flag and let PsipOut finish the abortion */
  521  
  522                             XPtr -> TCPFlags =| RST + ACK;
  523                             XPtr -> Flags =| 020;    /* abort connection */
  524  
  525                             MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  526  
  527                             break;
  528                           }
  529                        }
  530  
  531                     }
  532  
  533                    XPtr = SavePtr;
  534                  }
  535                 break;
  536               }
  537  
  538              default:
  539              {  /* this looks bad */
  540                 TempPtr = WorkPtr -> NextWork;
  541                 printf("Sched: bad work return: %d\n",Error);
  542               }
  543            }
  544           WorkPtr = TempPtr;
  545  
  546         }
  547  
  548        if (FlkFlag == 0)
  549        {  RTime = 1000;   /* seconds */
  550         }
  551  
  552        if (RetranHd != 0)
  553        {  if ((RetranHd -> RetranTm - *TimePtr) < (RTime - *TimePtr))
  554           {  TimeOut = RetranHd -> RetranTm - *TimePtr;
  555            }
  556           else
  557           {  TimeOut = RTime - *TimePtr;
  558            }
  559         }
  560        else
  561        {  TimeOut = RTime - *TimePtr;
  562         }
  563  
  564        if (TimeOut < 0)
  565        {  TimeOut = 0;
  566         }
  567  
  568        if (Debug == 1)
  569        {  printf("Sched: TimeOut = %d\n",TimeOut);
  570         }
  571  
  572        /* wait for some port activity */
  573        await(TimeOut, AwaitBuffer, AWTLENGTH);
  574  
  575        if (FlkFlag == 1)
  576        {  RTime =- *TimePtr;
  577  
  578           if (RTime <= 0)
  579           {  /* schedule sending of dup or out of order segment */
  580  
  581              MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, &FlkFlag, NetFdsW);
  582            }
  583         }
  584        n = 0;
  585        AwaitPtr = AwaitBuffer;
  586  
  587        /* search the await buffer, putting items on work list */
  588        while (*AwaitPtr != -1)
  589        {  FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[*AwaitPtr];
  590  
  591           /* put file descriptor blocks in work queue */
  592           if (WorkAvail == 0)    /* test free list */
  593           {  /* no more blocks */
  594              printf("Sched: no more work blocks\n");
  595            }
  596           else
  597           {  /* put work block in work queue */
  598              switch((FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags)&037)
  599              {  case USEROUTHP:
  600                 {  /* no entry in work queue */
  601                    break;
  602                  }
  603                 case TCPCMD:
  604                 {  /* no entry in work queue */
  605                    break;
  606                  }
  607                 case NETOUTHP:
  608                 {  /* no entry in work queue */
  609                    break;
  610                  }
  611                 case 0:
  612                 {  /* the FdsBffer entry was deleted! */
  613                    break;
  614                  }
  615                 case USERINHP:
  616                 {
  617                    /* fall through */
  618                  }
  619                 default:
  620                 {  /* put entry in work queue */
  621  
  622                    MakeWrk(0, FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags,
  623                    FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr, FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds);
  624  
  625                  }
  626               }
  627            }
  628           AwaitPtr++;
  629           n++;
  630         }
  631  
  632        if (n == 0)
  633        {  /* printf("empty wakeup\n"); */
  634         }
  635  
  636        /* switch time counts */
  637        if (TimeSw == 0)
  638        {  Time2 = 0;
  639           TimePtr = &Time2;
  640           OldTime = Time1;
  641           TimeSw = 1;
  642         }
  643        else
  644        {  Time1 = 0;
  645           TimePtr = &Time1;
  646           OldTime = Time2;
  647           TimeSw = 0;
  648         }
  649  
  650        if (RetranHd != 0)
  651  
  652        {  /* schedule retransmissions based on OldTime */
  653  
  654           Stop = 0;
  655           XPtr = RetranHd;
  656           TcbTm = -OldTime;
  657           n = 0;
  658  
  659           do
  660           {  XPtr -> RetranTm =+ TcbTm;
  661              if (XPtr -> RetranTm <= 0)
  662              {  /* schedule the TCB for retransmission */
  663                 /* put ptrs back to last acked byte */
  664                 XPtr -> SendSeq = XPtr -> LeftSeq;
  665                 XPtr -> DataNxt = XPtr -> DataHd;
  666  
  667                 if (Debug == 1)
  668                 {  printf("Sched: retran\n");
  669                  }
  670                 if (Debug == 4)
  671                 {  Log(5, "si", "retran", XPtr, -1);
  672                  }
  673  
  674                 /* indicate that a retransmission is being
  675                 scheduled */
  676                 XPtr -> Flags =| XRETRANOK;
  677  
  678                 XPtr -> NumRetran =+ 1;
  679                 XPtr -> RetranSeg =+ 1;
  680  
  681                 if (XPtr -> NumRetran == RetCount)
  682                 {  /* foreign process not responding */
  683                    /* send notice to user */
  684  
  685                    LPtr = CmdTcp;
  686                    LPtr -> YOpCode = OPROCNR;
  687                    LPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  688                    write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
  689  
  690                    XPtr -> Flags =| 4;    /* set retransmission mode flag */
  691                  }
  692  
  693                 NxtPtr = XPtr -> NxtRetran;
  694                 /* remove TCB from retransmission queue */
  695                 UnQTcb(XPtr);
  696  
  697                 if (RetranHd == 0)
  698                 {  /* UnQTcb removed the only entry */
  699                    Stop = 1;
  700                  }
  701  
  702                 /* make work for PsipOut */
  703                 MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  704                 TcbTm = XPtr -> RetranTm;
  705                 XPtr -> RetranTm = 0;
  706                 XPtr = NxtPtr;
  707               }
  708              else
  709              {  Stop = 1;
  710               }
  711              n++;
  712            }
  713           while((Stop == 0)&&(XPtr != RetranHd)&&(n < NUMBTCBS));
  714         }
  715      }
  716   }
  717  
  718  /* 
  719   */
  720  /* subroutine which acts on messages to the "ear" */
  721  
  722  EarIn(WrkPtr)
  723  
  724  struct WorkBlk *WrkPtr;
  725  {
  726     extern char NumLost;
  727     extern char NumBad;
  728     extern char NumDup;
  729     extern char TimeDup;
  730     extern char NumReOrd;
  731     extern char TimeReOrd;
  732  
  733     int UserPID;
  734     int i;
  735     int FDesc;
  736     int Cap[2];
  737     int UsrFds;
  738     struct PMask1 *AckPtr;
  739     int xx;
  740     struct FdsBlk *FdsBffPtr;
  741     struct PMask1 *InitPtr;
  742  
  743  
  744     if (Debug == 1)
  745     {  printf("EarIn: entered\n");
  746      }
  747     capac(WrkPtr -> PortFds,Cap,4);
  748     if (Cap[0] == 0)
  749     {  /* no real work here */
  750        return(0);
  751      }
  752  
  753     do
  754     {  /* flush zero length messages */
  755        /* these occur when processes close the ear port */
  756        read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, &PortHdr, HDRLNGTH);   /* get message header */
  757        Cap[0] =- HDRLNGTH;
  758      }
  759     while (((PortHdr.DCount&~0100000) == 0)&&(Cap[0] >= HDRLNGTH));
  760  
  761     if (Cap[0] == 0)
  762     {  /* no real messages here */
  763        return(0);
  764      }
  765  
  766     /* get rest of message */
  767     read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, CmdUsr, PortHdr.DCount&~0100000);
  768  
  769     InitPtr = CmdUsr;
  770     switch(InitPtr -> YOpCode)
  771     {  case OINIT:
  772        {
  773           /* create names of command in, command out ports */
  774           UserPID = PortHdr.UsrPID;
  775           for (i = PRTPREFIX+2; i > PRTPREFIX-1; i--)
  776           {  CmdUsrName[i] = '0' + (UserPID&07);
  777              CmdTcpName[i] = '0' + (UserPID&07);
  778              UserPID =>> 3;
  779            }
  780           unlink(CmdUsrName);
  781           UsrFds = port(CmdUsrName,0170222,0);       /* create user command port */
  782  
  783           if (UsrFds == -1)
  784           {  /* couldn't create user command port */
  785              printf("EarIn: couldn't create user command port\n");
  786              return(0);
  787            }
  788           FDesc = open(CmdTcpName,1);       /* open the tcp command port */
  789           if (FDesc == -1)
  790           {  /* ignore the clown */
  791              printf("EarIn: couldn't open tcp command port\n");
  792              close(UsrFds);
  793              return(0);
  794            }
  795  
  796           unlink(CmdTcpName);
  797  
  798           if (UsrFds > (FDSSIZE-1))
  799           {  /* too big */
  800              printf("EarIn: fds3 too big\n");
  801            }
  802           else
  803           {  /* enter file descriptors in FdsBffer */
  804              FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[UsrFds];
  805              FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = FDesc;  /* saved for Open response */
  806              FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = USRCMD;
  807              FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = UsrFds;
  808  
  809              awtenb(UsrFds);
  810            }
  811           if (FDesc > (FDSSIZE-1))
  812           {  /* too big */
  813              printf("EarIn: fds4 too big\n");
  814            }
  815           else
  816           {  FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[FDesc];
  817              FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = 0;
  818              FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = TCPCMD;
  819              FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = FDesc;
  820              awtenb(FDesc);
  821            }
  822  
  823           /* send back the acknowledge message */
  824           AckPtr = CmdTcp;
  825           AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;  /* acknowledge */
  826           AckPtr -> YHandle = 0;
  827           xx = write(FDesc,CmdTcp,4);
  828  
  829           break;
  830         }
  831  
  832        case OSTAT:
  833        {  /* special command to print status */
  834           Status();
  835           break;
  836         }
  837  
  838        case OFLAKY:
  839        {  /* new parameters for flaky gateway/2 */
  840  
  841           NumLost   = CmdUsr[2];
  842           NumBad    = CmdUsr[3];
  843           NumDup    = CmdUsr[4];
  844           TimeDup   = CmdUsr[5];
  845           NumReOrd  = CmdUsr[6];
  846           TimeReOrd = CmdUsr[7];
  847  
  848           printf("NL: %d NB: %d ND: %d TD: %d NR: %d TR: %d\n",
  849           CmdUsr[2], CmdUsr[3], CmdUsr[4], CmdUsr[5],
  850           CmdUsr[6], CmdUsr[7]);
  851  
  852           break;
  853         }
  854        default:
  855        {  printf("EarIn: some bad opcode %d\n", InitPtr -> YOpCode);
  856           break;
  857         }
  858  
  859      }
  860     Cap[0] =- PortHdr.DCount&~0100000;
  861     if (Cap[0] > 0)
  862     {  /* more ear commands */
  863        return(2);
  864      }
  865     return(0);
  866   }
  867  
  868  
  869  /* 
  870   */
  871  /* Module which handles commands sent from user to TCP */
  872  
  873  CmdIn(WrkPtr)
  874  
  875  struct WorkBlk *WrkPtr;
  876  
  877  {
  878     register struct TcpTcb *XPtr, *Handle, *TPtr;
  879  
  880     int i;
  881     int x;
  882     int j;
  883     int k;
  884     int T;
  885     int Found;
  886     int Stop;
  887     int SndFds;
  888     int RcvFds;
  889     int Error;
  890     int Cap[2];
  891     int UserPID;
  892     int R1;
  893     int UserFds;
  894     int ParamVal[20];
  895  
  896     struct WorkBlk *WPtr;
  897     struct PMask1 *ChngPtr;
  898     struct PMask1 *AckPtr;
  899     struct PMask1 *MovePtr;
  900     struct PMask1 *ErrPtr;
  901     struct MOpen *OpenPtr;
  902     struct MUrgent *UrgPtr;
  903     struct MClose *ClosePtr;
  904     struct PMask1 *AbortPtr;
  905     struct FdsBlk *FdsBffPtr;
  906     struct MUpdate *UPtr;
  907     struct MStatus *SPtr;
  908  
  909     if (Debug == 1)
  910     {  printf("CmdIn: entered\n");
  911      }
  912     /* get port capacity */
  913  
  914     capac(WrkPtr -> PortFds, Cap, 4);
  915  
  916     while(1)      /* exit by return */
  917  
  918     {  if (Cap[0] == 0)
  919        {  /* no more commands */
  920           return(0);
  921         }
  922        if (Cap[0] == HDRLNGTH)
  923        {  /* user closed his command port */
  924  
  925           UserFds = WrkPtr -> TCBPtr;
  926  
  927           awtdis(WrkPtr -> PortFds);
  928           awtdis(UserFds);
  929  
  930           close(WrkPtr -> PortFds);
  931           close(UserFds);
  932  
  933           FdsBffer[WrkPtr -> PortFds].BuffFlags = 0;
  934           FdsBffer[UserFds].BuffFlags = 0;
  935  
  936           return(4);   /* abort all connections for this process */
  937         }
  938  
  939        if (Cap[0] < HDRLNGTH)
  940        {  /* can't happen!? */
  941           printf("CmdIn: huh?\n");
  942           return(4);    /* abort all connections */
  943         }
  944  
  945        read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, &PortHdr, HDRLNGTH);  /* get msg header */
  946  
  947        /* read in the text */
  948        read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, CmdUsr, (PortHdr.DCount&~0100000));
  949  
  950        Cap[0] =- HDRLNGTH + PortHdr.DCount&~0100000;
  951  
  952        OpenPtr = CmdUsr;
  953        switch (OpenPtr -> OpCode)
  954  
  955        {  case OOPEN:
  956           {  /* find an unused TCB */
  957              if (TcbAvl == 0)
  958              {  /* preempt TCB if authorization bit on */
  959                 if (Auth == 1)
  960                 {  /* try to find a non-CAT-I TCB to steal */
  961  
  962                    T = -1;
  963                    XPtr = TcbHd;
  964  
  965                    /* find the lowest precedence TCB */
  966                    while(XPtr != 0)
  967                    {  if ((XPtr -> SndPrec > T)&&(XPtr -> SndPrec < 12))
  968                       {  /* CAT-I traffic has precedences of 12-15 */
  969  
  970                          T = XPtr -> SndPrec;
  971                          TPtr = XPtr;
  972                        }
  973                     }
  974  
  975                    if (T == -1)
  976                    {  /* no TCB available */
  977                       ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
  978                       ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
  979                       ErrPtr -> YMisc = ESYSC;
  980                       write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
  981                       break;
  982                     }
  983  
  984                    /* abort connection of this TCB */
  985  
  986                    CmdOut(TPtr, ORESET, 0, 0);
  987  
  988                    CmdOut(TPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
  989  
  990                    Abort(TPtr, 1);
  991  
  992                  }
  993                 else
  994                 {  /* no TCB available */
  995                    ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
  996                    ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
  997                    ErrPtr -> YMisc = ESYSC;
  998                    write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
  999                    break;
 1000                  }
 1001               }
 1002  
 1003              /* got a TCB */
 1004              Handle = TcbAvl;
 1005              TcbAvl = TcbAvl -> NxtActTcb;
 1006  
 1007              /* link it on the active list */
 1008              Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbHd;
 1009              TcbHd = Handle;
 1010  
 1011              /* set up TCB for possible error handling */
 1012              Handle -> ConnHandle = OpenPtr -> OHandle;
 1013              Handle -> TCPCmdPort = WrkPtr -> TCBPtr;
 1014  
 1015              if (Auth == 1)
 1016              {  /* verify that user can do an Open */
 1017                 Error = Authorize(PortHdr.RealUserID, OpenPtr, Handle);
 1018  
 1019                 if (Error != 0)
 1020                 {  /* user is not authorized in some way */
 1021  
 1022                    CmdOut(Handle, OERR, Error, 0);
 1023  
 1024                    /* return the TCB */
 1025                    TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1026                    Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1027                    TcbAvl = Handle;
 1028  
 1029                    break;
 1030                  }
 1031               }
 1032  
 1033              /* check validity of local TCP port number */
 1034  
 1035              if (OpenPtr -> SrcPrt == 0)
 1036              {  /* create a unique local port number */
 1037                 i = 0;    /* row index */
 1038                 Stop = 0;
 1039  
 1040                 while ((i < PORTCNT)&&(Stop == 0))
 1041                 {  k = 1;
 1042                    j = 0;    /* column index */
 1043                    while ((j < 16)&&(Stop == 0))
 1044                    {  if ((PortMap[i]&k) == 0)
 1045                       {  Stop = 1;
 1046                        }
 1047                       else
 1048                       {  k =<< 1;
 1049                          j++;
 1050                        }
 1051                     }
 1052                    if (Stop == 0)
 1053                    {  i++;
 1054                     }
 1055                  }
 1056  
 1057                 if (Stop == 0)
 1058                 {  /* no unused entries */
 1059  
 1060                    CmdOut(Handle, OERR, ESYSC, 0);
 1061  
 1062                    /* return the TCB */
 1063                    TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1064                    Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1065                    TcbAvl = Handle;
 1066  
 1067                    break;
 1068                  }
 1069  
 1070                 PortMap[i] =| k;   /* set the used bit */
 1071  
 1072                 /* make up the port number */
 1073                 OpenPtr -> SrcPrt = BASEPORT + (i << 4) + j;
 1074  
 1075               }
 1076              else if (OpenPtr -> SrcPrt < 0400)
 1077              {  /* check authorization to use first 256 ports */
 1078                 if (Auth == 1)
 1079                 {  k = GetFields(PortHdr.RealUserID, &ParamVal);
 1080                    if ((k == -1)||(ParamVal[0] != 1))
 1081                    {  /* unauthorized use of these ports */
 1082  
 1083                       CmdOut(Handle, OERR, EUNAUTH, 0);
 1084  
 1085                       /* return the TCB */
 1086                       TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1087                       Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1088                       TcbAvl = Handle;
 1089  
 1090                       break;
 1091                     }
 1092                  }
 1093               }
 1094  
 1095              else if ((OpenPtr -> SrcPrt >= BASEPORT)&&
 1096              (OpenPtr -> SrcPrt < BASEPORT + 0400))
 1097              {  /* ports reserved for tcp use */
 1098  
 1099                 CmdOut(Handle, OERR, EBADPARAM, 0);
 1100  
 1101                 /* return the TCB */
 1102                 TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1103                 Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1104                 TcbAvl = Handle;
 1105  
 1106                 break;
 1107               }
 1108  
 1109              /* check for Open of connection mode */
 1110              if (OpenPtr -> OpenMode == 0)
 1111              {  /* check for fully specified foreign socket */
 1112                 if ((OpenPtr -> Net == 0)||((OpenPtr -> HostH == 0)&&
 1113                 (OpenPtr -> HostL == 0))||(OpenPtr -> DstPrt == 0))
 1114                 {  /* parameter unspecified: return error */
 1115  
 1116                    CmdOut(Handle, OERR, EUNSPEC, 0);
 1117  
 1118                    /* return the TCB */
 1119                    TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1120                    Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1121                    TcbAvl = Handle;
 1122  
 1123                    break;
 1124                  }
 1125               }
 1126  
 1127              /* derive the data send and receive ports */
 1128              UserPID = PortHdr.UsrPID;
 1129              for (i = PRTPREFIX+2; i > PRTPREFIX-1; i--)
 1130              {  SendName[i] = '0' + (UserPID&07);
 1131                 RecvName[i] = '0' + (UserPID&07);
 1132                 UserPID =>> 3;
 1133               }
 1134  
 1135              j = OpenPtr -> OHandle;
 1136              for (i = PRTPREFIX+5; i > PRTPREFIX+2; i--)
 1137              {  SendName[i] = '0' + (j&07);
 1138                 RecvName[i] = '0' + (j&07);
 1139                 j =>> 3;
 1140               }
 1141  
 1142              /* create the send data port */
 1143              unlink(SendName);
 1144              SndFds = port(SendName,0170222,0);
 1145              if (SndFds == -1)
 1146              {  /* couldn't create send data port */
 1147                 /* send error message */
 1148  
 1149                 CmdOut(Handle, OERR, ESNDPRT, 0);
 1150  
 1151                 /* return the TCB */
 1152                 TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1153                 Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1154                 TcbAvl = Handle;
 1155  
 1156                 break;
 1157               }
 1158  
 1159              /* open the receive data port */
 1160              RcvFds = open(RecvName,1);
 1161              if (RcvFds == -1)
 1162              {  /* couldn't open recv data port */
 1163  
 1164                 CmdOut(Handle, OERR, ERCVPRT, 0);
 1165  
 1166                 /* return the TCB */
 1167                 TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1168                 Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1169                 TcbAvl = Handle;
 1170  
 1171                 close(SndFds);
 1172                 break;
 1173               }
 1174  
 1175              unlink(RecvName);
 1176  
 1177              /* fill in the relevant TCB parameters */
 1178              Handle -> State = OPEN;
 1179              Handle -> Flags = 0;
 1180              Handle -> TMode = OpenPtr -> OpenMode;
 1181              Handle -> ProcID = PortHdr.UsrPID;
 1182              Handle -> UserCmdPort = WrkPtr -> PortFds;
 1183              Handle -> SendPort = SndFds;
 1184              Handle -> RecvPort = RcvFds;
 1185              Handle -> DataHd = 0;
 1186              Handle -> DataNxt = 0;
 1187              Handle -> DataTl = 0;
 1188              Handle -> VerHdr = (INLNGTH/4)|(INVERNO << 4);
 1189              Handle -> TCPFlags = (TCPLNGTH/4) << 12;
 1190              Handle -> DataState = 0;
 1191  
 1192              /* select an initial sequence number */
 1193              /* say, zero! */
 1194              z = &(Handle -> SndISN);
 1195              z -> bb[0] = 0;
 1196              z -> bb[1] = 0;
 1197  
 1198              Handle -> LeftSeq = Handle -> SndISN;
 1199              Handle -> SendSeq = Handle -> SndISN;
 1200              Handle -> MaxSend = Handle -> SndISN;
 1201              Handle -> SndFSN = 0;
 1202  
 1203              Handle -> OpnNet = OpenPtr -> Net;
 1204              Handle -> OpnHstH = OpenPtr -> HostH;
 1205              Handle -> OpnHstL = OpenPtr -> HostL;
 1206              Handle -> OpnPort = OpenPtr -> DstPrt;
 1207              Handle -> DstNet = OpenPtr -> Net;
 1208              Handle -> DstHstH = OpenPtr -> HostH;
 1209              Handle -> DstHstL = OpenPtr -> HostL;
 1210              Handle -> DestPort = OpenPtr -> DstPrt;
 1211              Handle -> SrcePort = OpenPtr -> SrcPrt;
 1212              Handle -> SrcNet = LocNet;
 1213              Handle -> SrcHstH = LocHost;
 1214              Handle -> SrcHstL = LocImp;
 1215  
 1216              Handle -> Window = (PORTSIZE*WindNum)/WindDenom;
 1217              if (Handle -> Window > RWindow)
 1218              {  Handle -> Window = RWindow;
 1219               }
 1220  
 1221              Handle -> SndWindow = 1;    /* allow one byte */
 1222  
 1223              Handle -> NumRetran = 0;
 1224              Handle -> NxtRetran = 0;
 1225  
 1226              Handle -> TimeToLive = 10;      /* 10 seconds...? */
 1227              Handle -> Protocol = TCPVERNO;
 1228  
 1229              Handle -> Options[0] = OPTSPT;
 1230              Handle -> Options[1] = 4;
 1231  
 1232              Handle -> SegRecv = 0;
 1233              Handle -> DupRecv = 0;
 1234              Handle -> BusyRecv = 0;
 1235              Handle -> RetranSeg = 0;
 1236  
 1237              /* put user send and receive data ports in FdsBffer */
 1238              if (SndFds > (FDSSIZE-1))
 1239              {  /* too big */
 1240                 printf("CmdIn: fds5 too big\n");
 1241               }
 1242              else
 1243              {  FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[SndFds];
 1244                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = USERINHP;
 1245                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = SndFds;
 1246                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = Handle;
 1247                 x = awtenb(SndFds);
 1248               }
 1249              if (RcvFds > (FDSSIZE-1))
 1250              {  /* too big */
 1251                 printf("CmdIn: fds6 too big\n");
 1252               }
 1253              else
 1254              {  FdsBffPtr = &FdsBffer[RcvFds];
 1255                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffFlags = USEROUTHP;
 1256                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffFds = RcvFds;
 1257                 FdsBffPtr -> BuffTcbPtr = Handle;
 1258                 awtenb(RcvFds);
 1259               }
 1260              if ((OpenPtr -> OpenMode) == 0)
 1261              {  /* user wants to Open a connection */
 1262  
 1263                 /* check that socket pair is unique */
 1264  
 1265  Found = 0;
 1266                 XPtr = TcbHd;
 1267  
 1268                 while(XPtr != 0)
 1269                 {  if (XPtr != Handle)
 1270                    {  if ((XPtr -> SrcePort == OpenPtr -> SrcPrt)&&
 1271                       (XPtr -> DestPort == OpenPtr -> DstPrt)&&
 1272                       (XPtr -> DstNet == OpenPtr -> Net)&&
 1273                       (XPtr -> DstHstH == OpenPtr -> HostH)&&
 1274                       (XPtr -> DstHstL == OpenPtr -> HostL))
 1275                       {  /* socket pair duplication */
 1276  
 1277                          CmdOut(Handle, OERR, EBADPARAM, 0);
 1278  
 1279                          /* return the TCB */
 1280                          TcbHd = Handle -> NxtActTcb;
 1281                          Handle -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 1282                          TcbAvl = Handle;
 1283  
 1284  close(SndFds);
 1285  close(RcvFds);
 1286  
 1287  /* remove entries from FdsBffer */
 1288  FdsBffer[SndFds].BuffFlags = 0;
 1289  FdsBffer[RcvFds].BuffFlags = 0;
 1290  
 1291  Found = 1;
 1292  			break;  /* break out of while */
 1293                        }
 1294                     }
 1295                    XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 1296                  }
 1297  if (Found == 1)
 1298  { break;    /* break out of case */
 1299  }
 1300  
 1301                 /* create some work for PsipOut */
 1302                 MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, Handle, NetFdsW);
 1303  
 1304               }
 1305  
 1306              /* send back an acknowledge */
 1307              AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1308              AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1309              /* return the actual ptr to TCB this time only */
 1310              /* all other acks return the index into the usertcb */
 1311              AckPtr -> YHandle = Handle;
 1312              write(Handle -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
 1313  
 1314              break;
 1315            }
 1316  
 1317           case OCLOSE:
 1318           {  /* begin connection closing procedure */
 1319  
 1320              ClosePtr = CmdUsr;
 1321              XPtr = ClosePtr -> CHandle;
 1322  
 1323              Error = ChkPtr(XPtr, WrkPtr -> PortFds);
 1324  
 1325              if (Error == -1)
 1326              {  break;    /* user is doing something dumb */
 1327               }
 1328  
 1329              switch (XPtr -> State)
 1330              {  case OPEN:
 1331                 case SYNSENT:
 1332                 {  /* move connection state to CLOSED */
 1333  
 1334                    XPtr -> State = CLOSED;
 1335  
 1336                    /* send user a change notice */
 1337                    CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
 1338  
 1339                    break;
 1340                  }
 1341  
 1342                 case SYNRECD:
 1343                 case ESTAB:
 1344                 case CLOSEWAIT:
 1345                 {  XPtr -> Flags =| 010;    /* send FIN after data */
 1346                    XPtr -> SndFSN = XPtr -> SndISN + ClosePtr -> CFinNum + 1;
 1347                    MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
 1348                    break;
 1349                  }
 1350  
 1351                 default:
 1352                 {  break;    /* ignore request */
 1353                  }
 1354               }
 1355  
 1356              /* return an ack message to user */
 1357              CmdOut(XPtr, OACK, 0, 0);
 1358  
 1359              break;
 1360            }
 1361  
 1362           case OABORT:
 1363           {  /* abort the connection */
 1364              AbortPtr = CmdUsr;
 1365              XPtr = AbortPtr -> YHandle;
 1366  
 1367              Error = ChkPtr(XPtr, WrkPtr -> PortFds);
 1368  
 1369              if (Error == -1)
 1370              {  break;    /* user is doing something dumb */
 1371               }
 1372  
 1373              /* verify that abort is allowed here */
 1374  
 1375              switch(XPtr -> State)
 1376              {  case CLOSED:
 1377                 case OPEN:
 1378                 case SYNSENT:
 1379  
 1380                 {  /* clean up and close out the connection */
 1381                    Abort(XPtr, 0);
 1382                    break;
 1383                  }
 1384                 default:
 1385                 {  /* every other connection state */
 1386                    /* set the RST flag and let PsipOut finish the abortion */
 1387  
 1388                    XPtr -> TCPFlags =| RST + ACK;
 1389                    XPtr -> Flags =| 020;    /* abort connection */
 1390  
 1391                    MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
 1392  
 1393                    break;
 1394                  }
 1395               }
 1396              break;
 1397            }
 1398           case OURGENT:
 1399           {  /* send urgent condition */
 1400              UrgPtr = CmdUsr;
 1401              XPtr = UrgPtr -> UHandle;
 1402  
 1403              Error = ChkPtr(XPtr, WrkPtr -> PortFds);
 1404  
 1405              if (Error == -1)
 1406              {  break;    /* user is doing something dumb */
 1407               }
 1408  
 1409              XPtr -> SndUrgNo = (UrgPtr -> UByteNo) + (XPtr -> SndISN) + 1;
 1410  
 1411              /* check if everything has been ACKed */
 1412              R1 = ULCompare(&(XPtr -> SndUrgNo), &(XPtr -> LeftSeq));
 1413  
 1414              if (R1 != 1)
 1415              /* SndUrgNo <= LeftSeq */
 1416              {  break;   /* ignore it - already ACKed */
 1417               }
 1418  
 1419              /* set urgent work bit */
 1420              XPtr -> Flags =| 01;
 1421  
 1422              if ((XPtr -> State == ESTAB)||(XPtr -> State == CLOSEWAIT))
 1423              {  /* make work for PsipOut */
 1424                 MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
 1425               }
 1426  
 1427              break;
 1428            }
 1429           case OMOVE:
 1430           {  MovePtr = CmdUsr;
 1431  
 1432              TPtr = MovePtr -> YHandle;
 1433  
 1434              Error = ChkPtr(TPtr, WrkPtr -> PortFds);
 1435  
 1436              if (Error == -1)
 1437              {  break;    /* user is doing something dumb */
 1438               }
 1439  
 1440              if ((TPtr -> State != ESTAB)&&
 1441              (TPtr -> State != CLOSEWAIT)&&
 1442              (TPtr -> State != FINWAIT))
 1443              {  CmdOut(TPtr, OERR, EMVSTATE, 0);
 1444                 break;
 1445               }
 1446  
 1447              /* find a TCB in ACCEPT mode */
 1448              Found = 0;
 1449              XPtr = TcbHd;
 1450  
 1451              while((XPtr != 0)&&(Found == 0))
 1452              {  /* TMode = 2 => ACCEPT */
 1453                 if ((XPtr -> TMode == 2)&&(XPtr -> State == OPEN))
 1454                 {  /* check process ID */
 1455                    if ((MovePtr -> YMisc == 0 /* unspecified */ )||
 1456                    (MovePtr -> YMisc == XPtr -> ProcID))
 1457                    {  /* check for SPT data */
 1458  
 1459                       if (Auth == 1)
 1460                       {  /* validate S/P parameters */
 1461                          if ((TPtr -> ScMaxOut <= XPtr -> ScMaxOut)&&
 1462                          (TPtr -> ScMinOut >= XPtr -> ScMinOut)&&
 1463                          (TPtr -> ScMaxIn <= XPtr -> ScMaxIn)&&
 1464                          (TPtr -> ScMinIn >= XPtr -> ScMinIn)&&
 1465                          (TPtr -> SndPrec <= XPtr -> ATPrec)&&
 1466                          (TPtr -> MxRcvPrec <= XPtr -> MxRcvPrec)&&
 1467                          (TPtr -> MnRcvPrec >= XPtr -> MnRcvPrec))
 1468  
 1469                          {  /* so far so good */
 1470                             /* check for matching TCC */
 1471  
 1472                             for (i = 0; i < XPtr -> TCCCnt; i++)
 1473                             {  if (TPtr -> TccList[0] == XPtr -> TccList[i])
 1474                                {  Found = 1;    /* at last! */
 1475                                 }
 1476                              }
 1477                           }
 1478                        }
 1479                       else
 1480                       {  Found = 1;
 1481                        }
 1482                     }
 1483                  }
 1484  
 1485                 if (Found == 0)
 1486                 {  XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 1487                  }
 1488               }
 1489  
 1490              if (Found == 0)
 1491              {  CmdOut(TPtr, OERR, EBADPARAM, 0);
 1492                 break;
 1493               }
 1494  
 1495              /* exchange the four port fds */
 1496  
 1497              T = TPtr -> TCPCmdPort;
 1498              TPtr -> TCPCmdPort = XPtr -> TCPCmdPort;
 1499              XPtr -> TCPCmdPort = T;
 1500  
 1501              T = TPtr -> UserCmdPort;
 1502              TPtr -> UserCmdPort = XPtr -> UserCmdPort;
 1503              XPtr -> UserCmdPort = T;
 1504  
 1505              T = TPtr -> SendPort;
 1506              TPtr -> SendPort = XPtr -> SendPort;
 1507              XPtr -> SendPort = T;
 1508  
 1509              T = TPtr -> RecvPort;
 1510              TPtr -> RecvPort = XPtr -> RecvPort;
 1511              XPtr -> RecvPort = T;
 1512  
 1513              /* reset state of TCB */
 1514  
 1515              XPtr -> State = CLOSED;
 1516  
 1517              /* exchange connection handles */
 1518  
 1519              T = TPtr -> ConnHandle;
 1520              TPtr -> ConnHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
 1521              XPtr -> ConnHandle = T;
 1522  
 1523              /* reset FSM of DataIn routine */
 1524  
 1525              TPtr -> DataState = 0;
 1526  
 1527              /* set process ID */
 1528  
 1529              TPtr -> ProcID = XPtr -> ProcID;
 1530  
 1531              /* fix up FdsBffer */
 1532              for (i = 0; i < FDSSIZE; i++)
 1533              {  if (FdsBffer[i].BuffFlags != 0)
 1534                 {  if (FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr == XPtr)
 1535                    {  FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr = TPtr;
 1536                     }
 1537                    else if (FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr == TPtr)
 1538                    {  FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr = XPtr;
 1539                     }
 1540                  }
 1541               }
 1542              /* fix up work list */
 1543  
 1544              WPtr = WorkHead -> NextWork;
 1545  
 1546              while(WPtr -> NextWork != 0)
 1547              {  if (WPtr -> TCBPtr == XPtr)
 1548                 {  WPtr -> TCBPtr = TPtr;
 1549                  }
 1550                 else if (WPtr -> TCBPtr == TPtr)
 1551                 {  WPtr -> TCBPtr = XPtr;
 1552                  }
 1553                 WPtr = WPtr -> NextWork;
 1554               }
 1555  
 1556              /* ack the move command */
 1557  
 1558              CmdOut(XPtr, OACK, 0, 0);
 1559  
 1560              /* update the library UTCB parameters of mover */
 1561  
 1562              UPtr = CmdTcp;
 1563              UPtr -> ZOpCode = OUPDATE;
 1564              UPtr -> ZHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
 1565              UPtr -> ZConnIndex = XPtr;
 1566  
 1567              i = XPtr -> DataTl - XPtr -> DataHd;
 1568              if (i < 0)
 1569              {  i =+ RBUFFSIZE;
 1570               }
 1571  
 1572              UPtr -> ZSndCnt = TPtr -> LeftSeq + i - TPtr -> SndISN - 1;
 1573              UPtr -> ZRcvCnt = TPtr -> AckNo - TPtr -> RcvISN - 1;
 1574              write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 14);
 1575  
 1576              /* update the library UTCB parameters of movee */
 1577  
 1578              UPtr -> ZHandle = TPtr -> ConnHandle;
 1579              UPtr -> ZConnIndex = TPtr;
 1580              write(TPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 14);
 1581  
 1582              /* send CLOSED to the mover */
 1583  
 1584              CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
 1585  
 1586              /* send state to movee */
 1587  
 1588              CmdOut(TPtr, OCHNG, TPtr -> State, 0);
 1589  
 1590              /* make work in case movee has sent data */
 1591  
 1592              MakeWrk(0, USERINHP, TPtr, TPtr -> SendPort);
 1593  
 1594              break;
 1595            }
 1596  
 1597           case OSTAT:
 1598           {  /* send back status information to user */
 1599  
 1600              SPtr = CmdUsr;
 1601              XPtr = SPtr -> YHandle;
 1602  
 1603              Error = ChkPtr(XPtr, WrkPtr -> PortFds);
 1604  
 1605              if (Error == -1)
 1606              {  break;    /* user is doing something dumb */
 1607               }
 1608  
 1609              SPtr = CmdTcp;
 1610  
 1611              SPtr -> SOpCode    = OSTAT;
 1612              SPtr -> SState     = XPtr -> State;
 1613              SPtr -> SNet       = XPtr -> DstNet;
 1614              SPtr -> SHost      = XPtr -> DstHstH;
 1615              SPtr -> SImp       = XPtr -> DstHstL;
 1616              SPtr -> SLocPrt    = XPtr -> SrcePort;
 1617              SPtr -> SForPrt    = XPtr -> DestPort;
 1618              SPtr -> SScMxOt    = XPtr -> ScMaxOut;
 1619              SPtr -> SScMnOt    = XPtr -> ScMinOut;
 1620              SPtr -> SScMxIn    = XPtr -> ScMaxIn;
 1621              SPtr -> SScMnIn    = XPtr -> ScMinIn;
 1622              SPtr -> SSndPrec   = XPtr -> SndPrec;
 1623              SPtr -> SRcvPrec   = XPtr -> MnRcvPrec;
 1624              SPtr -> SNTcc      = XPtr -> TCCCnt;
 1625  
 1626              for (i = 0;i < XPtr -> TCCCnt; i++)
 1627              {  SPtr -> STcc[i] = XPtr -> TccList[i];
 1628               }
 1629  
 1630              SPtr -> SSndWind   = XPtr -> SndWindow;
 1631              SPtr -> SRcvWind   = XPtr -> Window;
 1632  
 1633              SPtr -> SAckData   = XPtr -> DataNxt - XPtr -> DataHd;
 1634              if (SPtr -> SAckData < 0)
 1635              {  SPtr -> SAckData =+ RBUFFSIZE;
 1636               }
 1637  
 1638              SPtr -> SSndBuff   = XPtr -> DataTl - XPtr -> DataHd;
 1639              if (SPtr -> SSndBuff < 0)
 1640              {  SPtr -> SSndBuff =+ RBUFFSIZE;
 1641               }
 1642  
 1643              SPtr -> SSegRecv   = XPtr -> SegRecv;
 1644              SPtr -> SDupSeg    = XPtr -> DupRecv;
 1645              SPtr -> SBusyRecv  = XPtr -> BusyRecv;
 1646              SPtr -> SRetran    = XPtr -> RetranSeg;
 1647  
 1648              write (XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 48);
 1649  
 1650              break;
 1651            }
 1652  
 1653           case OSTOP:
 1654           {  /* stop traffic from host/tcc */
 1655              ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1656  
 1657              if (Auth == 0)
 1658              {  /* this command not allowed */
 1659                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1660                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EUNAUTH;
 1661                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1662                 break;
 1663               }
 1664  
 1665              /* verify that user is priviledged */
 1666              k = GetFields(PortHdr.RealUserID, &ParamVal);
 1667  
 1668              if ((k == -1)||(ParamVal[0] != 1))
 1669              {  /* unauthorized action */
 1670                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1671                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EUNAUTH;
 1672                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1673                 break;
 1674               }
 1675  
 1676              ClosePtr = CmdUsr;
 1677              if (ClosePtr -> CHandle == 1)
 1678              {  /* foreign socket number */
 1679                 /* check if already in table */
 1680  
 1681                 k = -1;
 1682  
 1683                 for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 1684                 {  if (StopSkt[i] != 0)
 1685                    {  if (ClosePtr -> CFinNum == StopSkt[i])
 1686                       {  /* already in table */
 1687                          AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1688                          AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1689                          write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1690                          break;
 1691                        }
 1692                     }
 1693                    else
 1694                    {  k = i;   /* save index */
 1695                     }
 1696                  }
 1697  
 1698                 if (k == -1)
 1699                 {  /* no space */
 1700                    ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1701                    ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1702                    ErrPtr -> YMisc = EBADPARAM;
 1703                    write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1704  
 1705                    break;
 1706                  }
 1707                 else
 1708                 {  StopSkt[k] = ClosePtr -> CFinNum;
 1709  
 1710                    AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1711                    AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1712                    write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1713  
 1714                    break;
 1715                  }
 1716               }
 1717              else
 1718              {  /* TCC specified */
 1719                 /* check if already in table */
 1720  
 1721                 k = -1;
 1722                 j = ClosePtr -> CFinNum;
 1723  
 1724                 for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 1725                 {  if (StopTcc[i] < 0)
 1726                    {  if (j == (StopTcc[i]&0377))
 1727                       {  /* already in table */
 1728                          AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1729                          AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1730                          write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1731                          break;
 1732                        }
 1733                     }
 1734                    else
 1735                    {  k = i;   /* save index */
 1736                     }
 1737                  }
 1738  
 1739                 if (k == -1)
 1740                 {  /* no space */
 1741                    ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1742                    ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1743                    ErrPtr -> YMisc = EBADPARAM;
 1744                    write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1745  
 1746                    break;
 1747                  }
 1748                 else
 1749                 {  StopTcc[k] = j + 0100000;
 1750  
 1751                    AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1752                    AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1753                    write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1754  
 1755                    break;
 1756                  }
 1757               }
 1758            }
 1759  
 1760           case ORESUME:
 1761           {  /* resume traffic from host/tcc */
 1762              if (Auth == 0)
 1763              {  /* this command not allowed */
 1764                 ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1765                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1766                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EUNAUTH;
 1767                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1768                 break;
 1769               }
 1770  
 1771              k = GetFields(PortHdr.RealUserID, &ParamVal);
 1772  
 1773              if ((k == -1)||(ParamVal[0] != 1))
 1774              {  /* unauthorized action */
 1775                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1776                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EUNAUTH;
 1777                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1778                 break;
 1779               }
 1780  
 1781              ClosePtr = CmdUsr;
 1782              if (ClosePtr -> CHandle == 1)
 1783              {  /* foreign socket number */
 1784                 /* find in table */
 1785  
 1786                 for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 1787                 {  if (StopSkt[i] != 0)
 1788                    {  if (ClosePtr ->CFinNum == StopSkt[i])
 1789                       {  StopSkt[i] = 0;
 1790  
 1791                          AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1792                          AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1793                          write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1794  
 1795                          break;
 1796                        }
 1797                     }
 1798                  }
 1799  
 1800                 /* must not have been there */
 1801  
 1802                 ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1803                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1804                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EBADPARAM;
 1805                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1806  
 1807                 break;
 1808               }
 1809              else
 1810              {  /* TCC specified */
 1811                 /* find in table */
 1812                 j = ClosePtr -> CFinNum;
 1813                 for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 1814                 {
 1815                    if (StopTcc[i] < 0)
 1816                    {
 1817                       if (j == (StopTcc[i]&0377))
 1818                       {  StopTcc[i] = 0;
 1819  
 1820                          AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 1821                          AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 1822                          write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 2);
 1823  
 1824                          break;
 1825                        }
 1826                     }
 1827                  }
 1828  
 1829                 /* must not have been there */
 1830  
 1831                 ErrPtr = CmdTcp;
 1832                 ErrPtr -> YOpCode = OERR;
 1833                 ErrPtr -> YMisc = EBADPARAM;
 1834                 write(WrkPtr -> TCBPtr, CmdTcp, 6);
 1835  
 1836                 break;
 1837               }
 1838            }
 1839  
 1840           default:
 1841           {  printf("CmdIn: bad opcode\n");
 1842              break;
 1843            }
 1844         }
 1845      }
 1846   }
 1847  
 1848  /* 
 1849   */
 1850  /* Subroutine which handles data from the user */
 1851  
 1852  DataIn(WrkPtr)
 1853  
 1854  struct WorkBlk *WrkPtr;
 1855  
 1856  {  struct MSend *SendPtr;
 1857     struct PMask1 *SecPtr;
 1858     int DatCnt;
 1859     int Cap[2];
 1860     struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 1861     char *InAddr;
 1862     int SpcAvl;
 1863     int TailSpc;
 1864     struct WorkBlk *APtr;
 1865     int Error;
 1866     int d;
 1867     int RemData;
 1868     int Secur;
 1869     int i;
 1870     int NewData;
 1871  
 1872  
 1873     /* DataState format
 1874  
 1875     +--------+----+----+
 1876     ! secur  !eol !sta !   (bits: 8, 4, 4)
 1877     +--------+----+----+
 1878  
 1879     */
 1880  
 1881     /* FlagBuff format
 1882  
 1883     +-----+---+
 1884     !secur!eol!    (bits: 5, 3)
 1885     +-----+---+
 1886  
 1887     */
 1888  
 1889  
 1890     XPtr = WrkPtr -> TCBPtr;
 1891  
 1892     if (XPtr -> State == CLOSED)
 1893     {  return(0);
 1894      }
 1895  
 1896     capac(WrkPtr -> PortFds, Cap, 4);
 1897  
 1898     if (Debug == 1)
 1899     {  printf("DataIn: entered\n");
 1900        /*
 1901        printf("DataIn: %d %d %d %d %d\n", Cap[0], XPtr -> DataState,
 1902        XPtr -> DataHd, XPtr -> DataTl, XPtr -> DataNxt);
 1903        */
 1904      }
 1905  
 1906     if (Cap[0] == 0)
 1907     {  return(0);    /* no data to read */
 1908      }
 1909  
 1910     /*
 1911     if (Debug == 1)
 1912     {  XPtr = WorkPtr -> TCBPtr;
 1913        printf("%d %d %d %d\n", XPtr -> DataState,
 1914        XPtr -> DataHd, XPtr -> DataTl, XPtr -> DataNxt);
 1915        for (i = 0; i < RBUFFSIZE; i++)
 1916        {  printf("%o\n", XPtr -> FlagBuff[i]);
 1917         }
 1918      }
 1919     */
 1920  
 1921     /* calculate space available in input buffer */
 1922     SpcAvl = (XPtr -> DataHd) - (XPtr -> DataTl) -1;
 1923  
 1924     if (SpcAvl < 0)
 1925     {  /* wrap around */
 1926        SpcAvl =+ RBUFFSIZE;
 1927      }
 1928  
 1929     if (SpcAvl == 0)
 1930     {  /* input buffer full */
 1931        return(1);   /* leave work block on queue */
 1932      }
 1933  
 1934     NewData = 0;
 1935  
 1936     /* check if FIN flag set */
 1937     if (((XPtr -> Flags)&010) > 0)
 1938     {  /* check if all data has been received from user */
 1939  
 1940        RemData = XPtr -> DataTl - XPtr -> DataHd;
 1941        if (RemData < 0)
 1942        {  RemData =+ RBUFFSIZE;
 1943         }
 1944  
 1945        if ((XPtr -> LeftSeq + RemData) == (XPtr -> SndFSN))
 1946        {  /* incoming data is beyond the close point! */
 1947           return(0);    /* flush it */
 1948         }
 1949      }
 1950  
 1951     /* read in all data possible */
 1952  
 1953     while((SpcAvl > 0)&&(Cap[0] > 0))
 1954  
 1955     {  /* determine state of input stream */
 1956        switch ((XPtr -> DataState)&017)
 1957  
 1958        {  case 0:    /* read control message */
 1959           {  /* read in message header */
 1960              d = read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, &PortHdr, HDRLNGTH);
 1961              DatCnt = PortHdr.DCount&~0100000;
 1962              if (DatCnt == 0)
 1963              {  /* user closed his data port */
 1964                 /* printf("DataIn: user closed data port to tcp\n"); */
 1965                 return(0);
 1966               }
 1967              /* read in the rest of the message */
 1968              d = read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, CmdUsr, DatCnt);
 1969  
 1970              /* extract the byte count of data message */
 1971              SendPtr = CmdUsr;
 1972              Cap[0] =- (DatCnt + HDRLNGTH);
 1973              /* set state = 1; save EOL, security info */
 1974              XPtr -> DataState = ((SendPtr -> SnSecur) << 8) +
 1975              ((SendPtr -> SnFlags) << 4) + 1;
 1976  
 1977              /* check if security level is within range */
 1978              XPtr -> Flags =& ~0200;
 1979              if (Auth == 1)
 1980              {
 1981                 if ((SendPtr -> SnSecur > XPtr -> ScMaxOut)||
 1982                 (SendPtr -> SnSecur < XPtr -> ScMinOut))
 1983                 {  /* set flag to make data be discarded */
 1984                    XPtr -> Flags =| 0200;   /* discard data */
 1985                  }
 1986               }
 1987              break;
 1988            }
 1989  
 1990           case 1:    /* read in the data message */
 1991           {  /* read in the header */
 1992              d = read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, &PortHdr, HDRLNGTH);
 1993  
 1994              /* set state = 2, resid data */
 1995              /* need to increment to preserve security/flag info */
 1996              XPtr -> DataState =+ 1;
 1997              XPtr -> DataLeft = PortHdr.DCount&~0100000;
 1998              Cap[0] =- HDRLNGTH;
 1999  
 2000              break;
 2001            }
 2002  
 2003           case 2:    /* read some residual data in port */
 2004           {
 2005              DatCnt = XPtr -> DataLeft;
 2006  
 2007              /* check if need to discard */
 2008              if ((Auth == 1)&&(((XPtr -> Flags)&0200) > 0))
 2009              {  /* security level wrong - discard data */
 2010  
 2011                 /* save the count of discarded data */
 2012                 SecPtr = CmdTcp;
 2013                 SecPtr -> YMisc = XPtr -> DataLeft;
 2014  
 2015                 Cap[0] =- DatCnt;
 2016  
 2017                 /* flush the data */
 2018                 while ((XPtr -> DataLeft) > TCPINSZ)
 2019                 {  read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, TcpIn, TCPINSZ);
 2020                    XPtr -> DataLeft =- TCPINSZ;
 2021                  }
 2022                 read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, TcpIn, XPtr -> DataLeft);
 2023  
 2024                 /* inform user of illegal security level */
 2025  
 2026                 SecPtr -> YOpCode = OSECERR;
 2027                 SecPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
 2028                 write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 6);
 2029  
 2030                 /* return datain state to zero */
 2031                 XPtr -> DataState = 0;
 2032               }
 2033  
 2034              else
 2035              {  /* read in the residual data */
 2036                 /* read in only enough to fill buffer */
 2037  
 2038                 InAddr = &(XPtr -> RetranBuff[XPtr -> DataTl]);
 2039  
 2040                 /* calculate space remaining in tail of circular buffer */
 2041  
 2042                 if (XPtr -> DataTl >= XPtr -> DataHd)
 2043                 {  TailSpc = RBUFFSIZE - (XPtr -> DataTl);
 2044                    if (XPtr -> DataHd == 0)
 2045                    {  TailSpc =- 1;    /* special case */
 2046                     }
 2047                  }
 2048                 else
 2049                 {  TailSpc = (XPtr -> DataHd) - (XPtr -> DataTl) - 1;
 2050                  }
 2051  
 2052                 if (DatCnt <= TailSpc)
 2053                 {  d = read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, InAddr, DatCnt);
 2054  
 2055                    Cap[0] =- DatCnt;
 2056                    SpcAvl =- DatCnt;
 2057                    NewData = 1;
 2058  
 2059                    /* set EOL, security levels here */
 2060  
 2061                    if (Auth == 1)
 2062                    {  Secur = ((XPtr -> DataState) >> 5)&0170;
 2063                       for (i = XPtr -> DataTl; i < (XPtr -> DataTl) + DatCnt; i++)
 2064                       {  XPtr -> FlagBuff[i] = Secur;
 2065                        }
 2066  
 2067                       /* check for EOL */
 2068                       if ((((XPtr -> DataState) >> 4)&01) == 1)
 2069                       {  /* set EOL Flag */
 2070                          XPtr -> FlagBuff[i-1] =| 1;
 2071                        }
 2072                     }
 2073  
 2074                    (XPtr -> DataTl) =+ DatCnt;
 2075  
 2076                    if ((XPtr -> DataTl) == RBUFFSIZE)
 2077                    {  XPtr -> DataTl = 0;
 2078                     }
 2079  
 2080                    /* return data-in state to zero */
 2081                    XPtr -> DataState = 0;
 2082  
 2083                  }
 2084  
 2085                 else
 2086                 {  d = read(WrkPtr -> PortFds, InAddr, TailSpc);
 2087  
 2088                    Cap[0] =- TailSpc;
 2089                    SpcAvl =- TailSpc;
 2090                    NewData = 1;
 2091  
 2092                    /* set security level */
 2093                    if (Auth == 1)
 2094                    {  Secur = ((XPtr -> DataState) >> 5)&0170;
 2095                       for (i = XPtr -> DataTl; i < (XPtr -> DataTl) + TailSpc; i++)
 2096                       {  XPtr -> FlagBuff[i] = Secur;
 2097                        }
 2098                     }
 2099  
 2100                    XPtr -> DataLeft =- TailSpc;
 2101  
 2102                    XPtr -> DataTl =+ TailSpc;
 2103                    if ((XPtr -> DataTl) == RBUFFSIZE)
 2104                    {  XPtr -> DataTl = 0;
 2105                     }
 2106                  }
 2107               }
 2108              break;
 2109            }
 2110         }
 2111      }
 2112  
 2113     if (NewData == 1)
 2114  
 2115     {  if ((XPtr -> State == ESTAB)|(XPtr -> State == CLOSEWAIT))
 2116        {  MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
 2117         }
 2118      }
 2119  
 2120     if (Cap[0] == 0)
 2121     {  return(0);    /* remove work block */
 2122      }
 2123     return(1);    /* leave work on queue */
 2124   }
