 2159  
 2160  /* 
 2161   */
 2162  /* Subroutine for parsing "tcptable" */
 2163  
 2164  ReadTbl()
 2165  {
 2166     extern int LocNet;
 2167     extern int LocHost;
 2168     extern int LocImp;
 2169     extern int InLink;
 2170     extern int ANLngth;
 2171     extern int NetTable[NUMNET];
 2172  
 2173     int GtHost;
 2174     int GtImp;
 2175     int State;
 2176     int i, k;
 2177     char Chr;
 2178     char Numb[12];
 2179     int Param[NUMTCPTBL];
 2180  
 2181     struct buf
 2182     {  int fildes;
 2183        int nunused;
 2184        char *xfree;
 2185        char buff[512];
 2186      } ibuf;
 2187  
 2188  
 2189     /* first line of table has the following example format :
 2190  
 2191     Net Host Imp Link ANLngth GtHost GtImp : 10 0 63 155 2 2 5
 2192  
 2193     where:
 2194  
 2195     Net     : the local net tcp lives on
 2196     Host    : the local host tcp lives on
 2197     Imp     : the local imp tcp host is attached to
 2198     Link    : the link number for internet traffic
 2199     ANLngth : the Arpanet leader length in words
 2200     GtHost  : the smart gateway host to forward to unknown nets
 2201     GtImp   : the imp that the smart gateway host is attached to
 2202  
 2203     */
 2204  
 2205     /* The next N lines contain gateway routing information.
 2206  
 2207     Example:
 2208  
 2209     arpanet -> rccnet           :1: 10 3 2 5
 2210  
 2211     where:
 2212  
 2213     the parameter in colon brackets is the line enable bit,
 2214     with a 1 => use this line and 0 => ignore this line.
 2215  
 2216     format of following parameters:
 2217  
 2218     1. from net
 2219     2. to net
 2220     3. gateway host
 2221     4. gateway imp
 2222  
 2223     */
 2224  
 2225  
 2226     i = fopen("tcptable", &ibuf);
 2227     if (i == -1)
 2228     {  printf("make me a tcptable!\n");
 2229        exit();    /* die */
 2230      }
 2231  
 2232     State = 6;  /* read the first line */
 2233  
 2234     while((Chr = getc(&ibuf)) != -1)
 2235     {
 2236        switch(State)
 2237        {  case 0:    /* looking for colon */
 2238           {  if (Chr == ':')
 2239              {  State = 1;
 2240               }
 2241              break;
 2242            }
 2243  
 2244           case 1:    /* get enable bit */
 2245           {  if (Chr == '0')
 2246              {  /* this line is disabled */
 2247                 State = 2;    /* flush line */
 2248               }
 2249              else
 2250              {  State = 3;    /* look for colon */
 2251               }
 2252              break;
 2253            }
 2254  
 2255           case 2:    /* flush line */
 2256           {  if (Chr == '\n')
 2257              {  State = 0;
 2258               }
 2259              break;
 2260            }
 2261  
 2262           case 3:   /* look for terminating colon */
 2263           {  if (Chr == ':')
 2264              {  State = 4;    /* get parameters */
 2265                 i = 0;
 2266                 k = 0;
 2267               }
 2268              else
 2269              {  return(-1);
 2270               }
 2271              break;
 2272            }
 2273  
 2274           case 4:    /* get parameters */
 2275           {  if ((Chr != ' ')&&(Chr != '\t'))
 2276              {  State = 5;    /* stop at next blank/tab */
 2277               }
 2278              Numb[i] = Chr;
 2279              i++;
 2280  
 2281              if (i == 12)
 2282              {  return(-1);
 2283               }
 2284  
 2285              break;
 2286            }
 2287  
 2288           case 5:    /* wait for blank/tab/nl */
 2289           {  if ((Chr == ' ')||(Chr == '\t')||(Chr == '\n'))
 2290              {  Numb[i] = '\0';
 2291                 Param[k] = atoi(Numb);
 2292                 if (k == 0)
 2293                 {  if (Param[0] != LocNet)
 2294                    {  State = 2;    /* flush line */
 2295                       break;
 2296                     }
 2297                  }
 2298                 k++;
 2299  
 2300                 if ((k == 4)||(Chr == '\n'))
 2301                 {  /* enter in gateway table */
 2302  
 2303                    if (Param[1] >= NUMNET)
 2304                    {  return(-1);
 2305                     }
 2306  
 2307                    NetTable[Param[1]] = (Param[2] << 8) + Param[3];
 2308  
 2309                    if (Chr == '\n')
 2310                    {  State = 0;
 2311                       break;
 2312                     }
 2313                    State = 2;    /* flush line */
 2314                    break;
 2315                  }
 2316  
 2317                 i = 0;
 2318                 State = 4;   /* get next parameter */
 2319               }
 2320              else
 2321              {  Numb[i] = Chr;
 2322                 i++;
 2323  
 2324                 if (i == 12)
 2325                 {  return(-1);
 2326                  }
 2327               }
 2328              break;
 2329            }
 2330  
 2331           case 6:    /* looking for first line colon */
 2332           {  if (Chr == ':')
 2333              {  State = 7;
 2334  
 2335                 for (i = 0; i < NUMTCPTBL; i++)
 2336                 {  Param[i] = 0;
 2337                  }
 2338  
 2339                 i = 0;
 2340                 k = 0;
 2341               }
 2342              break;
 2343            }
 2344  
 2345           case 7:    /* getting parameters */
 2346           {  if ((Chr != ' ')&&(Chr != '\t'))
 2347              {  State = 8;    /* stop at next blank/tab */
 2348               }
 2349  
 2350              Numb[i] = Chr;
 2351              i++;
 2352  
 2353              if (i == 12)
 2354              {  return(-1);
 2355               }
 2356              break;
 2357            }
 2358  
 2359           case 8:    /* wait for blank/tab/nl */
 2360           {  if ((Chr == ' ')||(Chr == '\t')||(Chr == '\n'))
 2361              {  Numb[i] = '\0';
 2362  
 2363                 i = 0;
 2364                 Param[k] = atoi(Numb);
 2365                 k++;
 2366                 if ((k == NUMTCPTBL)||(Chr == '\n'))
 2367                 {  /* set up tcp network values */
 2368  
 2369                    LocNet  = Param[0];
 2370                    LocHost = Param[1];
 2371                    LocImp  = Param[2];
 2372                    InLink  = Param[3];
 2373                    ANLngth = Param[4];
 2374                    GtHost  = Param[5];
 2375                    GtImp   = Param[6];
 2376  
 2377                    if (Chr == '\n')
 2378                    {  State = 0;    /* get gateway connectivity values */
 2379                       if (k != NUMTCPTBL)
 2380                       {  return(-1);
 2381                        }
 2382                       break;
 2383                     }
 2384                    State = 2;    /* flush line */
 2385                  }
 2386                 else
 2387                 {  State = 7;    /* get next parameter */
 2388                  }
 2389               }
 2390              else
 2391              {  Numb[i] = Chr;
 2392                 i++;
 2393                 if (i == 12)
 2394                 {  return(-1);
 2395                  }
 2396               }
 2397              break;
 2398            }
 2399         }
 2400      }
 2401  
 2402     if (NetTable[LocNet] == 0)
 2403     {  /* set flag sending net output directly to host */
 2404        NetTable[LocNet] = -1;
 2405      }
 2406  
 2407     /* for unknown nets - send to more intelligent gateway */
 2408  
 2409     for (i = 0; i < NUMNET; i++)
 2410     {  if (NetTable[i] == 0)
 2411        {  NetTable[i] = (GtHost << 8) + GtImp;
 2412         }
 2413      }
 2414  
 2415     close(ibuf.fildes);
 2416  
 2417     return(0);
 2418  
 2419   }
 2420  
 2421  
 2422  
 2423  /* 
 2424   */
 2425  /* Subroutine for putting a work block on a work queue */
 2426  
 2427  MakeWrk(Queue, WFlags, WTcbPtr, WFds)
 2428  
 2429  int Queue;             /* specifies the particular work queue */
 2430  int WFlags;
 2431  struct TcpTcb *WTcbPtr;
 2432  int WFds;
 2433  
 2434  {  register struct WorkBlk *TempPtr;
 2435     int Stop;
 2436  
 2437     /* check if duplicate entry */
 2438  
 2439     TempPtr = WorkHead -> NextWork;
 2440     Stop = 0;
 2441  
 2442     while ((TempPtr -> NextWork != 0)&&(Stop == 0))
 2443     {
 2444        if ((TempPtr -> PortFlags == WFlags)&&
 2445        (TempPtr -> TCBPtr == WTcbPtr)&&(TempPtr -> PortFds == WFds))
 2446        {  /* found duplicate entry */
 2447           /* delete old block */
 2448           (TempPtr -> NextWork) -> LastWork = TempPtr -> LastWork;
 2449           (TempPtr -> LastWork) -> NextWork = TempPtr -> NextWork;
 2450           TempPtr -> NextWork = WorkAvail;
 2451           WorkAvail = TempPtr;
 2452           Stop = 1;
 2453         }
 2454        TempPtr = TempPtr -> NextWork;
 2455      }
 2456  
 2457     if (WorkAvail == 0)
 2458     {  /* no more blocks */
 2459        printf("MakeWrk: no more work blocks\n");
 2460        return(ENOBUFS);
 2461      }
 2462  
 2463     TempPtr = WorkAvail;
 2464     WorkAvail = WorkAvail -> NextWork;
 2465  
 2466     TempPtr -> LastWork = WorkTail -> LastWork;
 2467     TempPtr -> NextWork = WorkTail;
 2468     (WorkTail -> LastWork) -> NextWork = TempPtr;
 2469     WorkTail -> LastWork = TempPtr;
 2470  
 2471     /* fillin the block */
 2472     TempPtr -> PortFlags = WFlags;
 2473     TempPtr -> TCBPtr = WTcbPtr;
 2474     TempPtr -> PortFds = WFds;
 2475  
 2476     return(0);
 2477   }
 2478  
 2479  /* 
 2480   */
 2481  /* Subroutine for unsigned long compare */
 2482  
 2483  ULCompare(A, B)
 2484  
 2485  long *A, *B;
 2486  {  /* returns: 0 = equal */
 2487     /*             1 = A > B */
 2488     /*             2 = A < B */
 2489  
 2490     if (*A == *B)
 2491     {  return(0);
 2492      }
 2493     if ((*A - *B) < 0)
 2494     {  return(2);
 2495      }
 2496     return(1);
 2497   }
 2498  
 2499  
 2500  /* 
 2501   */
 2502  /* Subroutine for putting a TCB on the retransmission queue */
 2503  
 2504  QTcb(XPtr)
 2505  
 2506  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2507  
 2508  {  int First;
 2509     int Time;
 2510     struct TcpTcb *SPtr;
 2511     int Stop;
 2512     int AbsTime;
 2513  
 2514  
 2515     /* check debug parameter */
 2516     if (NoRetran == 1)
 2517     {  /* no retransmissions allowed today */
 2518        return(0);
 2519      }
 2520  
 2521     /* check if TCB is already on retransmission queue */
 2522  
 2523     if (XPtr -> NxtRetran != 0)
 2524     {  /* already linked in */
 2525        return(0);
 2526      }
 2527  
 2528     /* determine next retransmission time */
 2529  
 2530     if (XPtr -> NumRetran >= RetCount)
 2531     {  /* go into slow mode */
 2532        Time = *TimePtr + RetSlow;
 2533      }
 2534     else
 2535     {  /* still in fast mode */
 2536        Time = *TimePtr + RetFast;
 2537      }
 2538  
 2539     if (RetranHd == 0)
 2540     {  /* empty queue */
 2541        RetranHd = XPtr;
 2542        XPtr -> NxtRetran = XPtr;
 2543        XPtr -> LstRetran = XPtr;
 2544        XPtr -> RetranTm = Time;
 2545        return(0);
 2546      }
 2547  
 2548     /* find insertion point */
 2549     First = 1;
 2550     SPtr = RetranHd;
 2551     AbsTime = 0;
 2552     Stop = 0;
 2553  
 2554     do
 2555     {  AbsTime =+ SPtr -> RetranTm;
 2556        if (AbsTime < Time)
 2557        {  First = 0;
 2558           SPtr = SPtr -> NxtRetran;
 2559         }
 2560        else
 2561        {  Stop = 1;
 2562         }
 2563      }
 2564     while((Stop == 0)&&(SPtr != RetranHd));
 2565  
 2566     XPtr -> NxtRetran = SPtr;
 2567     XPtr -> LstRetran = SPtr -> LstRetran;
 2568     (SPtr -> LstRetran) -> NxtRetran = XPtr;
 2569     SPtr -> LstRetran = XPtr;
 2570  
 2571     if (First == 1)
 2572     {  /* TCB is at head of queue */
 2573        XPtr -> RetranTm = Time;
 2574        SPtr -> RetranTm =- XPtr -> RetranTm;
 2575        RetranHd = XPtr;
 2576        return(0);
 2577      }
 2578  
 2579     if (SPtr == RetranHd)
 2580     {  /* TCB is at end of queue */
 2581        XPtr -> RetranTm = Time - AbsTime;
 2582        return(0);
 2583      }
 2584  
 2585     /* TCB is in middle of queue */
 2586     XPtr -> RetranTm = Time - AbsTime + (SPtr -> RetranTm);
 2587     SPtr -> RetranTm =- XPtr -> RetranTm;
 2588     return(0);
 2589   }
 2590  
 2591  
 2592  
 2593  /* 
 2594   */
 2595  /* Subroutine for removing TCB from retransmission queue */
 2596  
 2597  UnQTcb(XPtr)
 2598  
 2599  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2600  
 2601  {  /* check if not queued */
 2602     if (XPtr -> NxtRetran == 0)
 2603     {  return(0);   /* not queued */
 2604      }
 2605  
 2606     /* check for single entry */
 2607     if (XPtr -> NxtRetran == XPtr)
 2608     {  /* just one entry in queue */
 2609        RetranHd = 0;
 2610        XPtr -> NxtRetran = 0;
 2611        return(0);
 2612      }
 2613  
 2614     (XPtr -> NxtRetran) -> LstRetran = XPtr -> LstRetran;
 2615     (XPtr -> LstRetran) -> NxtRetran = XPtr -> NxtRetran;
 2616  
 2617     if (XPtr -> NxtRetran != RetranHd)
 2618     {  /* update time increment */
 2619        (XPtr -> NxtRetran) -> RetranTm =+ XPtr -> RetranTm;
 2620      }
 2621  
 2622     if (XPtr == RetranHd)
 2623     {  /* deleted head of queue */
 2624        RetranHd = XPtr -> NxtRetran;
 2625      }
 2626  
 2627     XPtr -> NxtRetran = 0;
 2628  
 2629   }
 2630  
 2631  
 2632  
 2633  
 2634  /* 
 2635   */
 2636  /* Subroutine for cleaning up an aborted connection */
 2637  
 2638  Abort(XPtr, Flag)
 2639  
 2640  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2641  int Flag;
 2642  
 2643  {  struct TcpTcb *TempPtr;
 2644     struct PMask1 *AckPtr;
 2645     struct WorkBlk *WrkPtr;
 2646     int Stop;
 2647     int i,j,k;
 2648  
 2649     /* disable wakeups on data ports */
 2650     awtdis(XPtr -> SendPort);
 2651     awtdis(XPtr -> RecvPort);
 2652  
 2653     /* close the ports */
 2654  
 2655     close(XPtr -> SendPort);
 2656     close(XPtr -> RecvPort);
 2657  
 2658     /* clear FdsBffer entries */
 2659  
 2660     FdsBffer[XPtr -> SendPort].BuffFlags = 0;
 2661     FdsBffer[XPtr -> RecvPort].BuffFlags = 0;
 2662  
 2663     /* remove bit in PortMap if applicable */
 2664     if ((XPtr -> SrcePort >= BASEPORT)&&
 2665     (XPtr -> SrcePort < BASEPORT + 0400))
 2666     {  /* clear the Port Map bit */
 2667  
 2668        k = 1;    /* bit index */
 2669        j = (XPtr -> SrcePort)&017;
 2670  
 2671        for (i = 0; i < j; i++)
 2672        {  k =<< 1;
 2673         }
 2674  
 2675        PortMap[((XPtr -> SrcePort) >> 4)&017] =& ~k;  
 2676      }
 2677  
 2678     /* remove TCB from retransmission Q if applicable */
 2679  
 2680     UnQTcb(XPtr);
 2681  
 2682     /* remove work queue entries associated with TCB */
 2683  
 2684     WrkPtr = WorkHead -> NextWork;
 2685  
 2686     while(WrkPtr -> NextWork != 0)
 2687     {  TempPtr = WrkPtr -> NextWork;
 2688        if (WrkPtr -> TCBPtr == XPtr)
 2689        {  /* found an entry to remove */
 2690           (WrkPtr -> NextWork) -> LastWork = WrkPtr -> LastWork;
 2691           (WrkPtr -> LastWork) -> NextWork = WrkPtr -> NextWork;
 2692           WrkPtr -> NextWork = WorkAvail;
 2693           WorkAvail = WrkPtr;
 2694         }
 2695        WrkPtr = TempPtr;
 2696      }
 2697  
 2698     if (Flag == 0)
 2699  
 2700     {  /* send ACK to user */
 2701  
 2702        AckPtr = CmdTcp;
 2703        AckPtr -> YOpCode = OACK;
 2704        AckPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
 2705        write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
 2706      }
 2707  
 2708     /* delete TCB */
 2709     if (XPtr == TcbHd)
 2710     {  TcbHd = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 2711        XPtr -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 2712        TcbAvl = XPtr;
 2713      }
 2714     else
 2715     {  Stop = 0;
 2716        TempPtr = TcbHd;
 2717  
 2718        while((TempPtr -> NxtActTcb != 0)&&(Stop == 0))
 2719        {
 2720           if (TempPtr -> NxtActTcb == XPtr)
 2721           {  Stop = 1;
 2722            }
 2723           else
 2724           {  TempPtr = TempPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 2725            }
 2726         }
 2727  
 2728        if (Stop == 1)
 2729        {  /* found it, as expected */
 2730           TempPtr -> NxtActTcb = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 2731           XPtr -> NxtActTcb = TcbAvl;
 2732           TcbAvl = XPtr;
 2733         }
 2734      }
 2735   }
 2736  
 2737  
 2738  /* Subroutine which verifies that ptr to TCB is valid */
 2739  
 2740  ChkPtr(XPtr, Fds)
 2741  
 2742  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2743  int Fds;
 2744  
 2745  {  struct TcpTcb *TPtr;
 2746  
 2747     TPtr = TcbHd;
 2748  
 2749     while (TPtr != 0)
 2750     {  if (XPtr == TPtr)
 2751        {  /* check correct file desc */
 2752           if (Fds == TPtr -> UserCmdPort)
 2753           {  return(0);    /* everything okay */
 2754            }
 2755           else
 2756           {  printf("ChkPtr: bad fds\n");
 2757              return(-1);
 2758            }
 2759         }
 2760        else
 2761        {  TPtr = TPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 2762         }
 2763      }
 2764  
 2765     printf("ChkPtr: bad ptr\n");
 2766     return(-1);
 2767   }
 2768  
 2769  
 2770  
 2771  Status()
 2772  
 2773  {
 2774     extern int Auth;
 2775     extern long StopSkt[NSTOP];
 2776     extern int StopTcc[NSTOP];
 2777  
 2778     struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2779     int Cap0[2];
 2780     int Cap1[2];
 2781     int Cap2[2];
 2782     int Cap3[2];
 2783     int i;
 2784     struct WorkBlk *WPtr;
 2785  
 2786     struct
 2787     {  char XHost;
 2788        char XNet;
 2789        int XImp;
 2790      } *SktPtr;
 2791  
 2792     /* print the capacity of the ear port */
 2793  
 2794     capac(EarFds, Cap2, 4);
 2795  
 2796     printf("Ear: %d\n", Cap2[0]);
 2797  
 2798     /* print the work queue */
 2799  
 2800     WPtr = WorkHead -> NextWork;
 2801  
 2802     while(WPtr -> NextWork != 0)
 2803     {
 2804        printf("WrkQ: %d %d %o\n", WPtr -> PortFlags,
 2805        WPtr -> PortFds, WPtr -> TCBPtr);
 2806  
 2807        WPtr = WPtr -> NextWork;
 2808      }
 2809  
 2810  
 2811     /* print active entries in FdsBffer */
 2812  
 2813     for (i = 0; i < FDSSIZE; i++)
 2814     {
 2815        if (FdsBffer[i].BuffFlags != 0)
 2816        {  printf("Fds: %d %d %o\n", FdsBffer[i].BuffFlags,
 2817           FdsBffer[i].BuffFds, FdsBffer[i].BuffTcbPtr);
 2818         }
 2819      }
 2820  
 2821  
 2822     /* print the active TCB list */
 2823  
 2824     XPtr = TcbHd;
 2825  
 2826     while (XPtr != 0)
 2827     {
 2828        capac(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, Cap0, 4);
 2829        capac(XPtr -> UserCmdPort, Cap1, 4);
 2830        capac(XPtr -> SendPort, Cap2, 4);
 2831        capac(XPtr -> RecvPort, Cap3, 4);
 2832  
 2833        printf("TCB: %o %d %d %d %d S: %d N: %o H: %o I: %o, PID: %d\n",
 2834        XPtr, Cap0[1], Cap1[0], Cap2[0], Cap3[1], XPtr -> State,
 2835        XPtr -> DstNet, XPtr -> DstHstH, XPtr -> DstHstL, XPtr -> ProcID);
 2836  
 2837        XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 2838  
 2839      }
 2840  
 2841     /* print the retransmission queue */
 2842  
 2843     if (RetranHd != 0)
 2844  
 2845     {  XPtr = RetranHd;
 2846  
 2847        do
 2848        {  printf("RQ: %o %d\n", XPtr, XPtr -> RetranTm);
 2849           XPtr = XPtr -> NxtRetran;
 2850         }
 2851        while(XPtr != RetranHd);
 2852      }
 2853  
 2854     if (Auth == 1)
 2855     {  /* print the Stop/Resume entries */
 2856        printf("Stop Table\n");
 2857  
 2858        for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 2859        {  if (StopSkt[i] != 0)
 2860           {  SktPtr = &StopSkt[i];
 2861              printf("%d %d %d\n", SktPtr -> XNet,
 2862              SktPtr -> XHost, SktPtr -> XImp);
 2863            }
 2864         }
 2865  
 2866        for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
 2867        {  if (StopTcc[i] < 0)
 2868           {  printf("%d ", StopTcc[i]&0377);
 2869            }
 2870         }
 2871        printf("\n");
 2872      }
 2873   }

 2877  /* Subroutine for sending commands to user process */
 2878  
 2879  CmdOut(XPtr, OpCode, Param1, Param2)
 2880  
 2881  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
 2882  int OpCode;
 2883  int Param1;
 2884  int Param2;
 2885  
 2886  {  struct PMask1 *CPtr;
 2887     struct PMask1 *EPtr;
 2888     int DatCnt;
 2889  
 2890     CPtr = CmdTcp;
 2891     CPtr -> YOpCode = OpCode;
 2892     CPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
 2893  
 2894     switch(OpCode)
 2895     {
 2896        case OERR:
 2897        case OCHNG:
 2898        case ONETMSG:
 2899        {  CPtr -> YMisc = Param1;
 2900           DatCnt = 6;
 2901           break;
 2902         }
 2903  
 2904        case ORESET:
 2905        case OACK:
 2906        {  DatCnt = 4;
 2907           break;
 2908         }
 2909  
 2910        default:
 2911        {  printf("CmdOut: bad opcode\n");
 2912         }
 2913      }
 2914  
 2915     write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, DatCnt);
 2916   }

SHA-256 4ac5384c69398d8fcf935b1405650069493ece164aa91b2e4414681e1c43cbb1

    1  #
    2  # include "tcpstru.h"
    3  
    4  Authorize(Ident, InfoPtr, XPtr)
    5  
    6  char Ident;
    7  struct MOpen *InfoPtr;
    8  struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
    9  
   10  {
   11  
   12     int i,j,k;
   13     int ParamVal[20];
   14     int Found;
   15  
   16     /* check for proper Tcc parameter */
   17     if ((InfoPtr -> OpenMode == 0)&&(InfoPtr -> NumbTcc > 1))
   18     {  printf("X9\n");
   19        return(EBADPARAM);
   20      }
   21  
   22     /* number of entries returned in k */
   23  
   24     k = GetFields(Ident, &ParamVal);
   25  
   26     if (k == -1)
   27     {  /* some error present */
   28        return(EUNAUTH);
   29      }
   30  
   31     /* must have found it */
   32  
   33     /* check that authtable entries are okay */
   34  
   35     if ((ParamVal[0] > 1)||(ParamVal[0] < 0))
   36     {  printf("Auth: bad priv %d\n", Ident);
   37        return(EBADPARAM);
   38      }
   39  
   40     for (i = 0; i < 6; i++)
   41     {  if ((ParamVal[i] > 15)||(ParamVal[i] < 0))
   42        {  printf("Auth: bad S/P value %d\n", Ident);
   43           return(EBADPARAM);
   44         }
   45      }
   46  
   47     if ((ParamVal[2] > ParamVal[1])||(ParamVal[4] > ParamVal[3]))
   48     {  printf("Auth: sec fields swapped %d\n", Ident);
   49        return(EBADPARAM);
   50      }
   51  
   52     for (i = 6; i < k; i++)
   53     {  if ((ParamVal[i] < 0)||(ParamVal[i] > 255))
   54        {  printf("Auth: bad tcc %d\n", Ident);
   55           return(EBADPARAM);
   56         }
   57      }
   58  
   59     /* check parameters with authtable entries */
   60  
   61     if (InfoPtr -> AbsSecur < 0)
   62     {  /* absolute security level specified */
   63        InfoPtr -> AbsSecur =& 0177;
   64  
   65        if (InfoPtr -> AbsSecur > 15)
   66        {  /* too big */
   67           return(EBADPARAM);
   68         }
   69  
   70        if ((InfoPtr -> AbsSecur > ParamVal[3])||
   71        (InfoPtr -> AbsSecur < ParamVal[4]))
   72        {  /* out of range */
   73           printf("X1\n");
   74           return(EUNAUTH);
   75         }
   76        XPtr -> ScMaxOut = InfoPtr -> AbsSecur;
   77        XPtr -> ScMinOut = InfoPtr -> AbsSecur;
   78      }
   79     else
   80     {  if (InfoPtr -> MaxSecur < 0)
   81        {  /* maximum security level specified */
   82           InfoPtr -> MaxSecur =& 0177;
   83  
   84           if (InfoPtr -> MaxSecur > 15)
   85           {  /* too big */
   86              return(EBADPARAM);
   87            }
   88  
   89           if ((InfoPtr -> MaxSecur > ParamVal[3])||
   90           (InfoPtr -> MaxSecur < ParamVal[4]))
   91           {  /* out of range */
   92              printf("X2\n");
   93              return(EUNAUTH);
   94            }
   95           XPtr -> ScMaxOut = InfoPtr -> MaxSecur;
   96           XPtr -> ScMinOut = ParamVal[4];
   97         }
   98        else
   99        {  if (InfoPtr -> OpenMode == 1)
  100           {  /* unspecified listen here */
  101              XPtr -> ScMaxOut = -1;  /* mark unspecified */
  102              XPtr -> ScMinOut = -1;
  103            }
  104           else
  105           {  XPtr -> ScMaxOut = ParamVal[3];
  106              XPtr -> ScMinOut = ParamVal[4];
  107            }
  108         }
  109      }
  110  
  111     /* set the security parameters for net in */
  112     XPtr -> ScMaxIn  = ParamVal[1];  /* max net to user */
  113     XPtr -> ScMinIn  = ParamVal[2];  /* min net to user */
  114  
  115     /* do the precedence checking */
  116     if ((InfoPtr -> SendPrec) < 0)
  117     {  /* send precedence specified */
  118        InfoPtr -> SendPrec =& 0177;
  119  
  120        if (InfoPtr -> SendPrec > 15)
  121        {  /* too big */
  122           return(EBADPARAM);
  123         }
  124  
  125        if (InfoPtr -> SendPrec > ParamVal[5])
  126        {  /* out of range */
  127           printf("X6\n");
  128           return(EUNAUTH);
  129         }
  130        else
  131        {  XPtr -> SndPrec = InfoPtr -> SendPrec;
  132         }
  133      }
  134     else
  135     {  XPtr -> SndPrec = MINPREC;
  136      }
  137  
  138     if ((InfoPtr -> RecvPrec) < 0)
  139     {  /* minimum receive precedence specified */
  140        InfoPtr -> RecvPrec =& 0177;
  141  
  142        if (InfoPtr -> RecvPrec > 15)
  143        {  /* too big */
  144           return(EBADPARAM);
  145         }
  146  
  147        XPtr -> MnRcvPrec = InfoPtr -> RecvPrec;
  148  
  149      }
  150     else
  151     {  XPtr -> MnRcvPrec = MINPREC;
  152      }
  153  
  154     /* set maximum precedence since maximum
  155     receive precedence is not specified by user */
  156     XPtr -> MxRcvPrec = MAXPREC;
  157  
  158     /* save auth table max send prec */
  159     XPtr -> ATPrec = ParamVal[5];
  160  
  161     /* do the TCC checking */
  162  
  163     if (InfoPtr -> NumbTcc == 0)
  164     {  /* unspecified - get values from auth table */
  165        printf("no TCC\n");
  166        if (InfoPtr -> OpenMode == 0)
  167        {  /* just get the first one */
  168           InfoPtr -> OTcc[0] = ParamVal[6];
  169           InfoPtr -> NumbTcc = 1;
  170         }
  171        else
  172        {  i = 0;
  173           for (j = 6; j < k; j++)
  174           {  InfoPtr -> OTcc[i] = ParamVal[j];
  175              i++;
  176            }
  177           InfoPtr -> NumbTcc = k - 6;
  178         }
  179      }
  180     else
  181     {  /* check each TCC against authtable values */
  182  
  183        for (i = 0; i < InfoPtr -> NumbTcc; i++)
  184        {  j = 6;
  185           Found = 0;
  186           while((Found == 0)&&(j < k))
  187           {  if (InfoPtr -> OTcc[i] == ParamVal[j])
  188              {  Found = 1;
  189               }
  190              else
  191              {  j++;
  192               }
  193            }
  194  
  195           if (Found == 0)
  196           {  /* bad Tcc */
  197              printf("X8\n");
  198              return(EUNAUTH);
  199            }
  200         }
  201      }
  202     /* copy the TCC list into the TCB */
  203     for (i = 0; i < InfoPtr -> NumbTcc; i++)
  204     {  XPtr -> TccList[i] = InfoPtr -> OTcc[i];
  205      }
  206     XPtr -> TCCCnt = InfoPtr -> NumbTcc;
  207  
  208     return(0);
  209   }
  210  
  211  
  212  GetFields(Ident, PPtr)
  213  
  214  int Ident;
  215  
  216  struct
  217  {  int ParamVal[20];
  218   } *PPtr;
  219  
  220  
  221  {
  222     struct buf
  223     {  int fildes;
  224        int nunused;
  225        char *xfree;
  226        char buff[512];
  227      } ibuf;
  228  
  229     int Numb;
  230     char UserNmb[6];
  231     char Chr;
  232     int State;
  233     int Halt;
  234     int Found;
  235     int Error;
  236     int i,j,k;
  237     int AuthFds;
  238  
  239     /* scan Authorization Table for this user */
  240  
  241     Error = fopen("authtable", &ibuf);
  242  
  243     if (Error == -1)
  244     {  /* can't open it */
  245        printf("Authorize: can't open auth table\n");
  246        return(-1);
  247      }
  248  
  249     AuthFds = ibuf.fildes;
  250  
  251     State = 0;    /* looking for colon */
  252     while ((Chr = getc(&ibuf)) != -1)
  253     {
  254        switch(State)
  255        {  case 0:    /* looking for colon */
  256           {  if (Chr == ':')
  257              {  /* get user number */
  258                 i = 0;
  259                 State = 1;    /* check user number */
  260               }
  261              break;
  262            }
  263           case 1:    /* check user number */
  264           {  if (Chr == ':')
  265              {  /* end of string */
  266                 UserNmb[i] = '\0';    /* terminator */
  267  
  268                 /* convert string to number */
  269                 Numb = atoi(UserNmb);
  270  
  271                 if (Numb == Ident)
  272                 {  /* found it */
  273                    State = 2;    /* get rest of values */
  274                    k = 0;
  275                    i = 0;
  276                  }
  277                 else
  278                 {  /* go to next line */
  279                    State = 3;    /* look for end of line */
  280                  }
  281               }
  282              else
  283              {  /* stash it away */
  284                 UserNmb[i] = Chr;
  285                 i++;
  286               }
  287              break;
  288            }
  289  
  290           case 2:    /* get rest of values */
  291           {  switch(Chr)
  292              {  case ':':
  293                 case '\n':
  294                 {  /* end of string */
  295                    UserNmb[i] = '\0';
  296                    PPtr -> ParamVal[k] = atoi(UserNmb);
  297                    i = 0;
  298                    k++;
  299  
  300                    if (k > 6 + NUMBTCC)
  301                    {  /* too many entries */
  302                       printf("X5\n");
  303                       close(AuthFds);
  304                       return(-1);
  305                     }
  306  
  307                    if (Chr == '\n')
  308                    {  /* end of line */
  309  
  310                       if (k < 7)     /* the minimum number of entries */
  311                       {  close(AuthFds);
  312                          return(-1);
  313                        }
  314  
  315                       close(AuthFds);
  316                       return(k);
  317                     }
  318                    break;
  319                  }
  320  
  321                 default:
  322                 {  UserNmb[i] = Chr;
  323                    i++;
  324                  }
  325               }
  326              break;
  327            }
  328  
  329           case 3:    /* look for new line */
  330           {  if (Chr == '\n')
  331              {  State = 0;
  332               }
  333              break;
  334            }
  335         }
  336      }
  337     printf("X4\n");
  338     close(AuthFds);
  339     return(-1);
  340   }

BBN PDP-11 / tcp/psipin.c
SHA-256 acb2a44d7ed6fdb3ca42b3bdc4e50386ebc927d1202efb4a6a1a951136b091de

    1  #
    2  # include "tcpstru.h"
    3  # include "hdrmasks.h"
    4  
    5  # define MAXMSG      1012       /* input/output buffer length */
    6  # define ENDARGS       -1       /* end of Log argument list */
    7  
    8  /* ARPANET message types */
    9  # define REGULAR        0
   10  # define NOP            4
   11  # define RFNM           5
   12  # define NOHOST         7
   13  
   14  
   15  PsipIn(WrkPtr)
   16  
   17  struct WorkBlk *WrkPtr;
   18  
   19  {
   20     extern int ShortIn;
   21     extern int ANLngth;
   22     extern int Auth;
   23     extern int Debug;
   24     extern char TcpIn[TCPINSZ];
   25     extern char TcpOut[TCPOTSZ];
   26     extern struct FdsBlk FdsBffer[FDSSIZE];
   27     extern int WindNum;
   28     extern int WindDenom;
   29     extern struct TcpTcb *TcbHd;
   30     extern int RWindow;
   31     extern char CmdTcp[CMDTCPSIZE];
   32     extern int NetFdsW;
   33     extern int PartWr;
   34     extern char *OutPtr;
   35     extern int OutCnt;
   36     extern long StopSkt[NSTOP];
   37     extern int StopTcc[NSTOP];
   38  
   39     register struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
   40     register struct INMask *INPtr;
   41     register struct TCPMask *TCPPtr;
   42  
   43     struct ANMask *ANPtr;
   44     struct PortLdr PortHdr;
   45     int i;
   46     int INLength;
   47     int Found;
   48     struct BSLMask *BSLPtr;
   49     int Error;
   50     int PktLength;
   51     int Cap[2];
   52     long temp1, temp2;
   53     long UrgNo;
   54     int R1, R2;
   55     char *OctetPtr;
   56     int BCnt;
   57     struct WorkBlk *APtr;
   58     struct PMask1 *ChngPtr;
   59     struct MUrgent *UrgPtr;
   60     struct PMask1 *PPtr;
   61     struct PMask1 *ResetPtr;
   62     struct PMask1 *RejectPtr;
   63     int l;
   64     int *MPtr;
   65     int DataAmt;
   66     int AckOk;
   67     long W;
   68     int V;
   69     struct FdsBlk *FdsBffPtr;
   70     int InPrec;
   71     int InSec;
   72     int InTcc;
   73     int SptOpt;
   74     int TccOk;
   75     int Stat;
   76     int Count;
   77     char *SPtr;
   78     char *DPtr;
   79     int FinOn;
   80     int SynOn;
   81     int FinFlag;
   82  
   83     struct
   84     {  char XHost;
   85        char XNet;
   86        int XImp;
   87      } *SktPtr;
   88  
   89  
   90     if (Debug == 1)
   91     {  printf("PsipIn: entered\n");
   92      }
   93  
   94     /*
   95     if (Debug == 4)
   96     {  Log(6, "s", "PsipIn", ENDARGS);
   97      }
   98     */
   99  
  100     /* The ShortIn flag indicates that a segment addressed
  101     locally is ready in the TcpIn buffer */
  102  
  103     if (ShortIn == 0)
  104     {  /* read segment from the net */
  105        Stat = NetIn();
  106  
  107        if (Stat == -1)
  108        {  return(0);     /* no more incoming segments */
  109         }
  110  
  111        if (Stat == 0)
  112        {  /* non-tcp message */
  113           return(2);        /* move block to end of work queue */
  114         }
  115      }
  116  
  117     INPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH];
  118     TCPPtr = &TcpIn[(((INPtr -> IVerHdr)&017)*4) + BSLLNGTH];
  119  
  120     if (ShortIn == 1)
  121     {  /* log it */
  122        if (Debug == 4)
  123        {  Log(6, "n", INPtr, ENDARGS);
  124           Log(6, "t", TCPPtr, ENDARGS);
  125         }
  126  
  127        ShortIn = 0;
  128      }
  129  
  130     SynOn = 0;
  131     FinOn = 0;
  132  
  133     if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&SYN) > 0)
  134     {  SynOn = 1;
  135      }
  136     if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&FIN) > 0)
  137     {  FinOn = 1;
  138      }
  139  
  140     DataAmt = (INPtr -> ISegLength) - (((INPtr -> IVerHdr)&017)*4) -
  141     ((((TCPPtr ->TTCPFlags)&(0170000)) >> 12)*4);
  142  
  143     PktLength = DataAmt + SynOn + FinOn;
  144  
  145     if (Debug == 1)
  146     {  /* snap the segment info */
  147        printf("PsipIn: seg info: L = %d, F = %o, Src = %d\n",
  148        DataAmt, (TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&0777, TCPPtr -> TSrcePort);
  149      }
  150  
  151     /* check for internet options */
  152  
  153     INLength = (INPtr -> IVerHdr)&017;
  154  
  155     SptOpt = 0;
  156  
  157     i = 0;
  158  
  159     while (i < ((INLength - 5)*4))
  160     {  switch(INPtr -> IOptions[i])
  161        {  case OPTSPT:
  162           {  InSec = (INPtr -> IOptions[i+2])&017;
  163              InPrec = ((INPtr -> IOptions[i+2]) >> 4)&017;
  164              InTcc = INPtr -> IOptions[i+3];
  165              SptOpt = 1;
  166              i = i + 4;
  167              break;
  168            }
  169           default:
  170           {  /* unknown option */
  171              if (INPtr -> IOption[i+1] == 0)
  172              {  /* will cause hang */
  173                 i = (INLength - 5)*4;  /* get out of loop */
  174               }
  175              else
  176              {  i = i + INPtr -> IOptions[i+1];
  177               }
  178            }
  179         }
  180      }
  181  
  182     if (Auth == 1)
  183     {  /* check if incoming segment prohibited by Stop */
  184  
  185        for (i = 0;i < NSTOP; i++)
  186        {  SktPtr = &StopSkt[i];
  187  
  188           if ((SktPtr -> XHost == INPtr -> ISHstH)&&
  189           (SktPtr -> XNet == INPtr -> ISrcNet)&&
  190           (SktPtr -> XImp == INPtr -> ISHstL))
  191           {  /* ignore the segment */
  192              printf("PsipIn: Stop %o %o %o\n", INPtr -> ISrcNet,
  193              INPtr -> ISHstH, INPtr -> ISHstL);
  194  
  195              return(2);    /* move to end of queue */
  196            }
  197         }
  198  
  199        if (SptOpt == 0)
  200        {  return(2);    /* ignore segment */
  201         }
  202  
  203        /* check TCC */
  204        for (i = 0; i < NSTOP; i++)
  205        {  if (StopTcc[i] < 0)
  206           {  if ((StopTcc[i]&017) == InTcc)
  207              {  printf("PsipIn: Stop T %d\n", InTcc);
  208                 /* ignore the segment */
  209                 return(2);
  210               }
  211            }
  212         }
  213      }
  214  
  215     /* find the destination TCB */
  216     /* first check for non-OPENed connections */
  217     Found = 0;
  218     XPtr = TcbHd;
  219  
  220     while((Found == 0)&&(XPtr != 0))
  221     {  /* match local socket and foreign socket */
  222  
  223        if ((XPtr -> State != OPEN)&&(XPtr -> TMode != 2))
  224        {  /* check for exact match */
  225           if ((XPtr -> SrcePort == TCPPtr -> TDestPort)&&
  226           (XPtr -> DestPort == TCPPtr -> TSrcePort)&&
  227           (XPtr -> DstNet == INPtr -> ISrcNet)&&
  228           (XPtr -> DstHstH == INPtr -> ISHstH)&&
  229           (XPtr -> DstHstL == INPtr -> ISHstL))
  230  
  231           {  /* found the TCB */
  232              Found = 1;
  233            }
  234         }
  235        if (Found == 0)
  236        {  XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
  237         }
  238      }
  239  
  240     if (Found == 0)
  241     {  /* check TCBs with unspecified local socket parameters */
  242        XPtr = TcbHd;
  243  
  244        while ((Found == 0)&&(XPtr != 0))
  245        {  /* match local socket and foreign socket taking into
  246           account possible unspecified foreign socket parameters */
  247  
  248           if ((XPtr -> State == OPEN)&&(XPtr -> TMode != 2))
  249           {
  250              if ((XPtr -> SrcePort == TCPPtr -> TDestPort)&&
  251              ((XPtr -> DestPort == TCPPtr -> TSrcePort)||(XPtr -> DestPort == 0))&&
  252              ((XPtr -> DstNet == INPtr -> ISrcNet)||(XPtr -> DstNet == 0))&&
  253              (((XPtr -> DstHstH == INPtr -> ISHstH)&&
  254              (XPtr -> DstHstL == INPtr -> ISHstL))||
  255              ((XPtr -> DstHstH == 0)&&(XPtr -> DstHstL == 0))))
  256  
  257              {  /* found the TCB */
  258                 Found = 1;
  259               }
  260            }
  261           if (Found == 0)
  262           {  XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
  263            }
  264         }
  265      }
  266  
  267     /* check SPT parameters */
  268  
  269     if ((Found == 1)&&(Auth == 1))
  270     {  /* check for internet SPT option */
  271        if (SptOpt == 0)
  272        {  /* no option in segment */
  273           Found = 0;
  274         }
  275        else
  276        {  /* ignore security if unspecified */
  277           if (XPtr -> ScMaxIn != -1)
  278           {  /* validate the security */
  279              if ((InSec > XPtr -> ScMaxIn)||
  280              (InSec < XPtr -> ScMinIn))
  281              {  /* bad security level */
  282                 Found = 0;
  283               }
  284            }
  285  
  286           /* validate the receive precedence */
  287           if ((InPrec > XPtr -> MxRcvPrec)||
  288           (InPrec < XPtr -> MnRcvPrec))
  289           {  /* bad precedence level */
  290              Found = 0;
  291            }
  292  
  293           /* validate the TCC */
  294           TccOk = 0;
  295           i = 0;
  296  
  297           while ((i < XPtr -> TCCCnt)&&(TccOk == 0))
  298           {  if (XPtr -> TccList[i] == InTcc)
  299              {  TccOk = 1;
  300               }
  301              else
  302              {  i++;
  303               }
  304            }
  305  
  306           if (TccOk == 0)
  307           {  Found = 0;
  308            }
  309  
  310         }
  311      }
  312  
  313     if ((Found == 0)||(XPtr -> State == CLOSED))
  314     {  /* no matching TCB */
  315        printf("nm %o %o %o %o\n", TCPPtr -> TSrcePort, INPtr -> ISHstH,
  316        INPtr -> ISHstL, INPtr -> ISrcNet);
  317  
  318        if (PartWr == 0)      /* forget RST if waiting for netout */
  319        {  /* if no RST, send ACK, RST */
  320           if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&RST) == 0)
  321           {  /* immediately send a RST segment */
  322  
  323              /* swap internet fields */
  324              INPtr -> ISegLength = (((INPtr -> IVerHdr)&017)*4) + TCPLNGTH;
  325              INPtr -> IINChkSum = 0;
  326              V = INPtr -> ISrcNet;
  327              INPtr -> ISrcNet = INPtr -> IDstNet;
  328              INPtr -> IDstNet = V;
  329              V = INPtr -> ISHstL;
  330              INPtr -> ISHstL = INPtr -> IDHstL;
  331              INPtr -> IDHstL = V;
  332              V = INPtr -> ISHstH;
  333              INPtr -> ISHstH = INPtr -> IDHstH;
  334              INPtr -> IDHstH = V;
  335  
  336              /* swap TCP fields */
  337              V = TCPPtr -> TSrcePort;
  338              TCPPtr -> TSrcePort = TCPPtr -> TDestPort;
  339              TCPPtr -> TDestPort = V;
  340  
  341              W = TCPPtr -> TAckNo;  
  342              TCPPtr -> TAckNo = TCPPtr -> TSeqNo + PktLength;
  343              TCPPtr -> TSeqNo = W;
  344  
  345              TCPPtr -> TTCPChkSum = 0;
  346  
  347              TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags = ACK + RST
  348              + ((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&0170000);
  349  
  350              /* copy segment to TcpOut */
  351  
  352              SPtr = TcpIn;
  353              DPtr = TcpOut;
  354  
  355              for (i = 0; i < BSLLNGTH + (INPtr -> ISegLength); i++)
  356              {  *DPtr++ = *SPtr++;
  357               }
  358  
  359              /* send segment to the net */
  360  
  361              NetOut(0);
  362  
  363            }
  364         }
  365        return(2);    /* move work entry */
  366      }
  367  
  368     /* reset "foreign host not resp" bit */
  369     XPtr -> Flags =& ~0400;
  370  
  371     XPtr -> SegRecv =+ 1;
  372  
  373     AckOk = 0;
  374  
  375     /* check for ACK */
  376  
  377     if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&ACK) > 0)
  378     {  /* found an ACK bit on */
  379        /* check for range of Ack */
  380  
  381        R1 = ULCompare(&(XPtr -> LeftSeq), &(TCPPtr -> TAckNo));
  382        R2 = ULCompare(&(TCPPtr -> TAckNo), &(XPtr -> MaxSend));
  383  
  384        if ((R1 == 2)&&(R2 != 1))
  385        /* LeftSeq < AckNo <= MaxSend */
  386        {  /* acceptable */
  387  
  388           AckOk = 1;
  389  
  390           if (R2 == 0)
  391           /* AckNo = MaxSend */
  392           {  UnQTcb(XPtr);
  393  
  394              if (XPtr -> State == CLOSING)
  395              {  /* nothing else to send */
  396                 XPtr -> State = CLOSED;
  397  
  398                 /* inform user */
  399  
  400                 CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
  401               }
  402            }
  403  
  404           XPtr -> DataHd =+ (TCPPtr -> TAckNo) - (XPtr -> LeftSeq);
  405  
  406           /* compensate for SYN, FIN sequence numbers */
  407  
  408           switch(XPtr -> State)
  409           {  case FINWAIT:
  410              case CLOSING:
  411              case CLOSED:
  412              {  R1 = ULCompare(&(TCPPtr -> TAckNo), &(XPtr -> SndFSN));
  413                 if (R1 == 1)       /* AckNo > SndFSN */
  414                 {  XPtr -> DataHd =- 1;
  415                  }
  416                 break;
  417               }
  418              case SYNSENT:
  419              case SYNRECD:
  420              {  if (XPtr -> LeftSeq == XPtr -> SndISN)
  421                 {  XPtr -> DataHd =- 1;
  422                  }
  423                 break;
  424               }
  425            }
  426  
  427           if (XPtr -> DataHd >= RBUFFSIZE)
  428           {  XPtr -> DataHd =- RBUFFSIZE;
  429            }
  430  
  431           XPtr -> LeftSeq = TCPPtr -> TAckNo;
  432  
  433           R1 = ULCompare(&(TCPPtr -> TAckNo), &(XPtr -> SendSeq));
  434  
  435           if (R1 == 1)
  436           /* AckNo > SendSeq */
  437           {  XPtr -> SendSeq = TCPPtr -> TAckNo;
  438  
  439              XPtr -> DataNxt = XPtr -> DataHd;
  440            }
  441  
  442           /* check if send urgent can be turned off */
  443           R1 = ULCompare(&(XPtr -> LeftSeq), &(XPtr -> SndUrgNo));
  444  
  445           if (R1 != 2)   /* LeftSeq >= SndUrgNo */
  446           {  /* turn off send urgent flag */
  447              XPtr -> Flags =& ~01;
  448            }
  449  
  450           /* schedule work for DataIn in case there is
  451           more data in the port */
  452  
  453           MakeWrk(0, USERINHP, XPtr, XPtr -> SendPort);
  454  
  455           if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&RST) == 0)
  456           {  /* schedule work for PsipOut in case segmentation
  457              is being held up because of window or buffer limits */
  458  
  459              MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  460  
  461              if (XPtr -> State == SYNRECD)
  462              {  /* go into established state */
  463  
  464                 XPtr -> State = ESTAB;
  465  
  466                 /* inform user */
  467  
  468                 CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, ESTAB, 0);
  469  
  470                 /* bind receive precedence */
  471                 XPtr -> MxRcvPrec = InPrec;
  472                 XPtr -> MnRcvPrec = InPrec;
  473  
  474               }
  475  
  476              XPtr -> NumRetran = 0;
  477  
  478              if (((XPtr -> Flags)&04) > 0)
  479              {  /* inform user foreign proc is back */
  480                 PPtr = CmdTcp;
  481                 PPtr -> YOpCode = OPROCR;
  482                 PPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  483                 write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
  484  
  485                 XPtr -> Flags =& ~04;    /* reset flag */
  486               }
  487            }
  488         }
  489  
  490        else
  491        {  /* ACK not acceptable */
  492  
  493           /* decide whether to return a RST */
  494  
  495           switch(XPtr -> State)
  496           {  case OPEN:
  497              case SYNSENT:
  498              case SYNRECD:
  499              case CLOSED:
  500              {  /* check for RST */
  501  
  502                 if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&RST) == 0)
  503                 {  /* return a RST */
  504  
  505                    XPtr -> SeqNo = TCPPtr -> TAckNo;
  506                    XPtr -> AckNo = TCPPtr -> TSeqNo + PktLength;
  507                    XPtr -> TCPFlags =| ACK + RST;
  508  
  509                    MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  510                  }
  511               }
  512            }
  513         }
  514      }
  515  
  516  
  517     /* check for RST */
  518     /* ACK ignored since I could lose a "courtesy RST" this way */
  519  
  520     if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&RST) > 0)
  521     {  /* RST bit is on */
  522  
  523        switch (XPtr -> State)
  524        {  case OPEN:
  525           case CLOSED:
  526           {  break;    /* ignore the RST */
  527            }
  528           case SYNSENT:
  529           {  /* foreign TCP rejecting */
  530              /* send only one message to user */
  531  
  532              if (((XPtr -> Flags)&0100) == 0)
  533              {  /* inform user */
  534                 RejectPtr = CmdTcp;
  535                 RejectPtr -> YOpCode = OREJECT;
  536                 RejectPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  537                 write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
  538  
  539                 XPtr -> Flags =| 0100;    /* set flag */
  540  
  541               }
  542              XPtr -> LeftSeq = XPtr -> SndISN;
  543              XPtr -> SendSeq = XPtr -> SndISN;
  544              XPtr -> MaxSend = XPtr -> SndISN;
  545  
  546              /* put TCB on retransmission queue */
  547              QTcb(XPtr);
  548              break;
  549            }
  550           case SYNRECD:
  551           {  XPtr -> State = OPEN;
  552  
  553              /* restore unspecified foreign socket parameters */
  554  
  555              XPtr -> DstNet = XPtr -> OpnNet;
  556              XPtr -> DstHstH = XPtr -> OpnHstH;
  557              XPtr -> DstHstL = XPtr -> OpnHstL;
  558              XPtr -> DestPort = XPtr -> OpnPort;
  559  
  560              XPtr -> LeftSeq = XPtr -> SndISN;
  561              XPtr -> SendSeq = XPtr -> SndISN;
  562              XPtr -> MaxSend = XPtr -> SndISN;
  563  
  564              XPtr -> DataHd = 0;
  565              XPtr -> DataTl = 0;
  566              XPtr -> DataNxt = 0;
  567  
  568              /* restore precedence, TCC parameters */
  569  
  570              break;
  571            }
  572           default:
  573           {  /* all other connection states */
  574              /* close the connection and inform user */
  575  
  576              XPtr -> State = CLOSED;
  577  
  578              XPtr -> Flags =| 040;    /* send no more segments */
  579  
  580              UnQTcb(XPtr);
  581  
  582              /* send "connection reset" message to user */
  583  
  584              ResetPtr = CmdTcp;
  585              ResetPtr -> YOpCode = ORESET;
  586              ResetPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  587              write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 4);
  588  
  589              /* send state change message */
  590  
  591              CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
  592  
  593              break;
  594            }
  595         }
  596        return(2);    /* move work block */
  597      }
  598  
  599     /* update the send window size */
  600     XPtr -> SndWindow = TCPPtr -> TWindow;
  601  
  602     if (XPtr -> SndWindow > 0)
  603     {  /* reset flag which ensures that ACKs into
  604        a zero window do not send data unless retransmitting */
  605  
  606        XPtr -> Flags =& ~XRTNDATA;
  607      }
  608  
  609     switch (XPtr -> State)
  610     {  case OPEN:
  611        {
  612           if (SynOn > 0)
  613           {  /* segment contains SYN bit */
  614              /* fill in unspecified foreign socket parameters */
  615              XPtr -> DestPort = TCPPtr -> TSrcePort;
  616              XPtr -> DstNet = INPtr -> ISrcNet;
  617              XPtr -> DstHstH = INPtr -> ISHstH;
  618              XPtr -> DstHstL = INPtr -> ISHstL;
  619  
  620              if (Auth == 1)
  621              {  /* check if send precedence level is high enough */
  622                 if (InPrec > XPtr -> SndPrec)
  623                 {  /* check if allowable to boost send precedence */
  624                    if (InPrec <= XPtr -> ATPrec)
  625                    {  /* boost to match incoming */
  626                       XPtr -> SndPrec = InPrec;
  627  
  628                       /* inform user of send precedence change */
  629  
  630                       PPtr = CmdTcp;
  631                       PPtr -> YOpCode = OPRECCHNG;
  632                       PPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  633                       PPtr -> YMisc = XPtr -> SndPrec;
  634  
  635                       write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 6);
  636                     }
  637                    else
  638                    {  /* check if can boost to highest possible */
  639                       if (XPtr -> SndPrec < XPtr -> ATPrec)
  640                       {  /* boost up to auth table value */
  641                          XPtr -> SndPrec = XPtr -> ATPrec;
  642  
  643                          /* inform user of send precedence change */
  644  
  645                          PPtr = CmdTcp;
  646                          PPtr -> YOpCode = OPRECCHNG;
  647                          PPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  648                          PPtr -> YMisc = XPtr -> SndPrec;
  649  
  650                          write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 6);
  651                        }
  652                     }
  653                  }
  654                 /* bind the security for unspecified connection */
  655                 if (XPtr -> ScMaxOut == -1)
  656                 {  XPtr -> ScMaxOut = InSec;
  657                    XPtr -> ScMinOut = InSec;
  658                  }
  659  
  660                 /* bind the TCC */
  661                 XPtr -> TCCCnt = 1;
  662                 XPtr -> TccList[0] = InTcc;
  663               }
  664  
  665              /* begin 3-way handshake */
  666              /* assumes no data with SYN */
  667  
  668              XPtr -> TCPFlags =| ACK;  /* set ACK bit */
  669              XPtr -> AckNo = (TCPPtr -> TSeqNo) +1;
  670              XPtr -> RcvISN = TCPPtr -> TSeqNo;
  671  
  672              /* create some work for PsipOut */
  673              MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  674  
  675              /* go into SYN-RECD state */
  676              XPtr -> State = SYNRECD;
  677  
  678              /* notify user in case he is a server
  679              and needs to fork */
  680  
  681              CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, SYNRECD, 0);
  682            }
  683           return(2);    /* move work entry */
  684         }
  685        case SYNRECD:
  686        case CLOSED:      /* since ACK can cause CLOSED state */
  687        {
  688           return(2);    /* move work entry */
  689         }
  690        case SYNSENT:
  691        {
  692           if (SynOn > 0)
  693           {  /* found SYN = 1 */
  694              XPtr -> AckNo = (TCPPtr -> TSeqNo) + 1;
  695              XPtr -> TCPFlags =| ACK;   /* set ACK = 1 */
  696  
  697              if (Auth == 1)
  698              {  /* check if send precedence level is high enough */
  699                 if (InPrec > XPtr -> SndPrec)
  700                 {  /* check if allowable to boost send precedence */
  701                    if (InPrec <= XPtr -> ATPrec)
  702                    {  /* boost to match incoming */
  703                       XPtr -> SndPrec = InPrec;
  704  
  705                       /* inform user of send precedence change */
  706  
  707                       PPtr = CmdTcp;
  708                       PPtr -> YOpCode = OPRECCHNG;
  709                       PPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  710                       PPtr -> YMisc = XPtr -> SndPrec;
  711  
  712                       write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 6);
  713                     }
  714                    else
  715                    {  /* check if can boost to highest possible */
  716                       if (XPtr -> SndPrec < XPtr -> ATPrec)
  717                       {  /* boost up to auth table value */
  718                          XPtr -> SndPrec = XPtr -> ATPrec;
  719  
  720                          /* inform user of send precedence change */
  721  
  722                          PPtr = CmdTcp;
  723                          PPtr -> YOpCode = OPRECCHNG;
  724                          PPtr -> YHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  725                          PPtr -> YMisc = XPtr -> SndPrec;
  726  
  727                          write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 6);
  728                        }
  729                     }
  730                  }
  731               }
  732  
  733              R1 = ULCompare(&(XPtr -> LeftSeq),&(XPtr -> SndISN));
  734  
  735              /* check for ACK */
  736              if ((AckOk == 1)||(R1 == 1))
  737              /* R1 = 1 => LeftSeq > SndISN */
  738              /* implies SYN already acked */
  739              {  XPtr -> State = ESTAB;
  740  
  741                 /* send change notice to user */
  742  
  743                 CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, ESTAB, 0);
  744  
  745                 /* bind receive precedence */
  746                 XPtr -> MxRcvPrec = InPrec;
  747                 XPtr -> MnRcvPrec = InPrec;
  748  
  749               }
  750              else
  751              {  XPtr -> State = SYNRECD;
  752               }
  753  
  754              /* send ACK segment */
  755              /* make some work for PsipOut */
  756  
  757              MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  758  
  759            }
  760           return(2);    /* move work entry */
  761         }
  762  
  763  
  764  
  765        case ESTAB:
  766        case FINWAIT:
  767        case CLOSEWAIT:
  768        case CLOSING:
  769  
  770        {
  771           /* check for urgent flag */
  772           if ((XPtr -> State == ESTAB)||(XPtr -> State == FINWAIT))
  773           {  if (((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&URG) > 0)
  774              {  /* URG = 1 */
  775                 /* calculate actual sequence number */
  776                 /* the following craziness reqd because
  777                 of sign extension */
  778  
  779                 z = &UrgNo;
  780                 z -> bb[0] = 0;
  781                 z -> bb[1] = TCPPtr -> TUrgentPtr;
  782  
  783                 UrgNo =+ TCPPtr -> TSeqNo;
  784  
  785                 /* check to see if user should be notified */
  786                 /* fix this: segments may arrive out of order */
  787  
  788                 if ((((XPtr -> Flags)&02) == 0)||(XPtr -> RcvUrgNo != UrgNo))
  789                 {  /* send urgent message to user */
  790                    XPtr -> RcvUrgNo = UrgNo;
  791                    XPtr -> Flags =| 02;
  792  
  793                    UrgPtr = CmdTcp;
  794                    UrgPtr -> UOpCode = OURGENT;
  795                    UrgPtr -> UHandle = XPtr -> ConnHandle;
  796                    UrgPtr -> UByteNo = UrgNo - (XPtr -> RcvISN) - 1 /* for SYN */;
  797                    write(XPtr -> TCPCmdPort, CmdTcp, 8);
  798                  }
  799               }
  800            }
  801  
  802           if (PktLength > 0)
  803           {  /* data arrived (or data plus SYN or FIN) */
  804  
  805              /* get capacity of user receive port */
  806  
  807              capac(XPtr -> RecvPort, Cap, 4);
  808  
  809              /* calculate new receive window */
  810              XPtr -> Window = ((Cap[1]*WindNum)/WindDenom) - HDRLNGTH;
  811  
  812              if (XPtr -> Window > RWindow)
  813              {  /* reset to max size allowed */
  814                 XPtr -> Window = RWindow;
  815               }
  816  
  817              if (XPtr -> Window < 0)
  818              {  XPtr -> Window = 0;
  819               }
  820  
  821              /* check if next in sequence */
  822              /* check PKT-SEQUENCE */
  823              temp1 = (TCPPtr -> TSeqNo) + (PktLength-1);
  824              temp2 = (XPtr -> AckNo) + (XPtr -> Window);
  825  
  826              R1 = ULCompare(&(XPtr -> AckNo), &temp1);
  827              R2 = ULCompare(&temp1, &temp2);
  828  
  829              if ((R1 != 1)&&(R2 == 2))
  830              /* AckNo <= SeqNo + PktLength-1 < AckNo + Window */
  831  
  832              {  /* acceptable segment */
  833                 R1 = ULCompare(&(TCPPtr -> TSeqNo), &(XPtr -> AckNo));
  834  
  835                 if (R1 == 1)    /* SeqNo > AckNo */
  836  
  837                 {  /* not in order */
  838                    /* for now discard segment! */
  839                    XPtr -> BusyRecv =+ 1;
  840                    printf("out of order\n");
  841                  }
  842                 else
  843                 {
  844                    /* make ptr to first data octet */
  845  
  846                    OctetPtr = &TcpIn[(((INPtr -> IVerHdr)&017)*4) + BSLLNGTH +
  847                    ((((TCPPtr -> TTCPFlags)&0170000) >> 12)*4)];
  848  
  849                    /* determine number of next in sequence data bytes */
  850  
  851  		  /* the SynOn compensation is there to fix a bug
  852  		  which caused a loss of the first byte when a
  853  		  retransmission of SYN + Data was received in the
  854  		  ESTAB state.  */
  855  
  856  		  if (SynOn == 1)
  857  		  { printf("PsipIn: ESTAB and SYN + %d bytes\n", DataAmt);
  858  		   }
  859  
  860  		  BCnt = DataAmt + SynOn
  861  		  - ((XPtr -> AckNo) - (TCPPtr -> TSeqNo));
  862  
  863                    if (Cap[1] < HDRLNGTH + BCnt)
  864                    {  /* no space in receive port */
  865                       /* for now, discard segment */
  866                       printf("no recv space\n");
  867                     }
  868                    else
  869                    {  /* move ptr to next octet in sequence */
  870                       OctetPtr =+ DataAmt - BCnt;
  871  
  872                       if (BCnt != 0)
  873                       {  write(XPtr -> RecvPort, OctetPtr, BCnt);
  874  
  875                          /* update window */
  876                          XPtr -> Window =- BCnt + HDRLNGTH;
  877                          if (XPtr -> Window < 0)
  878                          {  XPtr -> Window = 0;
  879                           }
  880                        }
  881  
  882                       XPtr -> AckNo = (TCPPtr -> TSeqNo) + PktLength;
  883  
  884                       /* data sent to user, check for FIN */
  885  
  886                       if (FinOn > 0)
  887                       {  /* FIN bit is on */
  888  
  889                          /* close port to indicate EOF */
  890                          /* close(XPtr -> RecvPort); */
  891  
  892                          if (XPtr -> State == ESTAB)
  893                          {  XPtr -> State = CLOSEWAIT;
  894  
  895                             /* notify user of receipt of FIN */
  896  
  897                             CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSEWAIT, 0);
  898                           }
  899  
  900                          else if (XPtr -> State == FINWAIT)
  901                          {  /* check if all data and controls
  902                             have been acked */
  903  
  904                             if (XPtr -> LeftSeq == XPtr -> MaxSend)
  905                             {  /* everything acknowledged */
  906                                XPtr -> State = CLOSED;
  907  
  908                                /* send user connection state change */
  909  
  910                                CmdOut(XPtr, OCHNG, CLOSED, 0);
  911                              }
  912                             else
  913                             {  XPtr -> State = CLOSING;
  914                              }
  915                           }
  916                        }
  917                     }
  918                  }
  919               }
  920              /* send back an ACK */
  921              XPtr -> TCPFlags =| ACK;    /* set the ACK bit */
  922  
  923              /* make some work for PsipOut */
  924  
  925              MakeWrk(0, NETOUTHP, XPtr, NetFdsW);
  926  
  927            }
  928           return(2);    /* move work entry */
  929         }
  930  
  931        default:
  932        {
  933           printf("PsipIn: bad TCB state: %d\n", XPtr -> State);
  934           return(0);
  935         }
  936  
  937      }
  938   }
  939  
  940  
  941  /* Subroutine for handling traffic from the net */
  942  
  943  NetIn()
  944  
  945  {
  946     extern int ANLngth;
  947     extern Debug;
  948     extern int NetFdsR;
  949     extern char TcpOut[TCPOTSZ];
  950     extern char TcpIn[TCPINSZ];
  951     extern int DoChkSum;
  952     extern struct TcpTcb *TcbHd;
  953     extern int LocNet;
  954     extern int LocHost;
  955     extern int LocImp;
  956     extern int PartWr;
  957  
  958     register char *SPtr, *DPtr;
  959  
  960     int BCnt;
  961     struct ANMask1 *ANPtr1;
  962     struct ANMask2 *ANPtr2;
  963     struct BSLMask *BSLPtr;
  964     struct INLMask *INPtr;
  965     struct TCPMask *TCPPtr;
  966     struct TcpTcb *XPtr;
  967     int ILength;
  968     int DLength;
  969     int TLength;
  970     char *BPtr;
  971     int Chk;
  972     int Error;
  973     int PHdr[2];
  974     int BHost;
  975     int BImp;
  976     int MType;
  977     int i;
  978     int SType;
  979  
  980     BCnt = read(NetFdsR, &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH - ANLngth*2 - 2], MAXMSG);
  981  
  982     if (BCnt == -1)
  983     {  /* read error */
  984        printf("read error\n");
  985        return(0);
  986      }
  987  
  988     if (BCnt == 0)
  989     {  return(-1);
  990      }
  991     else
  992     {  /* check for message type */
  993        ANPtr1 = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH - ANLngth*2 - 2];
  994        ANPtr2 = ANPtr1;
  995  
  996        if (ANLngth == 2)
  997        {  MType = ANPtr1 -> Type1;
  998           SType = ANPtr1 -> SbType1;
  999         }
 1000        else
 1001        {  MType = ANPtr2 -> Type2;
 1002           SType = ANPtr2 -> SbType2;
 1003         }
 1004  
 1005        switch(MType)
 1006        {  case RFNM:
 1007           {  /* RFNM arrived */
 1008              return(0);
 1009            }
 1010  
 1011           case REGULAR:
 1012           {  /* a regular message arrived */
 1013              BSLPtr = TcpIn;
 1014  
 1015              INPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH];
 1016  
 1017              /* remember, the bytes are arriving swapped */
 1018              ILength = ((INPtr -> ITypeService)&017)*4;
 1019  
 1020              if (ILength < 20)
 1021              {  printf("PsipIn: IN ldr too short\n");
 1022                 return(0);
 1023               }
 1024  
 1025              /* swap the internet bytes */
 1026              Swap(INPtr, ILength);
 1027  
 1028              /* check if this is version 4 internet leader */
 1029  
 1030              if ((((INPtr -> IVerHdr) >> 4)&017)  != INVERNO)
 1031              {  /* printf("PsipIn: bad IN version\n"); */
 1032                 return(0);
 1033               }
 1034  
 1035              if (DoChkSum == 1)
 1036              {  /* check the internet checksum */
 1037  
 1038                 if (ChkSum(INPtr, ILength/2, 0, 0) != -1)
 1039                 {  /* bad internet checksum -
 1040                    drop incoming segment on the floor */
 1041  
 1042                    printf("bad IN checksum\n");
 1043                    return(0);
 1044                  }
 1045               }
 1046  
 1047              /* check if this host is the destination */
 1048  
 1049              if ((INPtr -> IDstNet != LocNet)||
 1050              (INPtr -> IDHstH != LocHost)||
 1051  	    (INPtr -> IDHstL != LocImp))
 1052              {  /* segment is destined for some other host */
 1053  
 1054                 if (PartWr == 1)
 1055                 {  /* discard it */
 1056                    return(0);
 1057                  }
 1058  
 1059                 /* copy to TcpOut */
 1060  
 1061                 DPtr = &TcpOut[BSLLNGTH];
 1062                 SPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH];
 1063                 BCnt = INPtr -> ISegLength;
 1064  
 1065                 for (i = 0; i < BCnt; i++)
 1066                 {  *DPtr++ = *SPtr++;
 1067                  }
 1068  
 1069                 NetOut(1);  /* send back out to net */
 1070  
 1071                 return(0);
 1072               }
 1073  
 1074              /* check for TCP protocol */
 1075  
 1076              if (INPtr -> IProtocol != TCPVERNO)
 1077              {  printf("Psipin: bad TCP version\n");
 1078                 return(0);
 1079               }
 1080  
 1081              /* get the length of the tcp header */
 1082              /* the 12th byte is the TCP offset byte */
 1083  
 1084              TCPPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH + ILength];
 1085  
 1086              TLength = ((TcpIn[BSLLNGTH + ILength + 12]) >> 4)*4;
 1087  
 1088              if (TLength < TCPLNGTH)
 1089              {  printf("PsipIn: TCP ldr too short\n");
 1090                 return(0);
 1091               }
 1092  
 1093              DLength = INPtr -> ISegLength - ILength - TLength;
 1094  
 1095              /* swap the TCP header bytes */
 1096  
 1097              Swap(TCPPtr, TLength);
 1098  
 1099              if ((Debug == 1)||(Debug == 3)||(Debug == 4))
 1100              {
 1101                 Error = Log(2, "n", INPtr, ENDARGS);
 1102                 Error = Log(2, "t", TCPPtr, ENDARGS);
 1103               }
 1104  
 1105              if (DoChkSum == 1)
 1106              {  /* check the TCP checksum */
 1107  
 1108                 BPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH + 12];
 1109  
 1110                 Chk = ChkSum(BPtr, 4, 0, 0);
 1111                 Chk = ChkSum(TCPPtr, TLength/2, Chk, 0);
 1112  
 1113                 /* don't forget the protocol and length additions */
 1114                 BPtr = &PHdr[0];
 1115                 PHdr[0] = (INPtr -> ISegLength) - ILength;
 1116                 PHdr[1] = INPtr -> IProtocol;
 1117  
 1118                 Chk = ChkSum(BPtr, 2, Chk, 0);
 1119  
 1120                 BPtr = &TcpIn[BSLLNGTH + ILength + TLength];
 1121  
 1122                 /* check for odd byte count */
 1123                 if ((DLength&01) > 0)
 1124                 {
 1125                    /* need to pad with zero byte */
 1126                    TcpIn[BSLLNGTH + ILength + TLength + DLength] = 0;
 1127                    DLength =+ 1;
 1128                  }
 1129                 Chk = ChkSum(BPtr, DLength/2, Chk, 1 /* swap */);
 1130  
 1131                 if (Chk != -1)
 1132                 {  /* TCP checksum error -
 1133                    drop segment on the floor */
 1134                    printf("bad TCP checksum\n");
 1135                    return(0);
 1136                  }
 1137               }
 1138              return(1);
 1139            }
 1140  
 1141           case NOP:
 1142           {  /* NOP message arrived */
 1143              return(0);
 1144            }
 1145  
 1146           case NOHOST:
 1147           {  /* host not responding */
 1148  
 1149              if (ANLngth == 2)
 1150              {  BHost = (ANPtr1 -> Host1) >> 6;
 1151                 BImp = (ANPtr1 -> Host1)&077;
 1152               }
 1153              else
 1154              {  BHost = ANPtr2 -> Host2;
 1155                 BImp = ANPtr2 -> Imp2;
 1156               }
 1157  
 1158              /* inform all users talking to this host */
 1159              /* search TCBs for this foreign host */
 1160  
 1161              XPtr = TcbHd;
 1162  
 1163              while(XPtr != 0)
 1164              {  if ((XPtr -> DstHstH == BHost)&&
 1165                 (XPtr -> DstHstL == BImp)&&
 1166                 (XPtr -> DstNet == LocNet))
 1167                 {  /* found one */
 1168                    /* inform user only once */
 1169                    if (((XPtr -> Flags)&0400) == 0)
 1170                    {  CmdOut(XPtr, ONETMSG, 1 /* host not resp */, 0);
 1171                       XPtr -> Flags =| 0400;  /* set flag */
 1172                     }
 1173                  }
 1174                 XPtr = XPtr -> NxtActTcb;
 1175               }
 1176  
 1177              return(0);
 1178            }
 1179  
 1180           default:
 1181           {  printf("Type %d message, Subtype %d\n", MType, SType);
 1182            }
 1183  
 1184         }
 1185      }
 1186   }
 1187  
 1188  
 1189  
 1190  /* Subroutine for swapping bytes to get them out in
 1191  the right order */
 1192  
 1193  Swap(P, BCnt)
 1194  
 1195  char *P;
 1196  int BCnt;
 1197  
 1198  {
 1199     struct aa
 1200     {  char Byte[MAXMSG];
 1201      };
 1202  
 1203     register struct aa *Ptr;
 1204     register int i;
 1205     register char T;
 1206  
 1207     Ptr = P;
 1208  
 1209     for (i = 0; i < BCnt;)
 1210     {
 1211        T = Ptr -> Byte[i];
 1212        Ptr -> Byte[i] = Ptr -> Byte[i+1];
 1213        Ptr -> Byte[i+1] = T;
 1214        i = i + 2;
 1215      }
 1216   }
 1217  
 1218  
 1219  
 1220  /* Subroutine which calculates the checksum */
 1221  
 1222  ChkSum(Ptr, WCnt, OldChk, SwapFlag)
 1223  
 1224  int *Ptr;
 1225  int WCnt;
 1226  int OldChk;
 1227  int SwapFlag;
 1228  
 1229  {
 1230     int i;
 1231     int CheckSum;
 1232     int C;
 1233     char Temp;
 1234     int NxtWd;
 1235  
 1236     struct
 1237     {  char Byte[2];
 1238      } *BPtr;
 1239  
 1240  
 1241     BPtr = &NxtWd;
 1242  
 1243     CheckSum = OldChk;
 1244  
 1245     for (i = 0; i < WCnt; i++)
 1246     {  NxtWd = *Ptr;
 1247  
 1248        if (SwapFlag == 1)
 1249        {  /* the bytes need to be swapped first */
 1250           Temp = BPtr -> Byte[0];
 1251           BPtr -> Byte[0] = BPtr -> Byte[1];
 1252           BPtr -> Byte[1] = Temp;
 1253         }
 1254  
 1255        /* determine the carry bit */
 1256        C = 0;
 1257  
 1258        if ((CheckSum < 0)&&(NxtWd < 0))
 1259        {  C = 1;
 1260         }
 1261        else if (((CheckSum^(NxtWd)) < 0)&&((CheckSum + NxtWd) >= 0))
 1262        {  C = 1;
 1263         }
 1264  
 1265        CheckSum =+ (NxtWd) + C;
 1266        Ptr++;
 1267      }
 1268     return(CheckSum);
 1269   }
