 2128  Appendix B -- C Structure Definitions
 2129  
 2130  
 2131       The  current  structure definitions which may be accessed by
 2132  
 2133  user  programs  are  summarized  below.   These  definitions  are
 2134  
 2135  available in on-line files as well.
 2136  
 2137  
 2138  struct  cab {
 2139       char    c_lph ;   /* local port, high byte */
 2140       char    c_xx1 ;   /* used internally */
 2141       int     c_lpm ;   /* local port, low 2 bytes */
 2142       char    c_fnid ;  /* destination net */
 2143       char    c_ftidh ; /* foreign TCP, high byte */
 2144       int     c_ftid ;  /* destination host (low 2 bytes) */
 2145       char    c_fph ;   /* destination port, high byte */
 2146       char    c_xx2 ;   /* used internally */
 2147       int     c_fpm ;   /* destination port (low 2 bytes) */
 2148       int     c_cnmask  /* change-notice mask */
 2149  };
 2150  
 2151  
 2152  
 2153  struct cstatus {
 2154          struct cab c_cab;   /* for picking up specified
 2155                                 params */
 2156          int c_sndspace ;    /* bytes to write before sending
 2157                                 to the TCP */
 2158          int c_sndblock ;    /* bytes to write before blocking
 2159                                 may hapen */
 2160          int c_rcvspace ;    /* bytes to read before reading
 2161                                 from TCP */
 2162          int c_rcvblock ;    /* bytes available (not text)
 2163                                 before blocking */
 2164          int c_sndsct ;      /* various relevant params from
 2165                                 statistics vars */
 2166          int c_nr_pkt ;      /* # packets rcvd */
 2167          int c_pr_dup ;      /* # duplicates rcvd */
 2168          int c-pr_bsy ;      /* # discarded packets */
 2169          int c_pr_dmg ;      /* # damaged packets */
 2170          int c_nr_txt ;      /* # text packets rcvd */
 2171          int c_ns_rtx ;      /* # packets retransmitted */
 2172          int c_sndfds ;      /* # fds of send */
 2173          int c_rcvfds ;      /* # fds of rcv */
 2174  };
 2175  
 2176  
 2177  
 2178  
 2179  
 2180                                -35-
 2181

 2250  
 2251  
 2252  Appendix D -- Change-Notices
 2253  
 2254  
 2255       Change-notices are presented to the user by the  TCP_receive
 2256  
 2257  routine.   A  change-notice  is  simply  a  word of data in which
 2258  
 2259  events are bit-encoded. The values currently assigned to bits are
 2260  
 2261  enumerated below. Note  that  a  single  change-notice  may  have
 2262  
 2263  several  bits  set, since several events may occur before the TCP
 2264  
 2265  process has an opportunity to transmit  the  change-notice.   The
 2266  
 2267  symbols  beginning  with  the  sequence  "chg_"  are included for
 2268  
 2269  reference.  They are the mnemonics used internally in the TCP.
 2270  
 2271  
 2272       chg_rc  01      ;remote close received
 2273       chg_cc  02      ;close completed
 2274       chg_es  04      ;connection established
 2275       chg_er  010     ;error (reset rcvd in open)
 2276       chg_rf  020     ;connection refused
 2277       chg_dd  040     ;foreign tcp not responding (connection
 2278                       ; still open)
 2279       chg.io  0100    ;bug! (i/o error)
 2280       chg_eo  0200    ;eol has been acked by foreign site
 2281       chg_nu  0400    ;net up again
 2282       chg_nd  01000   ;net went down
 2283       chg_bs  02000   ;data was discarded (user busy)
 2284  
 2285  
 2286       In the case of chg_eo, several eols may have been  acked  in
 2287  
 2288  the  intervals  between  change-notices.   If the actual count of
 2289  
 2290  eols is important, the TCP_status routine may be used  to  obtain
 2291  
 2292  current values of c_sndsct.
 2293  
 2294  
 2295       The chg_bs notice will occur when the TCP process was forced
 2296  
 2297  to  discard  one  or  more arriving packets because the preceding
 2298  
 2299  packets had not yet been processed.  The TCP protocol is designed
 2300  
 2301  
 2302  
 2303  
 2304                                -37-
 2305  
 2306  
 2307  
 2308  
 2309  
 2310  
 2311  Report No. 3724                      Bolt Beranek and Newman Inc.
 2312  
 2313  
 2314  to operate in an environment where packets may be lost,  so  such
 2315  
 2316  an  event  is not a fatal error.  A chg_bs notice indictates that
 2317  
 2318  either the user process did not read data fast enough, or the TCP
 2319  
 2320  could not process incoming packets quickly enough for the  system
 2321  
 2322  as a whole.
 2323  
 2324  
 2325       The  set of events which cause change-notices to be sent may
 2326  
 2327  be selected by the user process by supplying a mask  in  the  cab
 2328  
 2329  structure  given  to TCP_open.  Events which are not given in the
 2330  
 2331  mask will still appear in the  change-notices,  with  other  bits
 2332  
 2333  set,  but their occurrence will not cause any change-notice to be
 2334  
 2335  sent.
 2336  
 2337  
 2338  
 2339  
 2340  
 2341  
 2342  
 2343  
 2344  
 2345  
 2346  
 2347  
 2348  
 2349  
 2350  
 2351  
 2352  
 2353  
 2354  
 2355  
 2356  
 2357  
 2358  
 2359  
 2360  
 2361  
 2362  
 2363  
 2364  
 2365  
 2366                                -38-
 2367  
 2368  
 2369  
 2370  
 2371  
 2372  
 2373  Report No. 3724                      Bolt Beranek and Newman Inc.
 2374  
 2375  
 2376  Appendix E -- Implementation Costs
 2377  
 2378  
 2379       To  assist  in  planning  of  user  applications   programs,
 2380  
 2381  information about the TCP's usage of system resources is helpful.
 2382  
 2383  The  primary  resource  used  is file descriptors for the various
 2384  
 2385  ports between user processes and the TCP.  This impacts  both  on
 2386  
 2387  the  TCP  as  it  relates  to  the system, as well as on the user
 2388  
 2389  process.
 2390  
 2391  
 2392       Each user process known to the TCP uses two file descriptors
 2393  
 2394  for communication of commands and responses.  In  addition,  each
 2395  
 2396  connection  uses  two  additional  ports,  for  full-duplex  data
 2397  
 2398  transfer.  The number of files which any process  may  have  open
 2399  
 2400  simultaneously  is  a  parameter in UNIX set at system generation
 2401  
 2402  time.  This  parameter  must  be  large  enough  to  support  the
 2403  
 2404  intended  configuration  of  user  processes  and connections, to
 2405  
 2406  supply  the  TCP  process  with  an  adequate  number   of   file
 2407  
 2408  descriptors.
 2409  
 2410  
 2411       Similarly, the user process must have a sufficient supply of
 2412  
 2413  file   descriptors   to   support  the  intended  number  of  TCP
 2414  
 2415  connections, as well as any other I/O activity required.
 2416  
 2417  
 2418       For some applications involving many  TCP  connections,  the
 2419  
 2420  availability of file descriptors may be difficult to provide.  In
 2421  
 2422  such  a case, the TCP could be extended to permit multiplexing of
 2423  
 2424  data from several connections on a single pair of ports.
 2425  
 2426  
 2427  
 2428                                -39-
 2429  
 2430  
 2431  
 2432  
 2433  
 2434  
 2435  Report No. 3724                      Bolt Beranek and Newman Inc.
 2436  
 2437  
 2438       The  TCP  process  itself  is  limited  in   memory   space.
 2439  
 2440  Currently, each active connection is allocated two buffers within
 2441  
 2442  the  TCP  process  for  reassembly and retransmission.  The total
 2443  
 2444  number of active connections is thus limited by the address space
 2445  
 2446  available to  the  process.   If  necessry,  this  limit  can  be
 2447  
 2448  increased  by  changing  of  the  TCP to utilize a dynamic buffer
 2449  
 2450  pool.  These routines will supply two pieces of information.  The
 2451  
 2452  amount of space remaining in the internal  buffer  specifies  how
 2453  
 2454  much  data  may be sent before a system call will be needed.  The
 2455  
 2456  amount of space available in the system  port  specifies  if  the
 2457  
 2458  write call will suspend the user process.
 2459  
 2460  
 2461  
 2462  
 2463  
 2464  
 2465  
 2466  
 2467  
 2468  
 2469  
 2470  
 2471  
 2472  
 2473  
 2474  
 2475  
 2476  
 2477  
 2478  
 2479  
 2480  
 2481  
 2482  
 2483  
 2484  
 2485  
 2486  
 2487  
 2488  
 2489  
 2490                                -40-

   14  					blank pages on line printer listings.

   16  u1.3.dz         12 Dec 79       fram    Commented out all references to modem
   17  				        control bits in dz.c.  This was a  
   18  					quick-and-dirty fix to make dz driver
   19  					work with current-loop lines, for which
   20  					modem status is undefined.  Does not
   21  					hurt in EIA case either.  (Essentially,
   22  					all our lines are "hard-wired", so 
   23  					modem control is really unnecessary.)

   29  					line printer prints a max of 76 cols.

   50  					gtty to set the 0x10000 bit in the
   51  					flag word. This bit was unused by

BBN TCP/IP 3.0 beta, November 24, 1981 / include/con.h

    1  struct con {                    /* user connection structure */
    2  	unsigned short c_mode;		/* mode (see below) */
    3  	unsigned char c_sbufs;		/* # send buffers to use */
    4  	unsigned char c_rbufs;		/* # rcv buffers to use */
    5  	unsigned char c_lo;		/* low raw link or proto # */
    6  	unsigned char c_hi;		/* hi raw link or proto # */
    7  	unsigned char c_timeo;		/* tcp open timeout */
    8  	unsigned char c_x;		/* (unused) */
    9  	unsigned short c_lport;         /* local port */
   10  	unsigned short c_fport;         /* foreign port */
   11  	netaddr c_fcon;			/* foreign net address */
   12  	netaddr c_lcon;			/* local net address */
   13  };
   14  
   15  struct netstate {               /* network status structure */
   16  	unsigned char n_lo;		/* low link no. in range (IP, RAW) */
   17  	unsigned char n_hi;		/* high link no. in range (IP, RAW) */
   18  	unsigned char n_snd;		/* # send bufs allocated */
   19  	unsigned char n_rcv;		/* # receive bufs allocated */
   20  	unsigned char n_ssize;		/* # bufs on send buffer */
   21  	unsigned char n_rsize;		/* # bufs on receive buffer */
   22  	unsigned short n_state;		/* state of this connection */
   23  	unsigned short n_flags;		/* misc. flags (see below) */
   24  	unsigned short n_lport;         /* local port */
   25  	unsigned short n_fport;         /* foreign port */
   26  	netaddr n_fcon; 		/* foreign socket */
   27  	netaddr n_lcon; 		/* local socket */
   28  };
   29  
   30  				/* c_mode field definitions */
   31  #define CONACT		0000001		/* active connection */
   32  #define CONTCP  	0000002		/* open a tcp connection */
   33  #define CONIP   	0000004		/* open a raw ip connection */
   34  #define CONRAW  	0000010		/* open a raw local net connection */
   35  #define CONCTL  	0000020		/* open a control port (no conn) */
   36  #define CONDEBUG 	0000200		/* turn on debugging info */
   37  #define CONRAWCOMP	0001000		/* system supplies raw leaders */
   38  #define CONRAWVER	0002000		/* system supplies cksum only */
   39  #define CONRAWASIS	0004000		/* user supplies raw leaders */
   40  #define CONRAWERR	0010000		/* user wants raw ICMP error msgs */
   41  
   42  				/* net ioctl definitions */
   43  #define NETGETS 1                       /* get status */
   44  #define NETSETD 2                       /* set debugging info */
   45  #define NETSETU 3                       /* set urgent mode */
   46  #define NETRSETU 4                      /* reset urgent mode */
   47  #define NETSETE 5                       /* set EOL mode */
   48  #define NETRSETE 6                      /* reset EOL mode */
   49  #define NETCLOSE 7                      /* initiate tcp close */
   50  #define NETABORT 8                      /* initiate tcp abort */
   51  #define NETRESET 9                      /* forced tcp connection reset */
   52  #define NETDEBUG 10                     /* toggle debugging flag */
   53  #define NETGINIT 11			/* re-read gateway file */
   54  #define NETTCPOPT 12			/* set tcp option string */
   55  
   56  #define SIGURG 16               /* urgent signal */
   57  
   58  				/* n_flags field definitions */
   59  #define UEOL    0000001			/* EOL sent */
   60  #define UURG    0000002			/* urgent data sent */
   61  #define UDEBUG  0000004			/* turn on debugging info recording */
   62  #define ULOCK   0000010			/* receive buffer locked */
   63  #define UTCP    0000020			/* this is a TCP connection */
   64  #define UIP     0000040			/* this is a raw IP connection */
   65  #define URAW    0000100			/* this is a raw 1822 connection */
   66  #define ULISTEN 0000200			/* awaiting a connection */
   67  #define UCTL    0000400			/* this is a control port only */
   68  #define URMSK	0000560			/* mask for conn mode */
   69  #define RAWCOMP	0001000			/* system supplies raw headers */
   70  #define RAWVER	0002000			/* system supplies raw ip cksum only */
   71  #define RAWASIS	0004000			/* user supplies raw headers */
   72  #define RAWFLAG 0007000			/* mask for raw mode flags */
   73  #define RAWERR	0010000			/* give user ICMP error messages */
   74  
   75  
   76  				/* n_state field definitions */
   77  #define UCLOSED 0000                    /* connection closed */
   78  #define UCLSERR 0001                    /* error -- connection closing */
   79  #define UABORT  0002                    /* connection aborted */
   80  #define UINTIMO 0004                    /* open failed -- init timeout */
   81  #define URXTIMO 0010                    /* retransmit too long timeout */
   82  #define URESET  0020                    /* connection aborted due to reset */
   83  #define UOPERR  0040                    /* open failed -- not enough buffers */
   84  #define UURGENT 0100                    /* urgent data received */
   85  #define UNETDWN 0200                    /* connection aborted due to net */  
   86  
   87  #define DAEMSLEEP 60			/* sleep time for user level daemons */

   44  #define ENETDOWN 36     /* net i/f not available */
   45  #define ENETCON  37     /* too many net connections */
   46  #define ENETBUF  38     /* no more net buffer space */
   47  #define ENETPARM 39     /* bad mode in net open or request in net ioctl */
   48  #define ENETRNG  40     /* bad local net addr, port, raw link or proto range */
   49  #define ENETDED  41     /* can't open: destination dead */
   50  #define ERAWBAD	 42	/* unable to send on raw connection */
   51  #define ENETERR	 43	/* attempt to read or write on closed connection */
   52  #define ENETREF	 44	/* can't open: destination refused (reset) */
   53  #define ENETUNR  45	/* can't open: destination unreachable */
   54  #define ENETTIM	 46	/* can't open: destination not responding (timeout) */

    1  struct th {                     /* tcp header (fits over ip header) */
    2  	struct th *t_next;              /* -> next tcp on rcv chain */

    4  #ifdef mbb

    6  #endif mbb
    7  	unchar t_x1;			/* (unused) */

   10  	struct socket t_s;              /* source internet address */
   11  	struct socket t_d;              /* destination internet address */
   12  	unsigned short t_src;           /* source port */
   13  	unsigned short t_dst;           /* destination port */
   14  	sequence t_seq;                 /* sequence number */
   15  	sequence t_ackno;               /* acknowledgement number */
   16  #define t_end(x) (x->t_seq + x->t_len - 1)
   17  #ifndef mbb

   19  		t_x2:4,                 /* (unused) */

   26  		t_ack:1,                /* ack flag */

   33  		t_x2:4;			/* (unused) */
   34  	unchar
   35  		t_x3:4,			/* (unused) */
   36  		t_urg:1,		/* urgent flag */

   38  		t_eol:1,		/* eol flag */
   39  		t_rst:1,		/* reset flag */
   40  		t_syn:1,		/* syn flag */
   41  		t_fin:1;		/* fin flag */
   42  #endif mbb

   44  	unsigned short t_sum;           /* checksum */

   48  struct tcb {                    /* tcp control block */
   49  
   50  	/* various pointers */
   51  
   52  	struct th *t_rcv_next;          /* -> first el on rcv queue */
   53  	struct th *t_rcv_prev;          /* -> last el on rcv queue */
   54  	struct tcb *t_tcb_next;         /* -> next tcb */
   55  	struct tcb *t_tcb_prev;         /* -> prev tcb */
   56  	struct ucb *t_ucb;              /* -> ucb */
   57  	struct mbuf *t_rcv_unack;       /* -> unacked message queue */
   58  	char *t_opts;			/* -> ip option string */
   59  
   60  	/* sequence number variables */
   61  
   62  	sequence iss;                   /* initial send seq # */
   63  	sequence irs;                   /* initial recv seq # */
   64  	sequence rcv_urp;               /* rcv urgent pointer */
   65  	sequence rcv_nxt;               /* next seq # to rcv */
   66  	sequence rcv_end;               /* rcv eol pointer */
   67  	sequence seq_fin;               /* seq # of FIN sent */
   68  	sequence snd_end;               /* send eol pointer */
   69  	sequence snd_urp;               /* snd urgent pointer */
   70  	sequence snd_lst;               /* seq # of last datum to send */
   71  	sequence snd_nxt;               /* seq # of next datum to send */
   72  	sequence snd_una;               /* seq # of first unacked datum */
   73  	sequence snd_wl;                /* seq # of last sent window */
   74  	sequence snd_hi;                /* highest seq # sent */
   75  	sequence snd_wnd;               /* send window max */
   76  	sequence t_rexmt_val;           /* val saved in rexmt timer */
   77  	sequence t_rtl_val;             /* val saved in rexmt too long timer */
   78  	sequence t_xmt_val;             /* seq # sent when xmt timer started */
   79  
   80  	/* various flags and state variables */
   81  
   82  	unsigned short
   83  		ack_due:1,              /* must we send ACK */
   84  		cancelled:1,            /* retransmit timer cancelled */
   85  		dropped_txt:1,          /* dropped incoming data */
   86  		fin_rcvd:1,             /* FIN received */
   87  		force_one:1,            /* force sending of one byte */
   88  		new_window:1,           /* received new window size */
   89  		rexmt:1,                /* this msg is a retransmission */
   90  		snd_fin:1,              /* FIN should be sent */
   91  		snd_rst:1,              /* RST should be sent */
   92  		snd_urg:1,              /* urgent data to send */
   93  		syn_acked:1,            /* SYN has been ACKed */
   94  		syn_rcvd:1,             /* SYN has been received */
   95  		usr_closed:1,           /* user has closed connection */
   96  		waited_2_ml:1,          /* wait time for FIN ACK is up */
   97  		net_keep:1,             /* don't free this net input */
   98  		usr_abort:1;            /* user has closed and does not expect
   99  					   to receive any more data */
  100  
  101  	short t_maxseg;			/* max seg size peer can handle */
  102  	unsigned short t_lport;         /* local port */
  103  	unsigned short t_fport;         /* foreign port */
  104  	unchar t_state;			/* state of this connection */
  105  	unchar t_xmtime;		/* current rexmt time */
  106  	unchar t_optlen;		/* length of ip option string */
  107  
  108  	/* timers */
  109  
  110  	unchar t_init;			/* initialization too long */
  111  	unchar t_rexmt;			/* retransmission */
  112  	unchar t_rexmttl;		/* retransmit too long */
  113  	unchar t_persist;		/* retransmit persistance */
  114  	unchar t_finack;		/* fin acknowledged */
  115  	unchar t_xmt;			/* round trip transmission time */
  116  };

    1  struct ip {                     /* ip leader */

BBN TCP/IP 3.0 beta, November 24, 1981 / bbnnet/netuser.c
SHA-256 fc30755d4951958eb32aa3ac63655cfdd7dc211d4e4b08d227d3e03542f0b771

    1  #include "../h/param.h"
    2  #include "../h/systm.h"
    3  #include "../h/buf.h"
    4  #include "../h/dir.h"
    5  #include "../h/file.h"
    6  #include "../h/inode.h"
    7  #include "../h/user.h"
    8  #include "../h/proc.h"
    9  #include "../bbnnet/mbuf.h"
   10  #include "../bbnnet/net.h"
   11  #include "../bbnnet/imp.h"
   12  #include "../bbnnet/ifcb.h"
   13  #include "../bbnnet/tcp.h"
   14  #include "../bbnnet/ucb.h"
   15  #include "../bbnnet/fsm.h"
   16  #include "../bbnnet/tcp_pred.h"
   17  
   18  extern struct user u;
   19  
   20  /*
   21   * Network user i/f routines
   22   */
   23  
   24  /*
   25   * Open a network connection.  Allocate an open file structure and a network
   26   * user control block (ucb).  Verify user open parameters and do protocol
   27   * specific open functions.
   28   */
   29  netopen(ip, mode)
   30  struct inode *ip;
   31  char *mode;       
   32  {
   33  	register struct ucb *up;
   34  	register struct con *cp;
   35  	register struct file *fp;
   36  	register struct if_local *lp;
   37  	int snd, rcv, mask, i;
   38  	struct con const;
   39  	net_t net;
   40  
   41  	iput(ip);               /* release dev inode since we don't use it */
   42  
   43  	/* get user connection structure parm */
   44  
   45  	if (copyin(mode, &const, sizeof(const)) < 0) {
   46  		u.u_error = EFAULT;
   47  		return;
   48  	}
   49  	cp = (struct con *)&const;
   50  
   51  	/* calculate send and receive buffer allocations */
   52  
   53  	snd = cp->c_sbufs << 3;
   54  	rcv = cp->c_rbufs << 3;
   55  	if (snd < 8 || snd > 64)
   56  		snd = 8;
   57  	if (rcv < 8 || rcv > 64)
   58  		rcv = 8;
   59  
   60  	/* make sure system has enough buffers for connection */
   61  
   62  	if ((snd + rcv + 2) > (NNETPAGES*NMBPG-32-netcb.n_lowat)) {
   63  		u.u_error = ENETBUF;
   64  		return;
   65  	}
   66  
   67  	/* allocate a ucb from the connection table */
   68  
   69  	for (up = contab; up->uc_proc != NULL && up < conNCON; up++);
   70  
   71  	if (up == conNCON) {            /* connection table full */
   72  		u.u_error = ENETCON;
   73  		return;
   74  	}
   75  
   76  	/* get user file table entry and point at ucb */
   77  
   78  	if ((fp = falloc()) == NULL) {
   79  		return(-1);
   80  	}
   81  	i = u.u_r.r_val1;
   82  	fp->f_flag = (FNET|FREAD|FWRITE);
   83  	fp->f_un.f_ucb = up;
   84  	fp->f_inode = NULL;
   85  
   86  	/* init common ucb fields */
   87  
   88  	up->uc_proc = u.u_procp;
   89  	up->uc_snd = snd;
   90  	up->uc_rcv = rcv;
   91  	netcb.n_lowat += snd + rcv + 2;
   92  	netcb.n_hiwat = 2 * netcb.n_lowat;
   93  	up->uc_sbuf = NULL;
   94  	up->uc_rbuf = NULL;
   95  	up->uc_timeo = cp->c_timeo;             /* overlays uc_ssize */
   96  	up->uc_rsize = 0;
   97  	up->uc_state = 0;
   98  	up->uc_lo = 0;
   99  	up->uc_hi = 0;
  100  
  101  	/* save foreign host address, route will be filled in later */
  102  
  103  	up->uc_route = NULL;
  104  	up->uc_host = cp->c_fcon;
  105  
  106  	/* if local address is specified, make sure its one of ours and use
  107  	   it if it is, otherwise pick the "best" local address from the
  108  	   local i/f table */
  109  
  110  	if (cp->c_lcon.s_addr != NULL) {
  111  		for (lp=locnet; lp < &locnet[nlocnet]; lp++)
  112  			if (lp->if_addr.s_addr == cp->c_lcon.s_addr)
  113  				goto gotaddr;
  114  		u.u_error = ENETRNG;	/* spec local address not in table */
  115  		goto conerr;	
  116  gotaddr:
  117  		/* make sure specified i/f is available */
  118  
  119  		if (lp->if_ifcb->if_avail)
  120  			up->uc_srcif = lp;
  121  		else {
  122  			u.u_error = ENETDOWN;
  123  			goto conerr;
  124  		}
  125  	} else {
  126  
  127  		/* find default 'preferred' address, look for first 
  128  		   available i/f */
  129  
  130  		up->uc_srcif = NULL;
  131  		for (lp=locnet; lp < &locnet[nlocnet]; lp++) {
  132  			if (lp->if_ifcb->if_avail) {
  133  				up->uc_srcif = locnet;	
  134  				net = iptonet(cp->c_fcon);
  135  				break;
  136  			}
  137  		}
  138  
  139  		/* error if none available */
  140  
  141  		if (up->uc_srcif == NULL) {
  142  			u.u_error = ENETDOWN;
  143  			goto conerr;
  144  		}
  145  
  146  		/* if foreign host is on a net that we are, use the address
  147  		   of the i/f on that net */
  148  
  149  		for (lp=locnet; lp < &locnet[nlocnet]; lp++) 
  150  			if (lp->if_ifcb->if_net==net && lp->if_ifcb->if_avail) {
  151  				up->uc_srcif = lp;
  152  				break;
  153  			}
  154  	}
  155  
  156  	up->uc_flags = 0;
  157  	if (cp->c_mode & CONDEBUG)
  158  		up->uc_flags |= UDEBUG;
  159  
  160  	/* call protocol open routine for rest of init */
  161  
  162  	switch (cp->c_mode & 036) {
  163  
  164  	case CONTCP:
  165  		tcpopen(up, cp);
  166  		if (u.u_error)
  167  			goto conerr;
  168  		break;
  169  
  170  	case CONRAW:
  171  		mask = URAW;
  172  		up->uc_flags |= cp->c_mode & (RAWCOMP+RAWASIS+RAWERR+RAWRALL);
  173  		goto concom;
  174  
  175  	case CONIP:
  176  		mask = UIP;
  177  		up->uc_flags |= cp->c_mode & (RAWFLAG+RAWERR+RAWRALL);
  178  	concom:
  179  		if (raw_range(up, mask, &cp->c_fcon, cp->c_lo, cp->c_hi)) {
  180          		up->uc_flags |= mask;
  181          		up->uc_lo = cp->c_lo;
  182          		up->uc_hi = cp->c_hi;
  183  			break;
  184  		}
  185  		u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  186  		goto conerr;
  187  
  188  	case CONCTL:
  189  		up->uc_flags |= UCTL;
  190  		break;
  191  
  192         	default:
  193  		u.u_error = ENETPARM;
  194  	conerr:
  195  		netclose(fp);
  196  		u.u_ofile[i] = NULL;
  197  		return(-1);
  198  	}
  199  }
  200  
  201  /*
  202   * TCP specific open routine.  Allocate a TCB and set up an open work request
  203   * for the finite state machine.
  204   */
  205  tcpopen(up, cp)
  206  register struct ucb *up;
  207  register struct con *cp;
  208  {
  209  	register struct mbuf *m;
  210  	register struct tcb *tp;
  211  	register char *p;
  212  	register i;
  213  
  214  	/* allocate a buffer for a tcb */
  215  
  216  	if ((m = m_get(1)) == NULL) {           /* no buffers */
  217  		u.u_error = ENETBUF;    
  218  		return;
  219  	}
  220  	tp = (struct tcb *)((int)m + MHEAD);    /* -> tcb */
  221  
  222  	/* zero tcb for initialization */
  223  
  224  	for (p = (char *)tp, i = MLEN; i > 0; i--)
  225  		*p++ = 0;
  226  
  227  	/* fill in ucb and tcb fields */
  228  
  229  	tp->t_ucb = up;
  230  	tp->t_fport = cp->c_fport;
  231  
  232  	/* if local port is 0, make one up, make sure connection will
  233  	   be unique. */
  234  
  235  	if (cp->c_lport == 0) {
  236  		tp->t_lport = (((int)(up - contab) + 1) << 8) | 
  237  				(up->uc_proc->p_pid & 0xff);
  238  
  239  		/* keep incrementing port until connection is unique */
  240  
  241  		while (!uniqport(up, tp)) {
  242  			if (++tp->t_lport < 0x100)
  243  				tp->t_lport = 0x100;
  244  		}
  245  	} else {
  246  		tp->t_lport = cp->c_lport;
  247  		if (!uniqport(up, tp)) {
  248  			u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  249  			m_free(m);
  250  			return;
  251  		}
  252  	}
  253  
  254  	up->uc_tcb = tp;
  255  	up->uc_flags |= UTCP;
  256  
  257  	/*  make a user open work request for tcp */
  258  
  259  	if ((cp->c_mode & CONACT) == CONACT) 
  260  		w_alloc(IUOPENR, 0, tp, NULL);
  261  	else {
  262  		up->uc_flags |= ULISTEN;
  263  		w_alloc(IUOPENA, 0, tp, NULL);
  264  	}
  265  
  266  	/* block until connection has opened or error */
  267  
  268  	while (up->uc_state == 0 && !tp->syn_acked)
  269            	sleep(up, PZERO+1);
  270  
  271  	if (up->uc_state != 0) {
  272  		if (up->uc_state & UINTIMO)
  273  			u.u_error = ENETTIM;
  274  		else if (up->uc_state & URESET)
  275  			u.u_error = ENETREF;
  276  		else if (up->uc_state & UNRCH)
  277  			u.u_error = ENETUNR;
  278  		else if (up->uc_state & UDEAD)
  279  			u.u_error = ENETDED;
  280  		else
  281  			u.u_error = ENETERR;
  282  	} else if (!tp->syn_acked)
  283  		u.u_error = EINTR;
  284  }
  285  
  286  /*
  287   * Network close routine.  For TCP connections, issue a close work request to
  288   * the tcp fsm.  Otherwise, just clean up the ucb and the file structure 
  289   */
  290  netclose(fp)
  291  register struct file *fp; 
  292  {
  293  	register struct ucb *up;
  294  	register struct tcb *tp;
  295  
  296  	up = fp->f_un.f_ucb;
  297  	tp = up->uc_tcb;
  298  
  299  	if (up->uc_flags & UTCP) {      /* tcp connection */
  300  
  301  		/* if connection has not already been closed, by user or
  302  		   system, enqueue work request.  otherwise, if the tcb
  303  		   has been deleted (completed close), then release the ucb */
  304  
  305  		if (tp != NULL) {	/* tcb still exists */
  306  			tp->usr_abort = TRUE;	/* says we've closed */
  307  			if (!tp->usr_closed)
  308  				w_alloc(IUCLOSE, 0, tp, up);
  309  		} else
  310  			up->uc_proc = NULL;
  311  
  312  	} else {        /* raw connection */
  313  
  314  	/* free ucb and lower system buffer allocation */
  315  
  316          	netcb.n_lowat -= up->uc_snd + up->uc_rcv + 2;
  317          	netcb.n_hiwat = 2 * netcb.n_lowat;
  318  		if (up->uc_route != NULL) {
  319          		h_free(up->uc_route);
  320  			up->uc_route = NULL;
  321  		}
  322          	up->uc_proc = NULL;
  323  	}
  324  
  325  	fp->f_flag = 0;
  326  	fp->f_count = 0;
  327  	fp->f_un.f_ucb = NULL;
  328  }  
  329  
  330  /*
  331   * Network read routine. Just call protocol specific read routine.
  332   */
  333  netread(up)
  334  register struct ucb *up;
  335  {
  336  
  337  	if (up->uc_state)               /* new status to report */
  338  		u.u_error = ENETSTAT;
  339  	else {
  340          	switch (up->uc_flags & URMSK) {
  341          
  342          	case UTCP:              
  343          		tcp_read(up);
  344          		break;
  345          	case UIP:                                           
  346          	case URAW:
  347          		raw_read(up);
  348          		break;
  349          	default:
  350          		u.u_error = ENETPARM;
  351          	}
  352  	}
  353  }
  354  
  355  /*
  356   * Network write routine. Just call protocol specific write routine.
  357   */
  358  netwrite(up)
  359  register struct ucb *up;
  360  {
  361  	switch (up->uc_flags & URMSK) {
  362  
  363  	case UTCP:              
  364  		tcp_write(up);
  365  		break;
  366  	case UIP:                                           
  367  	case URAW:
  368  		raw_write(up);
  369  		break;
  370  	default:
  371  		u.u_error = ENETPARM;
  372  	}
  373  }       
  374  
  375  /*
  376   * TCP read routine.  Copy data from the receive queue to user space.
  377   */
  378  tcp_read(up)                     /* read data from the net */
  379  register struct ucb *up;
  380  {
  381  	register struct mbuf *m;
  382  	register struct tcb *tp;
  383  	register len, eol;
  384  	int incount;
  385  
  386  	if ((tp = up->uc_tcb) == NULL) {
  387  		u.u_error = ENETERR;
  388  		return;
  389  	}
  390  	eol = FALSE;
  391  	incount = u.u_count;
  392  
  393  	while (u.u_count > 0 && !eol && !u.u_error && !up->uc_state) 
  394  
  395  	        if (up->uc_rsize != 0) {   /* data available */
  396  
  397  			/* lock recieve buffer */
  398  
  399                          while (up->uc_flags & ULOCK)
  400                          	sleep(&up->uc_flags, PZERO);
  401                          up->uc_flags |= ULOCK;
  402                          
  403  			/* copy the data an mbuf at a time */
  404  
  405  			m = up->uc_rbuf;
  406  
  407  			/* Must check for bad read buffer count.  Count can
  408  			   be > 0 with no buffers on queue due to a race
  409  			   condition when a foreign close happens. Just
  410  			   correct count and ignore. */
  411  
  412  			if (m == NULL) {
  413  				printf("net_read: bad rsize %d ",up->uc_rsize);
  414  				if (up->uc_tcb == NULL)
  415  					printf(" no tcb\n");
  416  				else
  417  					printf(" tcb=%X state=%d\n", up->uc_tcb, up->uc_tcb->t_state);
  418  				up->uc_rsize = 0;
  419  			}
  420  
  421                          while (m != NULL && u.u_count > 0 && !eol && !u.u_error) {
  422                          
  423                          	len = MIN(m->m_len, u.u_count);
  424                          	iomove((caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), len, B_READ);
  425                          
  426                          	if (!u.u_error) {
  427  
  428  					/* free or adjust mbuf */
  429  
  430                          		if (len == m->m_len) {
  431          				        eol = (int)m->m_act;
  432                          			m = m_free(m);
  433                          			--up->uc_rsize;
  434                          		} else {
  435                          			m->m_off += len;
  436                          			m->m_len -= len;
  437                          		}
  438                          	}
  439                          }
  440  			up->uc_rbuf = m;
  441  			up->uc_flags &= ~ULOCK; /* unlock buffer */
  442  			wakeup(&up->uc_flags);
  443  
  444  			/* if any data was read, return even if the
  445  			 * full read request was not satisfied
  446  			 */
  447  			if (incount != u.u_count)
  448  				return;
  449  		} else {                
  450  
  451  			/* if no data left and foreign FIN rcvd, just
  452  			   return, otherwise make work request and wait
  453  			   for more data */
  454  
  455  			if (tp->fin_rcvd && rcv_empty(tp))       
  456  				break;
  457  
  458          		w_alloc(IURECV, 0, tp, NULL);
  459  			sleep(up, PZERO + 1);
  460  		}
  461  
  462  	/* return outstanding status only if no data was xfered */
  463  
  464  	if (!u.u_error && up->uc_state && incount == u.u_count)          
  465  		u.u_error = ENETSTAT;
  466  }
  467  
  468  /*
  469   * TCP write routine.  Copy data from user space to a net buffer chain.  Put
  470   * this chain on a send work request for the tcp fsm.
  471   */
  472  tcp_write(up)                    
  473  register struct ucb *up;
  474  {
  475  	register struct tcb *tp;
  476  	register struct mbuf *m, *n;
  477  	register len, bufs;
  478  	struct {
  479  		struct mbuf *top;
  480  	} nn;
  481  
  482  	if ((tp = up->uc_tcb) == NULL) {                    
  483  		u.u_error = ENETERR;
  484  		return;
  485  	}
  486  
  487  	while (u.u_count > 0 && !u.u_error && up->uc_state == 0) {
  488  
  489  		/* #bufs can send now */
  490  
  491  		bufs = (int)up->uc_snd - (int)up->uc_ssize;       
  492  
  493  		/* top of chain to send */
  494  
  495  		nn.top = NULL;                          
  496  		n = (struct mbuf *)&nn;
  497  
  498  		/* copy data from user to mbuf chain for send work req */
  499  
  500          	while (bufs-- > 0 && u.u_count > 0) {
  501          
  502  			/* set up an mbuf */
  503  
  504          		if ((m = m_get(1)) == NULL) {
  505  				u.u_error = ENETBUF;
  506  				return;
  507  			}
  508          		m->m_off = MHEAD;
  509  
  510  			/* copy user data in */
  511  
  512          		len = MIN(MLEN, u.u_count);
  513          		iomove((caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), len, B_WRITE);
  514  
  515  			/* chain to work request mbufs or clean up if error */
  516  
  517          		if (!u.u_error) {
  518          			m->m_len = len;
  519          			n->m_next = m;
  520          			n = m;
  521          		} else {
  522          			m_freem(nn.top);
  523  				return;
  524  			}
  525          	}
  526          
  527          	w_alloc(IUSEND, 0, tp, nn.top);         /* make work req */
  528  
  529  		/* if more to send, sleep on more buffers */
  530  
  531  		if (u.u_count > 0)
  532  			sleep(up, PZERO+1);
  533  	}
  534  
  535  	if (!u.u_error && up->uc_state)         /* outstanding status */
  536  		u.u_error = ENETSTAT;
  537  }
  538  
  539  /*
  540   * Raw IP and local net read routine.  Copy data from raw read queue to user
  541   * space.
  542   */
  543  raw_read(up)                     
  544  register struct ucb *up;
  545  {
  546  	register struct mbuf *m, *next;
  547  	register len;
  548  
  549  	while (up->uc_rsize == 0)
  550  		sleep(up, PZERO+1);
  551  
  552  	/* lock recieve buffer */
  553  
  554          while (up->uc_flags & ULOCK)
  555          	sleep(&up->uc_flags, PZERO);
  556          up->uc_flags |= ULOCK;
  557          
  558  	/* copy the data an mbuf at a time */
  559  
  560          if ((m = up->uc_rbuf) != NULL)
  561  		next = m->m_act;
  562  	else
  563  		next = NULL;
  564  
  565          while (m != NULL && u.u_count > 0 && !u.u_error) {
  566          
  567          	len = MIN(m->m_len, u.u_count);
  568          	iomove((caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), len, B_READ);
  569          
  570          	if (!u.u_error) {
  571  
  572  			/* free or adjust mbuf */
  573  
  574          		if (len == m->m_len) {
  575          			m = m_free(m);
  576          			--up->uc_rsize;
  577          		} else {
  578          			m->m_off += len;
  579          			m->m_len -= len;
  580          		}
  581          	}
  582          }
  583  
  584  	if (m == NULL)
  585  		up->uc_rbuf = next;
  586  	else {
  587  		m->m_act = next;
  588  		up->uc_rbuf = m;
  589  	}
  590  	up->uc_flags &= ~ULOCK; /* unlock buffer */
  591  	wakeup(&up->uc_flags);
  592  }
  593  
  594  /*
  595   * Raw IP or local net write routine.  Copy data from user space to a network
  596   * buffer chain.  Call the appropriate format routine to set up a packet
  597   * header and send the data.
  598   */
  599  raw_write(up)                    
  600  register struct ucb *up;
  601  {
  602  	register struct mbuf *m, *n, *top;
  603  	register len, bufs, ulen;
  604  
  605  	/* get header mbuf */
  606  
  607  	if ((top = n = m_get(1)) == NULL) {
  608  		u.u_error = ENETBUF;
  609  		return;
  610  	}
  611  
  612  	bufs = up->uc_snd;                      /* #bufs can send now */
  613  	ulen = u.u_count;
  614  
  615  	/* copy data from user to mbuf chain for send */
  616  
  617          while (bufs-- > 0 && u.u_count > 0) {
  618          
  619  		/* set up an mbuf */
  620  
  621          	if ((m = m_get(1)) == NULL) {
  622  			u.u_error = ENETBUF;
  623  			return;
  624  		}
  625          	m->m_off = MHEAD;
  626  			
  627  		/* copy user data in */
  628  
  629          	len = MIN(MLEN, u.u_count);
  630          	iomove((caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), len, B_WRITE);
  631  
  632  		/* chain to header mbuf or clean up if error */
  633  
  634          	if (!u.u_error) {
  635          		m->m_len = len;
  636          		n->m_next = m;
  637          		n = m;
  638          	} else {
  639          		m_freem(top);
  640  			return;
  641  		}
  642          }
  643  
  644  	/* only send raw message if all of it could be read */
  645  
  646  	if (u.u_count > 0) {
  647  		u.u_error = ERAWBAD;
  648  		m_freem(top);
  649  		return;
  650  	}
  651  
  652  	/* send to net */
  653  
  654  	switch (up->uc_flags & URMSK) {
  655  
  656  	case UIP:
  657          	ip_raw(up, top, ulen);
  658  		break;
  659  
  660  	case URAW:
  661  		(*up->uc_srcif->if_ifcb->if_raw)(up, top, ulen);
  662  		break;
  663  
  664  	default:
  665  		m_freem(top);
  666  		u.u_error = ENETPARM;
  667  	}
  668  
  669  	if (!u.u_error && up->uc_state)         /* outstanding status */
  670  		u.u_error = ENETSTAT;
  671  }
  672  
  673  /*
  674   * Network control functions
  675   */
  676  netioctl(up, cmd, cmdp)
  677  register struct ucb *up;
  678  int cmd;
  679  caddr_t cmdp;
  680  {
  681  	register struct inode *ip;
  682  	register struct tcb *tp;
  683  	register struct mbuf *m;
  684  	struct netstate nstat;
  685  	struct {
  686  		int optlen;
  687  		char *options;
  688  	} opt;
  689  
  690  	tp = up->uc_tcb;
  691  
  692  	switch (cmd) {
  693  
  694  	case NETGETS:                   /* get net status */
  695  		bcopy((caddr_t)&up->uc_lo, (caddr_t)&nstat, sizeof(struct netstate));
  696  		if (tp != NULL) {
  697  			nstat.n_lport = tp->t_lport;
  698  			nstat.n_fport = tp->t_fport;
  699  		}
  700  		nstat.n_fcon = up->uc_host;
  701  		nstat.n_lcon = up->uc_srcif->if_addr;
  702  
  703  		if (copyout((caddr_t)&nstat, cmdp, sizeof(struct netstate)))
  704  			u.u_error = EFAULT;
  705  		else
  706  			up->uc_state = 0;
  707  		break;
  708  
  709  	case NETSETU:                   /* set urgent mode */
  710  		up->uc_flags |= UURG;
  711  		break;
  712  
  713  	case NETRSETU:                  /* reset urgent mode */
  714  		up->uc_flags &= ~UURG;
  715  		break;
  716  
  717  	case NETSETE:                   /* set eol mode */
  718  		up->uc_flags |= UEOL;
  719  		break;
  720  
  721  	case NETRSETE:                  /* reset eol mode */
  722  		up->uc_flags &= ~UEOL;
  723  		break;
  724  
  725  	case NETCLOSE:                  /* tcp close but continue to rcv */
  726  		if ((up->uc_flags & UTCP) && tp != NULL && !tp->usr_closed) 
  727  			w_alloc(IUCLOSE, 0, tp, up);
  728  		break;
  729  
  730  	case NETABORT:                  /* tcp user abort */
  731  		if ((up->uc_flags & UTCP) && tp != NULL)
  732  			w_alloc(IUABORT, 0, tp, up);
  733  		break;
  734  
  735  	case NETSETD:                   /* set debugging file */
  736  		if (suser()) {
  737  			if (cmdp == NULL) {
  738  				if (netcb.n_debug) {
  739  					plock(netcb.n_debug);
  740  					iput(netcb.n_debug);
  741  					netcb.n_debug = NULL;
  742  				}
  743  
  744  			} else {
  745  				if (netcb.n_debug != NULL) {
  746  					u.u_error = EBUSY;
  747  					break;
  748  				}
  749  				u.u_dirp = cmdp;
  750  				if ((ip = namei(uchar, 0)) != NULL) {
  751  					if ((ip->i_mode & IFMT) != IFREG) {
  752  						u.u_error = EACCES;
  753  						iput(ip);
  754  					} else {
  755  						netcb.n_debug = ip;
  756  						prele(ip);
  757  					}
  758  				}
  759  			}
  760  		} else
  761  			u.u_error = EPERM;
  762  		break;
  763  
  764  	case NETRESET:                  /* forced tcp reset */
  765  		if (suser()) {
  766  			for (up = contab; up < conNCON; up++)
  767  				if ((up->uc_flags & UTCP) && 
  768  				     up->uc_tcb != NULL &&
  769  				     up->uc_tcb == (struct tcb *)cmdp) {
  770  					w_alloc(INCLEAR, 0, cmdp, NULL);
  771  					return;
  772  				}
  773  			u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  774  		} else
  775  			u.u_error = EPERM;
  776  		break;
  777  
  778  	case NETDEBUG:                  /* toggle debugging flag */
  779  		for (up = contab; up < conNCON; up++)
  780  			if ((up->uc_flags & UTCP) && up->uc_tcb != NULL &&
  781  			    up->uc_tcb == (struct tcb *)cmdp) {
  782  				up->uc_flags ^= UDEBUG;
  783  				return;
  784  			}
  785  		u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  786  		break;
  787  	
  788  	case NETTCPOPT:			/* copy ip options to tcb */
  789  		if ((up->uc_flags & UTCP) && tp != NULL && cmdp != NULL) {
  790  			
  791  			if (copyin((caddr_t)cmdp, &opt, sizeof opt)) {
  792  				u.u_error = EFAULT;
  793  				return;
  794  			}
  795  
  796  			/* maximum option string is 40 bytes */
  797  
  798  			if (opt.optlen > 40) {
  799  				u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  800  				return;
  801  			}
  802  
  803  			/* get a buffer to hold the option string */
  804  
  805  			if ((m = m_get(1)) == NULL) {
  806  				u.u_error = ENETBUF;
  807  				return;
  808  			}
  809  			m->m_off = MHEAD;
  810  			m->m_len = opt.optlen;
  811  
  812  			if (copyin((caddr_t)opt.options, 
  813  				(caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), opt.optlen)) {
  814  				m_free(m);
  815  				u.u_error = EFAULT;
  816  				return;	
  817  			}
  818  			tp->t_opts = (char *)((int)m + m->m_off);
  819  			tp->t_optlen = opt.optlen;
  820  		}
  821  		break;
  822  
  823  	case NETGINIT:			/* reread the gateway file */
  824  		if (suser())
  825  			if (gatinit() < 0)
  826  				u.u_error = ENETRNG;
  827  		else
  828  			u.u_error = EPERM;
  829  		break;
  830  
  831  	default:
  832  		u.u_error = ENETPARM;
  833  	}
  834  }
  835  
  836  /*
  837   * Check if specified connection is unique.
  838   */
  839  uniqport(up, tp)
  840  register struct ucb *up;
  841  register struct tcb *tp;
  842  {
  843  	register struct ucb *q;
  844  
  845  	for (q = contab; q < conNCON; q++) {
  846  		if (q->uc_proc != NULL && q != up &&
  847  		    q->uc_tcb->t_lport == tp->t_lport &&
  848  		    q->uc_tcb->t_fport == tp->t_fport &&
  849  		    q->uc_host.s_addr == up->uc_host.s_addr &&
  850  		    q->uc_srcif->if_addr.s_addr ==
  851  		      up->uc_srcif->if_addr.s_addr) 
  852  			return(FALSE);
  853  	}
  854  
  855  	return(TRUE);
  856  }

==============================================================================
BBN TCP/IP 3.0 beta, November 24, 1981 / bbnnet/ip_output.c
SHA-256 3af6cda5c7989cdc2d9ae46efe922fcc26def04dbf45cd1d6a4fe5b5e8ae864f

    1  #include "../h/param.h"
    2  #include "../h/dir.h"
    3  #include "../h/user.h"
    4  #include "../bbnnet/mbuf.h"
    5  #include "../bbnnet/net.h"
    6  #include "../bbnnet/tcp.h"
    7  #include "../bbnnet/ip.h"
    8  #include "../bbnnet/ifcb.h"
    9  #include "../bbnnet/ucb.h"
   10  
   11  extern struct user u;
   12  
   13  /*
   14   *	ip_output is called from tcp and passed an mbuf chain of a packet to
   15   *	send to the local network.  The first mbuf contains a tcp and partial
   16   *	ip header which is filled in.  After determining a local route for
   17   *	the packet, it is sent to the local net through ip_send.
   18   */
   19  ip_output(up, mp, proto, len, optlen, options)
   20  register struct ucb *up;
   21  struct mbuf *mp;
   22  int proto, len, optlen;
   23  char *options;
   24  {
   25  	register struct ip *p, *q;
   26  	int hlen;
   27  	struct socket local;
   28  	struct socket ip_route();
   29  
   30  	p = (struct ip *)((int)mp + mp->m_off); /* -> ip header */
   31  
   32  	/* Find route for datagram, if one has not been assigned.
   33  	   Make a host table entry for the local network route to
   34  	   the destination */
   35  
   36  	if (up->uc_route == NULL) {
   37  		local = ip_route(&p->ip_src, &p->ip_dst);
   38  		if (local.s_addr == NULL || 
   39  		    (up->uc_route = h_make(&local)) == NULL) {
   40  			m_freem(mp);
   41  			return(FALSE);
   42  		} 
   43  	}
   44  
   45  	/* copy ip option string to header */
   46  
   47  	if (optlen > 0) {
   48  		mp->m_off -= optlen;
   49  		mp->m_len += optlen;
   50  		q = p;
   51  		p = (struct ip *)((int)p - optlen);
   52  		bcopy((caddr_t)q, (caddr_t)p, sizeof(struct ip)); 
   53  		bcopy(options, (caddr_t)((int)p + sizeof(struct ip)), optlen);
   54  	}
   55  
   56  	/* fill in ip header fields */
   57  
   58  	hlen = sizeof(struct ip) + optlen;
   59  	p->ip_len = len + hlen;
   60  	p->ip_p = proto;
   61  	p->ip_v = IPVERSION;    
   62  	p->ip_hl = hlen >> 2;
   63  	p->ip_off = 0;
   64  	p->ip_ttl = MAXTTL;
   65  	p->ip_id = netcb.n_ip_cnt++;
   66  
   67  	return(ip_send(p,up->uc_srcif->if_ifcb,&up->uc_route->h_addr,hlen,FALSE));
   68  }
   69  
   70  /*
   71   *	ip_send is called with a packet with a completed ip header 
   72   *	(except for checksum) by the raw internet driver.  It fragments the
   73   *	packet, inserts the ip checksum, and calls the appropriate local net
   74   *	output routine to send it to the net.
   75   */
   76  ip_send(p, ip, ap, hlen, asis)
   77  register struct ip *p;
   78  register struct ifcb *ip;
   79  struct socket *ap;
   80  int hlen, asis;
   81  {	 
   82  	register struct mbuf *m, *n;
   83  	register i, rnd;
   84  	struct mbuf *mm;
   85  	int adj, max, len, off;
   86  	unsigned short j;
   87  	unsigned olen;
   88  	
   89  	m = dtom(p);
   90  
   91  	if (p->ip_len > ip->if_mtu) {		/* must fragment */
   92  		if (p->ip_off & ip_df) {
   93  			m_freem(m);
   94  			return(FALSE);
   95  		}
   96  		max = ip->if_mtu - hlen;	/* max data length in frag */
   97  		len = p->ip_len - hlen;         /* data length */
   98  		off = 0;                        /* fragment offset */
   99  
  100  		while (len > 0) {
  101  
  102  			/* correct the header */
  103  
  104  			p->ip_off |= off >> 3;
  105  
  106  			/* find the end of the fragment */
  107  
  108  			i = -hlen;
  109  			while (m != NULL) {
  110  				i += m->m_len;
  111  				if (i > max)
  112  					break;
  113  				n = m;
  114  				m = m->m_next;
  115  			}
  116  
  117  			if (i < max || m == NULL) {     /* last fragment */
  118  				p->ip_off = p->ip_off & ~ip_mf;
  119  				p->ip_len = i + hlen;
  120  				break;
  121  
  122  			} else {                        /* more fragments */
  123  
  124  				/* allocate header mbuf for next fragment */
  125  
  126  				if ((mm = m_get(1)) == NULL) {
  127  					m_freem(m);
  128  					return(FALSE);
  129  				}
  130  
  131  				p->ip_off |= ip_mf;
  132  
  133  				/* terminate fragment at 8 byte boundary 
  134  				   (round down) */
  135  
  136  				i -= m->m_len;
  137          			rnd = i & ~7;           /* fragment length */
  138  				adj = i - rnd;          /* leftover in mbuf */
  139  				p->ip_len = rnd + hlen; 
  140  
  141  				/* setup header for next fragment and 
  142  				   append remaining fragment data */
  143  
  144  				n->m_next = NULL;                   
  145  				mm->m_next = m;        
  146  				m = mm;
  147  				m->m_off = MSIZE - hlen - adj;
  148  				m->m_len = hlen + adj;
  149  
  150  				/* copy old header to new */
  151  
  152  				bcopy(p, (caddr_t)((int)m + m->m_off), hlen);
  153  
  154  				/* copy leftover data from previous frag */
  155  
  156  				if (adj) {
  157  					n->m_len -= adj;
  158  					bcopy((caddr_t)((int)n+n->m_len+n->m_off),
  159  					      (caddr_t)((int)m+m->m_off+hlen), 
  160  							adj);
  161  				}
  162  			}
  163  
  164                          /* finish ip leader by calculating checksum and doing
  165                             necessary byte-swapping and send to local net */
  166                          
  167  			if (!asis) {
  168  				p->ip_sum = 0;
  169  				olen = p->ip_len;
  170  				ip_to_net(p);
  171  #ifndef mbb
  172  				p->ip_sum = cksum(m, hlen);     
  173  #else
  174  				j = cksum(m, hlen);
  175  				p->ip_sum = short_to_net(j);
  176  #endif mbb
  177  			}
  178  			(*ip->if_send)(m, ip, ap, olen, IPLINK, asis);
  179  
  180  			p = (struct ip *)((int)m + m->m_off);   /* ->new hdr */
  181  			len -= rnd;
  182  			off += rnd;
  183  		}
  184  	} 
  185  
  186  	/* complete header, byte swap, and send to local net */
  187  
  188  	if (!asis) {
  189  		p->ip_sum = 0;
  190  		olen = p->ip_len;
  191  		ip_to_net(p);
  192  #ifndef mbb
  193  		p->ip_sum = cksum(m, hlen);     
  194  #else
  195  		j = cksum(m, hlen);
  196  		p->ip_sum = short_to_net(j);
  197  #endif mbb
  198  	}
  199  	return((*ip->if_send)(m, ip, ap, olen, IPLINK, asis));     
  200  }
  201  
  202  /*
  203   * Find a route to this destination.  Given the source and destination 
  204   * addresses, it returns a local net address
  205   * to send to (either the address of the destination itself or a gateway).
  206   */
  207  struct socket ip_route(src, dst)
  208  struct socket *src;
  209  struct socket *dst;
  210  {
  211  	register struct if_local *lp;
  212  	register struct gway *gp;
  213  	struct socket nullsock;
  214  	net_t snet, dnet;	
  215  
  216  	nullsock.s_addr = 0;
  217  
  218  	/* get network parts of src and dest addresses */
  219  
  220  	snet = iptonet(*src);
  221  	dnet = iptonet(*dst);	
  222  
  223  	/* first, look for dest net in local nets table */
  224  
  225  	for (lp = locnet; lp < &locnet[nlocnet]; lp++) 
  226  		if (dnet == lp->if_ifcb->if_net && snet == dnet &&
  227  		    lp->if_ifcb->if_avail) 
  228  			return(*dst);
  229  
  230  	/* no local access to dest net, search gateway table */
  231  
  232  	for (gp = gateway; gp < gatewayNGATE; gp++)
  233  		if (snet == gp->g_lnet && dnet == gp->g_fnet && 
  234  		    gp->g_ifcb->if_avail) 
  235  			return(gp->g_local);
  236  
  237  	/* no direct gateway access, pick a smart gateway */
  238  
  239  	for (gp = gateway; gp < gatewayNGATE; gp++)
  240  		if (gp->g_flags & GWSMART && gp->g_ifcb->if_avail) 
  241  			return(gp->g_local);
  242  
  243  	/* can't get theah from heah */
  244  
  245  	return (nullsock);
  246  }
  247  
  248  /*
  249   * Raw IP output routine.  Called from raw_write with the user's message
  250   * chained to an empty mbuf for header construction.  There are three
  251   * possibilities:  1) header is constructed by ip_output in the empty mbuf,
  252   * 2) header is supplied by the user, but the checksum is generated in
  253   * ip_send, or 3) header is supplied by the user including checksum.  In
  254   * cases (2) and (3), the user supplied header is copied into the first
  255   * mbuf which accomodates the local net header as well.
  256   */
  257  ip_raw(up, m, len)
  258  register struct ucb *up;
  259  register struct mbuf *m;
  260  int len;
  261  {
  262  	register struct ip *p, *q;
  263  	register struct ifcb *ip;
  264  	register struct mbuf *n;
  265  	int hlen;
  266  	net_t net;
  267  	struct socket route;
  268  	struct socket ip_route();
  269  
  270  	switch (up->uc_flags & RAWFLAG) {
  271  
  272  	case RAWCOMP:			/* compose header */
  273  
  274  		/* build header in bottom of first mbuf */
  275  
  276  		m->m_off = MSIZE - sizeof(struct ip);
  277  		m->m_len = sizeof(struct ip);
  278  		p = (struct ip *)((int)m + m->m_off);
  279  
  280  		/* insert source and destination addrs from ucb */
  281  
  282  		p->ip_src = up->uc_srcif->if_addr;
  283  		p->ip_dst = up->uc_host;
  284  
  285  		/* call ip routine to compose rest of header */
  286  
  287  		if (!ip_output(up, m, up->uc_lo, len, 0, NULL))
  288  			u.u_error = ERAWBAD;
  289  		break;
  290  	
  291  	case RAWASIS:			/* send header asis */
  292  	case RAWVER:			/* send asis but insert checksum */
  293  
  294  		/* copy header into first mbuf */
  295  
  296  		n = m->m_next;
  297  		p = (struct ip *)((int)n + n->m_off);
  298  		hlen = p->ip_hl << 2;
  299  		if (hlen > MSIZE) 
  300  			goto bad;
  301  		m->m_off = MSIZE - hlen;
  302  		m->m_len = hlen;
  303  		q = (struct ip *)((int)m + m->m_off);
  304  		bcopy((caddr_t)p, (caddr_t)q, hlen);
  305  		n->m_off += hlen;
  306  		n->m_len -= hlen;
  307  
  308  		/* find an i/f to send this out on */
  309  
  310  		route = ip_route(&q->ip_src, &q->ip_dst);
  311  		if (route.s_addr == NULL)
  312  			goto bad;
  313  		net = iptonet(route);
  314  		for (ip=ifcb; ip < &ifcb[nifcb]; ip++)
  315  			if (ip->if_net == net)
  316  				break;
  317  		if (ip == &ifcb[nifcb])
  318  			goto bad;
  319  		if (!ip_send(q, ip, &route, hlen, up->uc_flags&RAWASIS))
  320  			u.u_error = ERAWBAD;
  321  		break;
  322  	}
  323  	return;
  324  
  325  bad:
  326  	m_freem(m);
  327  	u.u_error = ERAWBAD;
  328  }
  329  
  330  /*
  331   * Take the non-byte data in the ip header as it is stored internally, and
  332   * convert it to the form that goes out over the network (i.e., either byte
  333   * swap or add holes).
  334   */
  335  ip_to_net(p)
  336  register struct ip *p;
  337  {
  338  	p->ip_len = short_to_net(p->ip_len);
  339  	p->ip_id = short_to_net(p->ip_id);
  340  	p->ip_off = short_to_net(p->ip_off);
  341  }

==============================================================================
BBN TCP/IP 3.0 beta, November 24, 1981 / bbnnet/ip_input.c
SHA-256 aa298eb90bf2a3e1b73180a5f50830cc9c4ec34517c5c4a4d17d3e99feaede0b

    1  #include "../h/param.h"
    2  #include "../bbnnet/mbuf.h"
    3  #include "../bbnnet/net.h"
    4  #include "../bbnnet/ifcb.h"
    5  #include "../bbnnet/tcp.h"
    6  #include "../bbnnet/ip.h"
    7  #include "../bbnnet/icmp.h"
    8  #include "../bbnnet/ucb.h"
    9  
   10  int nosum = 0;
   11  
   12  /*
   13   * IP level input routine 
   14   *
   15   * Called from 1822 level upon receipt of an internet datagram or fragment.  
   16   * This routine does fragment reassembly, if necessary, and passes completed
   17   * datagrams to higher level protocol processing routines on the basis of the 
   18   * ip header protocol field.  It is passed a pointer to an mbuf chain 
   19   * containing the datagram/fragment. The mbuf offset/length are set to point 
   20   * at the ip header.
   21   */
   22  ip_input(mp, ifp)
   23  struct mbuf *mp;
   24  struct ifcb *ifp;
   25  {
   26  	register struct ip *ip, *q;
   27  	register struct ipq *fp;
   28  	register struct mbuf *m;
   29  	register i;
   30  	struct mbuf *n;
   31  	int hlen;
   32  	struct ip *p, *savq;
   33  	struct ipq *ip_findf();
   34  
   35  	ip = (struct ip *)((int)mp + mp->m_off);        /* ->ip hdr */
   36  
   37  	/* make sure header does not overflow mbuf */
   38  
   39  	if ((hlen = ip->ip_hl << 2) > mp->m_len) {
   40  		printf("ip header overflow\n");
   41  		netlog(mp);
   42  		return;
   43  	}
   44  
   45  #ifndef mbb
   46  	i = (unsigned short)ip->ip_sum;
   47  #else
   48  	i = (unsigned short)short_from_net(ip->ip_sum);
   49  #endif mbb
   50  	ip->ip_sum = 0;
   51  
   52  	if (i != (unsigned short)cksum(mp, hlen)) {     /* verify checksum */
   53  		netstat.ip_badsum++;
   54  		if (!nosum) {
   55  			netlog(mp);
   56          		return;
   57  		}
   58  	}
   59  
   60  	ip_from_net(ip);	/* byte-swap header */
   61  
   62  	netcb.n_ip_lock++;      /* lock frag reass.q */
   63  	fp = ip_findf(ip);      /* look for chain on reass.q with this hdr */
   64  
   65  	/* adjust message length to remove any padding */
   66  
   67  	for (i=0, m=mp; m != NULL; m = m->m_next) {
   68  		i += m->m_len;
   69  		n = m;
   70  	}
   71  	i -= ip->ip_len;
   72  
   73  	if (i != 0) 
   74          	if (i > (int)n->m_len)
   75          		m_adj(mp, -i);
   76          	else
   77          		n->m_len -= i;
   78  
   79  	ip->ip_len -= hlen;     /* length of data */
   80    	ip->ip_mff = ((ip->ip_off & ip_mf) ? TRUE : FALSE);                
   81  	ip->ip_off <<= 3;                                                 
   82  	if (!ip->ip_mff && ip->ip_off == 0) {    /* not fragmented */
   83  
   84  		if (fp != NULL) {               /* free existing reass.q chain */
   85  		        
   86  			q = fp->iqx.ip_next;    /* free mbufs assoc. w/chain */
   87  			while (q != (struct ip *)fp) {
   88  				p = q->ip_next;
   89  				m_freem(dtom(q));
   90  				q = p;
   91  			}
   92  			ip_freef(fp);           /* free header */
   93  		} 
   94  		netcb.n_ip_lock = 0;
   95  
   96  		if (hlen > sizeof(struct ip))
   97  			ip_opt(ip, hlen);	/* option processing */
   98  		i = ip->ip_p;
   99  
  100  		/* pass to next level */
  101  
  102  		if (i == TCPROTO)
  103  			tcp_input(mp, ifp);
  104  		else if (i == GGPROTO)
  105  			ggp(mp, ifp);
  106  		else if (i == ICMPROTO)
  107  			icmp(mp, ifp);
  108  		else
  109  			raw_input(mp, ifp, &ip->ip_src, &ip->ip_dst, i, 
  110  				  UIP, FALSE);
  111  
  112  	} else {                       
  113  
  114          	/* -> msg buf beyond ip hdr if not first fragment */
  115  
  116  		if (ip->ip_off != 0) {
  117                  	mp->m_off += hlen;
  118                  	mp->m_len -= hlen;
  119  		}
  120  
  121  		if (fp == NULL) {               /* first fragment of datagram in */
  122  
  123  		/* set up reass.q header: enq it, set up as head of frag
  124  		   chain, set a timer value, and move in ip header */
  125  
  126  			if ((m = m_get(1)) == NULL) {   /* allocate an mbuf */
  127  				m_freem(mp);
  128  				return;
  129  			}
  130  
  131  			fp = (struct ipq *)((int)m + MHEAD);
  132  			fp->iqx.ip_next = fp->iqx.ip_prev = (struct ip *)fp; 
  133  			bcopy(ip, &fp->iqh, 
  134  			      MIN(MLEN-(sizeof(struct ipq)-sizeof(struct ip)), 
  135  			    		hlen));
  136  			fp->iqx.ip_ttl = MAXTTL;
  137  			fp->iq_next = NULL;                   
  138  			fp->iq_prev = netcb.n_ip_tail;
  139  			if (netcb.n_ip_head != NULL) 
  140  				netcb.n_ip_tail->iq_next = fp;
  141  			else 
  142  				netcb.n_ip_head = fp;
  143  			netcb.n_ip_tail = fp;
  144  		} 
  145  
  146  		/*
  147                   * Merge fragment into reass.q
  148  		 *
  149                   * Algorithm:   Match  start  and  end  bytes  of new
  150                   * fragment  with  fragments  on  the  queue.   If no
  151                   * overlaps  are  found,  add  new  frag. to the queue.
  152                   * Otherwise, adjust start and end of new frag.  so  no
  153                   * overlap   and   add  remainder  to  queue.   If  any
  154                   * fragments are completely covered by the new one,  or
  155                   * if  the  new  one is completely duplicated, free the
  156                   * fragments.
  157                   */	              
  158  	        q = fp->iqx.ip_next;    /* -> top of reass. chain */
  159  		ip->ip_end = ip->ip_off + ip->ip_len - 1;
  160  
  161  		/* skip frags which new doesn't overlap at end */
  162  
  163  		while ((q != (struct ip *)fp) && (ip->ip_off > q->ip_end))
  164  			q = q->ip_next;
  165  
  166  		if (q == (struct ip *)fp)	/* frag at end of chain */
  167  			ip_enq(ip, fp->iqx.ip_prev);
  168  		else { 				/* frag doesn't overlap any */ 
  169  			if (ip->ip_end < q->ip_off)     
  170  				ip_enq(ip, q->ip_prev);
  171  
  172  			/* new overlaps beginning of next frag only */
  173  
  174  			else if (ip->ip_end < q->ip_end) {
  175  				if ((i = ip->ip_end-q->ip_off+1) < ip->ip_len) {
  176          				ip->ip_len -= i;
  177          				ip->ip_end -= i;
  178  					m_adj(mp, -i);
  179  					ip_enq(ip, q->ip_prev);
  180  				} else
  181  					m_freem(mp);
  182  
  183  			/* new overlaps end of previous frag */
  184  
  185  			} else {
  186  				savq = q;
  187  				if (ip->ip_off <= q->ip_off) {  
  188  
  189  					/* complete cover */
  190  
  191  					savq = q->ip_prev;
  192  					ip_deq(q);
  193  					m_freem(dtom(q));
  194  
  195  				} else {	/* overlap */
  196  					if ((i = q->ip_end-ip->ip_off+1) 
  197  								< ip->ip_len) {
  198          					ip->ip_off += i;  
  199          					ip->ip_len -= i;  
  200          					m_adj(mp, i);
  201  					} else
  202  						ip->ip_len = 0;
  203  				}
  204  
  205  			/* new overlaps at beginning of successor frags */
  206  
  207  				q = savq->ip_next;
  208  				while ((q != (struct ip *)fp) &&
  209  					(ip->ip_len != 0) && 
  210  					(q->ip_off < ip->ip_end)) 
  211  
  212  					/* complete cover */
  213  
  214  					if (q->ip_end <= ip->ip_end) {
  215  						p = q->ip_next;
  216  						ip_deq(q);
  217  						m_freem(dtom(q));
  218  						q = p;
  219  
  220  					} else {        /* overlap */
  221  						if ((i = ip->ip_end-q->ip_off+1) 
  222  								< ip->ip_len) {
  223          						ip->ip_len -= i;
  224          						ip->ip_end -= i;
  225          						m_adj(mp, -i);
  226  						} else
  227  							ip->ip_len = 0;
  228  						break;
  229  					}
  230  
  231  			/* enqueue whatever is left of new before successors */
  232  
  233  				if (ip->ip_len != 0)
  234  					ip_enq(ip, savq);
  235  				else
  236  					m_freem(mp);
  237  			}
  238  		}
  239  
  240  		/* check for completed fragment reassembly */
  241  
  242  		if ((i = ip_done(fp)) > 0) {
  243  
  244  			p = fp->iqx.ip_next;            /* -> top mbuf */
  245  			m = dtom(p);
  246  			p->ip_len = i;                  /* total data length */
  247  
  248  			/* option processing */
  249  
  250  			if ((hlen = p->ip_hl<<2) > sizeof(struct ip))
  251  				ip_opt(p, hlen);       
  252  
  253  			ip_mergef(fp);                  /* clean frag chain */
  254  
  255  			/* copy src/dst internet address to header mbuf */
  256  
  257  			bcopy(&fp->iqh.ip_src, &p->ip_src, 
  258  					2*sizeof(struct socket));
  259  			ip_freef(fp);                   /* dequeue header */
  260  			netcb.n_ip_lock = 0;
  261  
  262  			/* call next level with completed datagram */
  263  
  264  			i = p->ip_p;
  265  			if (i == TCPROTO)
  266  				tcp_input(m, ifp);
  267  			else if (i == GGPROTO)
  268  				ggp(m, ifp);
  269  			else if (i == ICMPROTO)
  270  				icmp(m, ifp);
  271          		else
  272  				raw_input(m, ifp, &p->ip_src, &p->ip_dst, i, 
  273  					  UIP, FALSE);
  274  		} else
  275  			netcb.n_ip_lock = 0;
  276  	}
  277  }
  278  
  279  /*
  280   * Check to see if fragment reassembly is complete
  281   */
  282  ip_done(p)      
  283  register struct ip *p;
  284  {
  285  	register struct ip *q;
  286  	register next;
  287  
  288  	q = p->ip_next;                       
  289  
  290  	if (q->ip_off != 0)
  291  		return(0);
  292  	do {
  293  		next = q->ip_end + 1;
  294  		q = q->ip_next;
  295  	} while ((q != p) && (q->ip_off == next));
  296  
  297  	if ((q == p) && !(q->ip_prev->ip_mff))        /* all fragments in */
  298  		return(next);                         /* total data length */
  299  	else
  300  		return(0);
  301  }
  302  
  303  /*
  304   * Merge mbufs of fragments of completed datagram 
  305   */
  306  ip_mergef(p)    
  307  register struct ip *p;
  308  {
  309  	register struct mbuf *m, *n;
  310  	register struct ip *q;
  311  	int dummy;
  312  
  313  	q = p->ip_next;                         /* -> bottom of reass chain */
  314  	n = (struct mbuf *)&dummy;              /* dummy for init assignment */
  315  
  316  	while (q != p) {        /* through chain */
  317  
  318  	        n->m_next = m = dtom(q);
  319  		while (m != NULL) 
  320  			if (m->m_len != 0) {
  321  				n = m;
  322  				m = m->m_next;
  323  			} else                  /* free null mbufs */
  324  				n->m_next = m = m_free(m);
  325  		q = q->ip_next;
  326  	}
  327  }
  328  
  329  /*
  330   * Dequeue and free reass.q header 
  331   */
  332  ip_freef(fp)	        
  333  register struct ipq *fp;
  334  {
  335  	if (fp->iq_prev != NULL)                               
  336  		(fp->iq_prev)->iq_next = fp->iq_next;  
  337  	else                                           
  338  		netcb.n_ip_head = fp->iq_next;         
  339  	if (fp->iq_next != NULL)                               
  340  		(fp->iq_next)->iq_prev = fp->iq_prev;  
  341  	else
  342  		netcb.n_ip_tail = fp->iq_prev;
  343  	m_free(dtom(fp));
  344  }
  345  
  346  /*
  347   * Does fragment reassembly chain with this header exist? 
  348   */
  349  struct ipq *ip_findf(p)         
  350  register struct ip *p;
  351  {
  352  	register struct ipq *fp;
  353  
  354  	for (fp = netcb.n_ip_head; (fp != NULL && (
  355  			p->ip_src.s_addr != fp->iqh.ip_src.s_addr ||
  356  			p->ip_dst.s_addr != fp->iqh.ip_dst.s_addr ||
  357  			p->ip_id != fp->iqh.ip_id ||
  358  			p->ip_p != fp->iqh.ip_p)); fp = fp->iq_next);
  359  	return(fp);
  360  }
  361  
  362  /*
  363   * Process ip options 
  364   */
  365  ip_opt(ip, hlen)        
  366  struct ip *ip;
  367  int hlen;
  368  {
  369  	register char *p, *q;
  370  	register i, len;
  371  	register struct mbuf *m;
  372  
  373  	p = q = (char *)((int)ip + sizeof(struct ip));  /* -> at options */
  374  	
  375  	if ((i = hlen - sizeof(struct ip)) > 0) {       /* any options */
  376  
  377  /*      *** IP OPTION PROCESSING ***
  378  
  379  		while (i > 0)
  380  
  381    			switch (*q++) {
  382  			case 0:                 
  383  			case 1:                 
  384  				i--;
  385  				break;
  386  
  387  			default:
  388  				i -= *q;
  389  				q += *q;
  390  			}
  391  */              q += i;
  392  		m = dtom(q);
  393  		len = (int)m + m->m_off + m->m_len - (int)q;
  394  		bcopy((caddr_t)q, (caddr_t)p, len);    /* remove options */
  395  		m->m_len -= i;
  396  	}
  397  }
  398  
  399  /*
  400   * Enqueue a fragment on the reassembly chain.
  401   */
  402  ip_enq(p, prev)
  403  register struct ip *p;
  404  register struct ip *prev;
  405  {
  406  	p->ip_prev = prev;
  407  	p->ip_next = prev->ip_next;
  408  	prev->ip_next->ip_prev = p;
  409  	prev->ip_next = p;
  410  }
  411   
  412  /*
  413   * Dequeue a fragment from reassembly chain.
  414   */
  415  ip_deq(p)
  416  register struct ip *p;
  417  {
  418  	p->ip_prev->ip_next = p->ip_next;
  419  	p->ip_next->ip_prev = p->ip_prev;
  420  }
  421  
  422  /*
  423   * Take the non-byte data in the ip header as it comes in from the net
  424   * and convert it to the form used internally (i.e., either byte swap
  425   * or squeeze).
  426   */
  427  ip_from_net(p)    
  428  register struct ip *p;
  429  {
  430  
  431  	p->ip_len = short_from_net(p->ip_len);
  432  	p->ip_id = short_from_net(p->ip_id);
  433  	p->ip_off = short_from_net(p->ip_off);
  434  }
  435  
  436  /*
  437   * IP fragment reassembly timeout routine.
  438   */
  439  ip_timeo()      
  440  {
  441  	register struct ip *p, *q;
  442  	register struct ipq *fp;
  443  
  444  	timeout(ip_timeo, 0, HZ);       /* reschedule every second */
  445  
  446  	if (netcb.n_ip_lock)    /* reass.q must not be in use */
  447  		return;
  448  
  449  	/* search through reass.q */
  450  
  451  	for (fp = netcb.n_ip_head; fp != NULL; fp = fp->iq_next) 
  452  
  453  		if (--(fp->iqx.ip_ttl) == 0) {  /* time to die */
  454  
  455  			q = fp->iqx.ip_next;    /* free mbufs assoc. w/chain */
  456  			while (q != (struct ip *)fp) {                         
  457  				p = q->ip_next;                                
  458  				m_freem(dtom(q));   
  459  				q = p;                                
  460  			}                                                      
  461  			ip_freef(fp);           /* free header */              
  462  		}
  463  }
